eṉṟum viḷakkamākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  olkāp perum pukaḻ paṭitta tolkāppiyam tōṉṟuvataṟkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉarē yāḻ arumpiviṭṭatu.  tolkāppiyattilē aiyamaṟa yāḻ eṉṉum icikkaruvi kuṟippiṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.  paṇṇatti muṟikaḷ eṉpaṉa yāḻiṉ vaḻi yeḻātu vēṟu etaṉ vaḻi eḻum?  tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattil
teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puṭpaṟi
ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokii
avvaki piṟavum karuveṉa moḻipa
eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  yāḻum, yāḻil eḻum iciyum tol kāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉ tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa eṉpatē aṟiñar karuttu.  mēlum tolkāppiyar kālattil yāḻi mīṭṭum pāṇar kaḷum, āṭum kūttarkaḷum, pāṭum porunarkaḷum, viṟaliyarkaḷum iruntaṉar eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu.  tolkāppiyattil,
tāvil nallici karutiya kiṭantōrkku
cūtar ēntiya tuyileṭi niliyum
kūttarum pāṇarum porunarum viṟaliyum					(tol. poruḷ. 1034)
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatiṉiṉṟu tolkāppiyar kālattil nāṭakam, nāṭṭiyam, kūttu, ici ākiyavikaḷum icik karuviyākiya muracu, yāḻ, kuḻal pōṉṟavikaḷi iyakkupavarkaḷum iruntaṉar eṉṟu aiyamaṟat terikiṉṟataṉṟō!
tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻakattil pāṇaṉ, paṟiyaṉ, tuṭiyaṉ mutaliya iṉattavarkaḷ tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉar.  pāṇar eṉpataṟkup paṇ pāṭuvōr eṉpatu poruḷākum.  pāṇaril icippāṇar, yāḻppāṇar, maṇṭippāṇar eṉṟa pala piriviṉar iruntaṉarām.  ivaruḷ - yāḻppāṇaril ciṟu pāṇar, perum pāṇar eṉṟu iru piriviṉar uṇṭu.  cīṟiyāḻi yum, pēriyāḻiyum uṭimiyāl ippeyar eḻuntatu.  poruna rāṟṟuppaṭi; pāṭiṉiyiṉ kiyiluḷḷatu pāli yāḻ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.  pāṇarkaḷ yāḻ vācippatil vallavarkaḷ eṉṟum itaṉāl ivarkaḷukku yāḻppāṇar eṉṟa peyar eḻuntatu eṉṟum kūṟuvatiliruntu tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē yāḻum ati mīṭṭum pāṇarkaḷum pāṭiṉikaḷum tōṉṟi viṭṭaṉar eṉṟu naṉkaṟiyalām.  tamiḻiṉ toṉmiyum ataṉālē viḷaṅkum.
tamiḻarkaḷ tolkāppiyam tōṉṟuvataṟku 4 āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē yāḻi uruvākki viṭṭaṉar.  tamiḻ makkaḷ kuṟiñci nilattil vēṭṭuva vāḻkki naṭattiyapoḻutē vil yāḻi uruvākki ati icipaṭa mīṭṭi vantamiyāl iṉṟikku aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ arappā nākarikattil vil yāḻ pōṉṟa yāḻ kāṇappaṭuvati aṟiñarkaḷ aṉivarum ārāyntu itu tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ kaṇṭa yāḻ eṉṟu pōṟṟi varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  arappā nākarikattiṟ kaṇṭa yāḻkaḷip pōṉṟa icikkaruvikaḷ pala cumēriyā nākarikattilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṅku akaḻntu ārāynta pēraṟiñarkaḷ ci. iliyōṉārṭ ulli, ci. cē. kēṭ, vi. kōrṭaṉ cilṭ jiyārj kōṉṭaṉā pōṉṟavarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
caṅka nūṟkaḷil yāḻiṉ uruvattip paṟṟi paṭam varintāṟ pōṉṟu ariya ōviyam pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ciṟupāṇāṟṟup paṭi, yāḻ, paciya kaṇkaḷ vāynta karuṅkuraṅku pāmpiṉ taliyip piṭittapoḻutu pāmpu orukāl iṟukavum orukāl nekiḻavum ataṉ kiyic cuṟṟuvatu pōṉṟu yāḻiṉ tivaviṉi narampu ceṟiyac cuṟṟiyuḷḷatu.  yāḻiṉ ariya viḷimpukaḷ porunta āṇikaḷ iṇikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  atu oḻuṅkāṉa toḻil vaki yaminta pattari (akaḷattiṉi) uṭiyatu.  tuvarūṭṭiṉa ciṟanta tōl pōrvi kumiḻam kompu pōṉṟa niṟattil uḷḷatu.  tēṉvaṇṭu miḻaṟṟuvatu pōṉṟa iṉṉoli eḻuppum narampu kaḷiyuṭiyatu1 eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
iḵtaṉṟi perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi pacumiyāṉa ili kaḷi utirtta tāḷiṉiyuṭiya pātiri malariṉ uḷḷiṭatti yotta tōl pōrviyi uṭiyatu.  ippōrvi naṉṟāka iṟukkik kaṭṭap peṟṟirukkum cuṉi vaṟṟi uḷ iruṇṭāṟ pōṉṟa vaṟuvāyiṉi uṭiyatu.  mutaṟ piṟi tōṉṟi ēnti irun tāṟ pōṉṟu ēntiya kavik kaṭiyiyum, nekiḻavum iṟukavum ēṟṟāṟpōṉṟa tivavu, nīṇṭa mūṅkil pōṉṟa tōlkaḷiyuṭiya maṅkiyiṉ muṉkiyi aṇi ceytu niṟkum aḻakiya toṭiyip pōṉṟu tikaḻum yāḻil ilaṅkum aḻakiya maruppu, nīlamaṇi oḻuki ṉāṟ pōṉṟa karumi niṟamuṭiyatu.2 eṉṟellām kūṟukiṟatu.
tamiḻ makkaḷāl peritum pōṟṟap peṟum civa viṇavat tōttirap pāṭalkaḷ palavaṟṟilum yāḻ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  teyvīka icik karuviyākiya yāḻiṉ ici civaperumāṉ, tirumāl mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷil cintikkiṉiyatāy cevikku uṇavāy iruntatu.  māṇikkavācakar tiruvācakattiluḷḷa tiruppaḷḷi eḻucciyil,
iṉṉici vīṇiyar yāḻiṉar orupāl
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  tiruñāṉacampantar tēvārattil,
kūṭarava monti kuḻali yāḻ muḻaviṉoṭu mici ceyya
eṉṟum,
paṟiyuñ ciṟukuḻalum yāḻum pūtam payiṟṟavē
maṟiyum palapāṭi mayāṉat tuṟi mintaṉār
eṉṟum,
iṉ kuralici keḻumi yāḻ murala
eṉṟum pāṭiyuḷḷār.
civaperumāṉ, eṉ appaṉ eṉṟu ēttip pukaḻum peṟṟi vāynta camaya kuravarkaḷiṉ mutiyavarāyt tikaḻnta appar aṭikaḷ,
aṟikalanta mutalmonti vīṇi yāḻum
eṉṟum
paṇṇōṭu yāḻvīṇi payiṉṟāy pōṟṟi
eṉṟum,
ēḻici yāḻvīṇi muralak kaṇṭēṉ
eṉṟum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  civacamaya kuravaril oruvarākiya cuntarar
maṇṇellām muḻava matirtara māṭamāḷiki kōpurattiṉ 										mēl
paṇṇiyāḻ muralum paḻaṉat tiruppaṉi yūr
eṉṟum pāṭiyuḷḷār.
viṇava camaya nūlākiya ātiyulāvil yāḻum pala icik karuvikaḷum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  kuṟippāka,
kuṭamuḻavaṅ kokkari vīṇi kuḻal yāḻ
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭiruppatu ārāyattakkatu.  viṇava aṭiyār kaḷil oruvarāṉa tiruppāṇāḻvār iciyilum, yāḻ mīṭṭuva tilum ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉār eṉṟum ici tirumālukku ukantatu eṉṟum naṉkaṟikiṟōm.  yāḻiṉ ici vētattiyal eṉpatu taṉiyaṉ vākku.
yāḻiṉ tōṟṟam
yāḻ iṉṟikku aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tōṉṟiyatu.  atu tōṉṟiya tol ūḻik kālattilē atu vaḷarcci peṟṟatāy ellā ēṟṟamum peṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu colvataṟkilli eṉpar.  atu ātiyil toṭakka niliyil oru narampip peṟṟup paṭip paṭiyāy nāḷaṭivil iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu narampukaḷip peṟṟu oru niṟivāṉa icik karuviyāy miḷirntatu.  piṉṉartāṉ yāḻukku ēḻu narampukaḷ ēṟṟatu eṉṟu muṭivukaṭṭiṉar.  ātiyil arumpiyavil yāḻ oru narampuṭaṉ tōṉṟi nāḷaṭivil mūṉṟāy piṉṉar aintu narampukaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟa ātiyāḻākap pariṇa mittatu.  ātiyil tamiḻ makkaḷ, tāram mutal narampu piṉṉar peṇ kuraliyotta kural, appāl uḻi, iḷi, tuttam eṉṉum mūṉṟum kūṭṭi aintu narampukaḷ ātiyil kaṇṭaṉar eṉpar.  aintu narampilē arumpum paṇ taḷam cila iruntaṉa.  kural mutalāka eṭuttu viḷari kikkiḷiyi nīkkip pāṭa, matumātavi eṉṉum irākam ma, pa eṉṟum tāram mutalāka eṭuttu viḷari kikkiḷiyi viḷakkippāṭa irāmakiruti irākam, ma,pa eṉṟum iḷi mutal eṭuttu viḷari kikkiḷi nīkkippāṭa tanyāci irākam eṉṟum nārata caṅkīta makarantam naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉavum kūṟuvar.
piṉṉāḷil oru curam, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu curaṅkaḷāki iṟutiyil ēḻākap pariṇamittatu eṉṟu ellā ici vallunarkaḷum ēṟṟukkoḷḷukiṉṟārkaḷ.  vaṭamoḻi ici marapilum muṉṉar aintu curaṅkaḷōṭu kūṭiya ici iruntuvantatu eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm.  iṉṟum cīṉa nāṭṭil aintu curaṅkaḷōṭu kūṭiya ici vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.  ēḻu iciyi kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉṟu aḻippatu potuvaḻakku.  vaṭa nūṟpulavarkaḷ caṭcam, iṭapam (ricapam), kāntāram, mattimam, pañcamam, tivatam, nicātam eṉpar.  (1) uḻi eṉṉum ici narampiṉ ōciyi yāṉiyiṉ oli eṉṟum (2) kikkiḷi narampiṉ ōciyi kutiriyiṉ oli eṉṟum (3) tuttam eṉṉum narampiṉ oliyi kiḷi kuyil ākiyavikaḷiṉ ōci eṉṟum (4) kural eṉṉum narampiṉ ōciyi vaṇṭu, kokku ākiya vikaḷiṉ oli eṉṟum, (5) tāram eṉṉum narampiṉ ōciyi āṭṭiṉ oli eṉṟum (6) viḷari eṉṉum narampiṉ ōciyi pacuviṉ oli eṉṟum (7) iḷi eṉṉum narampiṉ ōciyi mayiliṉ oli eṉṟum ici aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟiṉar.  itu oliyik kuṟikkum aṭi yāḷap peyarkaḷākak karutappaṭalām eṉṟu cilar moḻikiṉṟaṉar.
yāḻiṉ vaṭivam
yāḻiṉ vaṭivam, ati amikkum muṟi, curuti kūṭṭum muṟi, icikkum vaḻi mutaliyavi paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  āṉāl yāḻiṉ vaṭivatti ituvari nammāl kāṇa muṭiyavilli.  namatu nāṭṭup puti poruḷ ārāyccikkārarkaḷ evarum yāḻi akaḻntu kaṇṭatākak kūṟavilli.  muṟkāla yāḻkaḷil oṉṟiyāvatu namatu kōyil kaḷilō, piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilō pātukāttu vikkappaṭṭiruppatāka nām kēḷvippaṭṭatum illi;  kaṇṭatumilli.  yāḻkaḷiṉ uruvattik kāṭṭum ōviyamō, ciṟpamō nēṟṟu vari evarum kaṇṭatākak kūṟamuṭiyavilli.  yāḻ nūlik kaṇṭa pērāciriyar vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ tamiḻakattil yāḻ ōviya vaṭivattiyō, ciṟpa vaṭivattiyō kāṇavilli.  avar kaṇṭa amarāvati ciṟpaṅkaḷil tamiḻarkaḷ ātiyil kaṇṭa cīṟiyāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, vilyāḻ mutaliyavi etuvum illi.  yāḻ eṉṉum teyvīka ikkaruvi paṉṉūṟu āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu maṟintu pōyviṭṭatu.  tamiḻarkaḷ ataṉip piṟkāla makkaḷukkākap pātukāttu vikka villi.  ataṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷi ōviyattilum ciṟpattilum tīṭṭi vikkavilli eṉṟu ematu tantiyār uḷam nontu varuntiya tuṇṭu.  yāḻ maṟintatōṭaṉṟi ataṉ uruvamum tamiḻakatti ṉiṉṟu maṟintoḻintu pōṉatu, cemmiyum, taṇmiyum, tiruvum, piṟa ciṟappum iḻantu viṭṭatu pōlākum.  ippēriḻappi ici aṟiñarkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟit tamiḻ makkaḷ aṉivarum kaṉavilum naṉavilum niṉintu niṉintu neñcam puṇṇāṉārkaḷ eṉṟārkaḷ.  itu 40 āṇṭukaḷākiyum eṉ niṉiviṉiṉṟu akala villi.  yāḻiṉ ārāycciyi mutaṉmutalāka ārampitta uyartiru vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ amarāvati, cāñci mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa cila ciṟpaṅkaḷil yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ cila iruppatik kaṇṭu kaḻipēruvakiyuṟṟār.  āṉāl anta yāḻkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi aṭikaḷō piṟarō kūṟamuṭiyavilli.  aṉittulakak kalimēti uyartiru ānantak kumāracuvāmi avarkaḷ eḻutiya āṅkilak kaṭṭuri oṉṟil paḻamiyāṉa yāḻ eṉṉum icikkaruviyi amarāvatiyil uḷḷa kallōviyattil kaṇṭu kaḷittup paṇṭik kālat tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa iccīriya icikkaruvi tamiḻnāṭṭilē vaḻaṅkivantatu eṉpatiyum mikat toṉmiyāṉa ici marapu oṉṟu tamiḻakattil nilavi maṟintatu eṉpatiyum ulakil uḷḷa ici nipuṇarkaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār.  ikkaṭṭuri aṟiñar vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷiṉ uḷḷattiyum kavarntatu.
ilaṇṭaṉ tolporuḷ kāṭciccāliyilum ceṉṉi tolporuḷ kāṭciccāliyilum amarāvati ciṟpaṅkaḷ cila uḷḷaṉa.  acciṟpaṅkaḷil cila yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  anta ariya yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷik kaṇṭa aṟiñarkaḷ evarum avikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷik kuṟippiṭavilli.  āṉāl tiru vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ ilaṇṭaṉ tolporuḷ kāṭciccāli yil uḷḷa amarāvati ciṟpattil oru yāḻi cakōṭayāḻ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  mēlum anta yāḻip paṟṟit tamatu yāḻ nūlil oru viḷakkamum tantuḷḷār.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
ilaṇṭaṉ mānakarp paḻam poruṭkāṭciccāliyilē yuḷḷa amarāvatik kallōviyattilē kāṭṭappaṭṭiruppatu cakōṭa yāḻ.  itilē tivavu eṉṉum uṟuppu ilatu, narampukaḷi valittal, melittal ceyyum māṭakam eṉṉum iṟukkāṇi pattarilē amikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.  pattar yāḻ vācikkum peṇṇiṉ uṭaliṉāl maṟivuṇṭiruttaliṉ nām pattariṉiyum māṭakattiṉiyum kāṇamuṭiyavilli; kōṭum narampumē tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.  kōṭṭiṉ uruvatti yāḻ vācikkum peṇṇiṉ uruvattōṭu oppiṭṭup pārkkumiṭattup peṟappaṭu kiṟa kōṭṭiṉ nīḷattiṉiyuṅ koṇṭu ikkaruviyiṉ vaṭivam aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟi oruvāṟu kaṇittaṟiyalām.  iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭuk kallōviyattilē cakōṭayāḻ uruvattiṉi nām kāṇappeṟṟatu ici maṭanti namakku aḷitta periyatōr aruṭ koṭiyē.  ceṉṉi mānakarp paḻam poruḷkāṭciccāliyi luḷḷa yāḻēntiya ōviyaṅkaḷiṉ uruvatti VIM« paṭattil kāṇka.  pēriyāḻiṉip poṟuttavariyil ataṉ uruvattiṉi namatu nāṭṭilē kāṇap peṟṟilamēṉum kaṭalkōḷiṉ piṉ, tamiḻar kaṭal kaṭantuc ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiya piṟa nāṭukaḷilē ik karuviyiṉ uruvattiṉik kāṇak kūṭiyatāyirukkiṟatu.1  aṭikaḷār kuṟippiṭṭa cakōṭayāḻ eṉṉum amarāvati ciṟpam avaratu yāḻ nūlil 141ām pakkattil iṇikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.  aṭikaḷār kaṇṭa cakōṭayāḻ uruvam oṉṟu atē yāḻ nūlil 53ām pakkattil iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  ivviru uruvaṅkaḷum oṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭavilli.  aṭikaḷār kaṇṭa cakōṭayāḻ vaḷinta kompiyuṭiyatāy paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa ilakkaṇa vitikaḷit taḻuviyatāy iruk kiṟatu.  amarāvatic ciṟpattil pāṭiṉiyākiya aḻakiya āraṇaṅku kiyēnti niṟkum yāḻ nimirntu nīṇṭa kompukaḷiyuṭiyatāy irukkiṟatu.  mēlum avar tīṭṭiya amarāvatic ciṟpattil uḷḷa ēṉiya mūṉṟu yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷi nōkkiṉālum ivar kaṇṭa cakōṭayāḻukku oppuvami uṭiyaṉavāka illi.  avvāṟu iruntum ēṉ amarāvati yāḻic cakōṭayāḻ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭ ṭār eṉṟu eṉṉāl cintikka muṭiyavilli.  eṉavē namatu vācakarkaḷiṉ muṭipiṟku iti viṭṭuviṭa eṇṇi amarāvatiyil uḷḷa kōli eṉṉum iṭattil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa ciṟpa uruvaṅ kaḷi niḻal uruvap paṭam piṭittu innūlil veḷiyiṭṭirup patōṭu aṭikaḷār kaṇṭa cakōṭayāḻ uruvattiyum veḷiyiṭ ṭuḷḷōm.  (paṭam VII pārkka)
yāḻ uruvam, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ tavappēṟākac camīpattil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  tirucci māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa putuk kōṭṭikku aṇmiyil uḷḷa cuvaril aḻakiya yāḻ uruvam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itik kēṭṭut tamiḻulakam kaḻipēruvaki aṭiyum eṉṟu nampukiṟōm.  iṅkuḷḷa ciṟpattil muṉivarkaḷiṉ naṭuvil nāratar yāḻuṭaṉum tumpuruvar vīṇiyuṭa ṉum kāṇappaṭukiṟār eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.  peru māṉiṉ talippakkattil cuvaril kāṇappaṭum ciṟpa uruvil muṉivar kūṭṭattil nāratar mūṉṟāvatākavum tumpuruvar nāṉkāva tākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟār.
tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar kiyiliruntatu cakōṭayāḻ.  inta cakōṭayāḻtāṉ tiruñāṉa campantap piḷḷiyārāl pārāṭṭap peṟṟatu.  inta icik karuviyiṉ uruvam namakku veku nāḷākat teriyātu.  ippoḻutu tiru erukkattampuliyūril uḷḷa tirukkōyilil tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇariṉ tiruuruvattil yāḻ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  atu cakōṭayāḻ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
āṉāl tiruñāṉa campantariṉ varalāṟṟil tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇarum avartam tuṇiviyār mataṅka cūḷāmaṇiyārum kaḻumalattīcaṉ kavuṇiyap piḷḷiyārkku mūvāṇṭil aruḷ ñāṉam kuḻittūṭṭiya aṟputa nikaḻcciyik kēṭṭu campantarik kaṇṭu pōṟṟa cīrkāḻikkup pōntaṉar.  ati aṟinta campantar avarkaḷi varavēṟṟu upacarittār.  tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar campantarit toḻutu tantiriyāḻiṉi vīkki ici yārāyntu iṟivaṟkukanta pāṇiyiṉi ēḻici valla mataṅka cūḷāmaṇiyā ruṭaṉ pāṭi yāvarum viyakka yāḻiliṭṭu vācittup pōṟṟiṉār eṉṟu kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu.1
itaṉāl tantiriyāḻum cakōṭayāḻum oṉṟā?  vēṟā? eṉpatu nammāl tiṭṭavaṭṭamākak kūṟamuṭiyavilli.  patiṉāṉku narampukaḷiyuṭiyatu cakōṭa yāḻ.  ataṉ paḻiya peyar cey muṟik kēḷvi eṉpatākum.  itu cakōṣam eṉa vaṭamoḻiyāḷa rāl aḻikkappeṟṟuc cakōṭayāḻ eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkiyatu.  yāḻkkaruviyiṉ vaḷinta kōṭu nimirkkap peṟṟu cemmiyāka māṟṟap peṟṟu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻāka maṟumalarcci peṟṟu ēḻu narampukaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟaṉa.
kumpakōṇattiṟku aṇmiyil uḷḷa tārācuram eṉṉum kōyililum tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar uruvac ciṟpattil yāḻ uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
yāḻum - vīṇiyum
yāḻ tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa toṉmiyāṉa icikkaruvi - yāḻ tamiḻakatti viṭṭu iṉṟu maṟintuviṭṭatu.  muṟkālattil tamiḻakattil eṇṇaṟṟa yāḻ vakikaḷ iruntaṉa.  iṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka oru yāḻ uruvaṅ kūṭak kiṭikkavilli.  yāḻip paṟṟi aṟiya pala mēṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ tuṭituṭikkiṉṟārkaḷ.  tamiḻ nāṭṭil attakiya tuṭituṭippō akkaṟiyō iṉṉum eḻa villi.  irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuvil tiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ yāḻip paṟṟi ārāya muṟpaṭṭār.  mikunta ciramappaṭṭu yāḻ nūli eḻutit tamiḻ ulakiṟku aḷittār.  annūlil cila yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷiyum namakku  varintu tantār.  marattāl cila yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷiyum ceytu tantār.  avar kālattiṟkup piṉ anta āyvu toṭarntu naṭipeṟavilli.  avar namakkuc ceytu tanta yāḻiṉ uruvaṅkaḷik kūṭap pātukāttu vikkat tavaṟi viṭṭōm.
tamiḻaṉukku ātiyil aṟamuritta aṟak kaṭavuḷ iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉum muttamiḻ vaḻaṅkiya muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ - mukkaṇṇaṉ tamatu tirukkarattāl yāḻi mīṭṭiṉār.  itaṉ iṉiya oliyic cuvittu iṉpuṟṟār.  tamiḻ makkaḷukku yāḻi mīṭṭuvatiyum avar kaṟpittuk koṭuttār eṉṟu namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ kūṟiṉar.  patiṉintu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toṉmi yāṉa aṟaneṟikaḷukkup puttuyirum putuvāḻvum ūṭṭiya camaya aṟivarkaḷ nutalviḻi nāṭṭat tiṟivaṉi yāḻmoḻi ammiyōṭallavā kaṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpati nām ciṟitum cintikkavilli.  pala ūrkaḷil uḷḷa tēviyārkku, yāḻiṉumeṉ moḻiyammi, yāḻiṉum meṉmoḻi nāyaki, yāḻip patitta moḻiyammi eṉṉum peyarkaḷ cūṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  atōṭu mukkaṇṇuṭiya īcaṉār karaṅkaḷil yāḻēnti iṉṉici mīṭṭi ulaki iṉpuṟac ceytār.  ati appar aṭikaḷ
paṇṇoṭu yāḻvīṇi payiṉṟāy pōṟṟi
eṉṟum,
ēḻici yāḻvīṇi muralak kaṇṭēṉ
eṉṟum,
aṟi kalanta kuḻal monti vīṇiyāḻum
eṉṟum pōṟṟiya puṉitamāṉa teyvat tiruyāḻit tamiḻarkaḷ iḻantuviṭṭaṉar; maṟantuviṭṭaṉar.  iṉṟum avarkaḷ antat tūya teyvīka icikkaruviyākiya yāḻiṉ uruvattik kāṇa uḷḷam tuṭittu eḻavilli.  patiṉmūṉṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar, maṟintutoḻintupōṉa yāḻi āyntu paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭiya tōṭamiyātu ati ilakkaṇa vitippaṭi marattāṟ ceytu tamiḻaṉṉiyiṉ poṟpāta kamalaṅkaḷil aṟiñar vipulā ṉantar kāṇikkiyākap paṭittār.  tamiḻaṉ anta ariya icik karuviyikkūṭa iḻantuviṭṭāṉ.  tamiḻakattiṟku itu oru perum iḻukkākum.  namatu nāṭṭup paṟṟukku, namatu teyva paktikku, namatu paṇpiṟku, namatu icip perumikku, namatu pukaḻukku itu pēriḻappākum.
inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ naṭuvil, tamiḻaṉiṉ teyvīkac cottāṉa, amutamāṉa yāḻkkaruviyi mīṇṭum tamiḻarkaḷ peṟacceyta iru perum īḻat tamiḻarkaḷāṉa uyartiru ānantak kumāracuvāmi avarkaḷukkum vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷukkum eṉṟum nām naṉṟip pārāṭṭit taṉatu vaṇakkattic celuttak kaṭamip paṭṭuḷḷōm.
patiṉintu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē yāḻ tamiḻakattil celvākkup peṟṟirukkum poḻutē tamiḻarkaḷ yāḻiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyil putiya icik karuviyik kaṇṭaṉar.  atu yāḻ pōl pērum perumiyum peṟṟatu.  tirukkōyilkaḷil yāḻip pōṉṟu mīṭṭap peṟṟu makkaḷiṉ naṉmatippip peṟṟu vantatu.  ataṉiyē vīṇi eṉṟu aḻittaṉar.
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil pāṇar kiyilum pāṭiṉi kiyilum iruntu pēriya paṇkaḷi olitta pāriya pēriyāḻ cīṟiya cīṟiyāḻ, uḷḷam kavarum vilyāḻ pōṉṟa icik karuvikaḷ pala iḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷ kālattil icip pulavar kikaḷilum nāṭaka makaḷir ki kaḷilum iyintu, cittirap paṭattil cērntu, ceḻuṅkōṭṭil malar puṉintu, maṇamakaḷir eḻilnili eṉṉak kaviṉ peṟak kāṭci nalkiṉa. āḷuṭiya piḷḷiyār avaṉiyil avataritta aruṭ kālattilē āṇṭavaṉ aḷitta aṟputap poṟpalakiyiṉ mītēṟi, aṟavōr akaṅkuḷira aruṭkavi pāṭuvataṟkut tuṇi purintaṉa.  aruḷ vaṭivāy avaṉiyeṅkum pōṟṟa eḻunta araṉār karattil iṭam peṟṟatu.  yāḻmurinātar ēntiya teyvat tīntamiḻ yāḻeṉa ciṟappuṟṟatu.  koṅku vēḷirum, tiruttakka tēvarum ēṭeḻutiya kālattil eḻil mikka iḷavaracikaḷ piṅkaraṅkaḷilē iṭam peṟṟu, avarkaḷiṉ aṉpiṟkuriya aḻakiya kātalari avarukku aḷikkum arum perum kātaṟ karuviyāyt tikaḻntu, varalāṟṟup perumiyum ilakkiyac ciṟappum peṟṟut tikaḻntu viḷaṅkiyatu.
varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟkum muṟpaṭṭa mutuperum tamiḻ nilattilēyuḷḷa mulliyil āyarkaḷ kaṇṭa vilyāḻ eṉṉum iṉmoḻi payanta iṉiya kuḻavi tōṉṟi, maḻali miḻaṟṟik kuṟiñci nilak kuṟavarkaḷiyum kuṟa makaḷirkaḷiyum akamakiḻac ceytatu. appāl atu vaḷarntu eḻilum iḷamiyum peṟṟuttāṉ pōṟṟiya mulli nilattil naṉmoḻi naviṉṟu mulli nila āyarkaḷiyum āycciyarkaḷiyum iṉpuṟac ceytatu.  piṉ, atu cīṟiyāḻ eṉṉum pētip paruvatti eyti cīriḷamic celvac ciṟumiyāy pāṇaṉ, pāṭiṉiyōṭu kūṭi nāṭeṅkum ceṉṟu tali makaṉum talimakaḷum maṭṭumiṉṟi ēḻikaḷum, pāḷikaḷum, pāṭṭāḷikaḷum kūṭṭāḷikaḷum iṉpuṟa iṉmoḻi pēci vantatu.  piṉṉar pēriyāḻ eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ petumpip paruvam eyti māṭamāḷiki kūṭakōpuram ellām pukuntu kuṟunila maṉṉar kaḷum muṭiyuṭiya vēntarkaḷum vaḷḷalkaḷum pulavarkaḷum kēṭṭu viyakkumpaṭi nayavuri pakarntatu, appāl maṅkip paruvamuṟṟu āṭi aṇikaḷ pūṇṭu aḻaku oḷira nāṭaka araṅkukaḷil tōṟṟit taṉ āṟṟal aṉittiyum kāṭṭip perumiyuṟṟatu.  piṟaku maṭantip paruvam eyti kaliñarkaḷum kaviñarkaḷum, vallōr kaḷum, nallōrkaḷum pōṟṟip pukaḻum cīrum ciṟappum eytiyatu.  tirunīlakaṇṭa teyvap perumpāṇarōṭu avaratu akamuṭi yāḷ mataṅka cūḷāmaṇiyārōṭu teyvat tirukkōyilkaḷil valam vantu teyva iciyi nalki aṭiyār uḷḷatti urukki āḷuṭiya piḷḷiyāriṉ akaṅkuḷirac ceytatu.  avaratu pārāṭṭip peṟṟatu.  atu arivip paruvam uṟṟatum araciḷaṅ kumarikaḷukku uyirp pāṅkiyāy avarkaḷukku ēṟṟa talivarkaḷi avarkaḷiṭam cērkkum mantira caktiyāy miḷirntatu.  uyar neṟiyāḷar tam uyiriṉum iṉiya talivaṉāy viḷaṅkum civaperumāṉ yāḻmuri nātar eṉṉum peyarip peṟac ceytatum avaratu āṟṟalāy aruḷ caktiyāy viḷaṅkum tēviyi avar, yāḻip paḻitta moḻiyammi eṉṟu tittikkat tēṉ oḻuka aḻikkum perum pēṟṟip peṟṟatu,  teyvat tiruvaruḷi yuṟṟatu, civaṉār tiruvā yāl aṭikkaṭi aḻikkappeṟṟa aruṭ pēṟṟi aṭintatu, ivvāṟu pērum perumiyum cīrum ciṟappum peṟṟa yāḻ eṉṉum naṉmoḻi vāynta iṉmoḻi payakkum tīñcuvi celviyām yāḻ iṉṟu irunta iṭam teriyātu maṟintu pōṉāḷ eṉṟu uḷḷam urukik kaṇṇīr malki namakku ellām aṟivuc cuṭar kāṭṭi avaḷ irunta tiru uruvam kāṭṭi mīṇṭum avaḷ tamiḻakat til koluvīṟṟirukkac ceyyumpaṭi tūṇṭiṉār, nam aruṭṭiru vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷār.
mātaṅki uṟiyum maṇiyoli yeḻuppum teyvat tiru vuṭiya yāḻ mīṇṭum tamiḻ makkaḷ illaṅkaḷ aṉittilum olikkac ceyya vēṇṭumeṉa vipulāṉantar kaṉavu kaṇṭār; mīṇṭum paḻantamiḻar kaṇṭa yāḻ eṉṉum teyva naṅki tamiḻakat til aracuk kaṭṭili aṭiyac ceyya iṭiviṭātu muyaṉṟār.  tamiḻaṉākap piṟanta ovvoruvar illattilum avarkaḷ tāyt tirunāṭṭil iruntālum cari allatu cēy nāṭākiya īḻa nāṭṭil iruntālum cari allatu ēla nāṭṭil vāḻntālum cari allatu mali nāṭṭil vaṇikam purintālum cari, ciṅkapurattil ceṅ kōlōcciṉālum cari, intōṉēciyāvil irantālum cari, parmā nāṭṭil pāṭupaṭṭālum cari, allatu pālittīvil pārli payiriṭṭu uḻaṉṟālum cari, allatu campā, pōrṉiyā, keṉiyā, morēciya nāṭukaḷil kūliyākavō, aracāṅka aluvalar kaḷākavō amiccarākavō, akatiyākavō alintālum cari avarkaḷatu cevikaḷil ovvoru nāḷum yāḻ oli kēṭkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu viḻintār vipulāṉantar.  avaratu kaṉavi avaratu āciyit tamiḻaṉṉiyiṉ putalvarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟik koḷvōr aṉivarum naṉavākka naṭimuṟiyil ceytu kāṭṭa muyala vēṇṭum.  atuvē nam kaṭami eṉpatiyum uṇaral vēṇṭum.
olic ciṟappu
makkaḷukkuc cevikkiṉpattiyum uḷḷattiṟku uvakiyi yum ūṭṭattakkatāy amiyappeṟṟa, ēṟṟattāḻvaṟṟa ici, oli aḷavōṭu kūṭa eḻumpōtu iciyoli eṉap peṟṟatu.  appāl icikkaruvikaḷil iciyōrttup pāṭum paṇṇiṟkuriya pāṇi yiṉ naṭiyum tāṉavuṟutiyum cantak kuḻippilakkaṇam vaḻuvātu miḻaṟṟuvatāl eḻum oliyiyum iciyoli eṉṟu ilakkaṇa varampu niṟuvappeṟṟatu.  ivviciyoli mūlam mutal makkaḷiṉ miṭaṟṟilē (kaṇṭattilē) tāṉa niliyil arumpiyatu.  appāl avarkaḷiṉ aṟiviṉṟiṟattāl iyaṟkiyiṉ vaḻittāy cūḻal, yāḻ, mattaḷam, tuṭi, nātacuram pōṉṟa pala icik karuvikaḷip paṟṟi āyntu ataṟku ilakkaṇamum kaṇṭu vitippaṭi uruvākki avikaḷiṉiṉṟu iṉṉolikaḷi eḻuppi iṉpuṟṟu vantaṉar.
pā, paṇ, pāṇi eṉṉum mūṉṟu ciṟanta uṟuppukkaḷi yuṭiya icippāṭṭiṉi vaḻuviṉṟi icikkum ēṟṟamuṭiya karuvikaḷ icikkaruvikaḷ eṉṟaḻikkappeṟṟaṉa.  ivikaḷi iruvakiyākap pirittup payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.  avi muṟiyē miṭaṟṟuk karuvi kuyiluvak karuvi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
1.  miṭaṟṟuk karuvi
miṭaṟṟuk karuvi ici olikaḷiyum pākkaḷiyum oruṅkē nikaḻttum pāṉmi vāyntatu.  makkaḷatu kaṇṭam - atāvatu miṭaṟēyākum.  itaṉik kural eṉṟum cārīram eṉṟum kūṟuvar.  miṭaṟu eṉpatu ākupeyarāṉ, miṭaṟṟup pāṭalukkāyiṟṟu eṉpar.
2.  kuyiluvak karuvi
icip pākkaḷiṉ iciyiyum pāṇiyiyum nikaḻtta vallatum maṉitaṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatumāṉa karuvikaḷ aṉittum kuyiluvak karuvikaḷ ākum.  kuyilutal eṉṟāl olittal eṉṟu poruḷ tarum.
kuyiluvak karuvi, tōṟkaruvi, tuḷikkaruvi, narampuk karuvi, kañcak karuvi eṉa nāṉku vakippaṭum.  namatu nikaṇṭukaḷilum akarātikaḷilum inta nāṉku vakik karuvikaḷiyum icik karuvikaḷāka kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
yāḻiṉ ēṟṟam
pāṭṭiṟku urukum paramacivaṉ, pāṇapattiraṉ poruṭṭu, pāṇṭiya nāṭṭut talimip patiyākiya maturiyilē tōṉṟi ṉār.  viṇṇiṉiṉṟu maṇṇiṟku iṟaṅkiṉār. avaratu teyvat tiru uruvi māṟṟi makkaḷ uruvan tāṅki, atilum viṟaku viṟpavaṉ pōla vēṭantāṅkiṉār.  tamiḻ nāṭṭu icivāṇaṉ muṉ, vaṭa nāṭṭu icivāṇaṉ niṟkavum añci iravōṭiravāyt tamiḻ nāṭṭit tuṟantu - iti ēṟiṭṭuppārkkavum añci vaṭa nāṭṭil pōyp patuṅkik koḷḷac ceytār.
tamiḻiciyum tamiḻ nāṭṭu yāḻiciyum kēṭṭa mayilum kuyilum, māṉum āṉum pariyum kariyum, puliyum eliyum, taṉvaya miḻantu oṉṟukūṭi niṉṟaṉa eṉṟu namatu purāṇaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tirunīla kaṇṭa yāḻppāṇarukkup poṟpalaki īntu yāḻiṉ perumiyi viṇṇiṟku uyarttiṉār civaperumāṉ.  uḷḷam makiḻntu tiruñāṉa campantap piḷḷiyārukkup poṟṟāḷa mīntu tamiḻ iciyiṉ ēṟṟatti ulakil nilināṭṭiṉa teyvam civaṉēyām.
aruṭperuntakiyām tirukkurukip pirāṉ kaṉṉalum kaṉiracamum pōṉṟa kārmukil vaṇṇaṉi,
muṉ nalyāḻ payiṟnūl narampiṉ mutircuviyē
eṉṟu pukaḻntu, avaṉ yāḻiṉ narampukaḷāy mutircuviyāy tittikkum tīñcuviyāy iṉimiyāy niṉṟāṉ eṉṟa uṇmiyi eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
tiṉitiṉṉavanta yāṉi yāḻiṉ iṉṉōciyik kēṭṭu taṉṉi maṟantu aṭutta aṭiyum vikkātu uḷḷa muruki niṉṟatu.  itaṉip pālikkaliyil,
yāḻvari nillāk karuṅkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal
yāḻvarit taṅki yāṅkut tāḻpuniṉ
tolkaviṉ tolita lañci yeṉ
colvarit tāṅkiṉar kātalōrē
eṉṟu talivikkup poruḷ tēṭa eṇṇip piriya virukkum talimakaṉiṉ nilippaṟṟi tōḻi orutti kūṟukiṉṟāḷ.
icik karuvikaḷ pala oruṅkē iyintu olippatu paṇṭit tamiḻakattil āmantiriki eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭatu.  itaṉi āṅkilēyar ārcceṭṭirā (Orchestra) eṉṟu aḻippar.  cīvaka cintāmaṇi eṉṉum cīriya kāppiyattiṟku uri kaṇṭa āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, kuḻal vaḻi yāḻeḻīit taṇṇumi piṉṉar muḻaviyampa lāmantiriki eṉṟu mēṟkōḷum kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
kuḻalvaḻi niṉṟa tiyāḻē yāḻvaḻit
taṇṇumi niṉṟatu takavē taṇṇumip
piṉvaḻi niṉṟatu muḻavē muḻavoṭu
kūṭiniṉ ṟicitta tāman tiriki
eṉṟa cilappatikāra araṅkēṟṟuk kātip pakutiyālum muṟkālat tamiḻakattil icik karuvikaḷ pala oruṅku cērntu olitta kāṭciyi aṟikiṉṟōm.
malimakaḷ makaṉām mayilēṟum māmakaṉiṉ maliyil (tirupparaṅkuṉṟattil) orupuṟam iyaṟki eḻilum maṟṟorupuṟam ceyaṟkic cīrum mayaṅkuvatu pōl iyintu, kaviñar uḷḷattik kaḷippikkiṉṟatu.  paṇṇārnta pāṇariṉ pintamiḻp paḻam perum yāḻiṉiṉṟu piṟakkum paḻañcuvi pōṉṟa tīñcuviyiṉ iṉṉoli oru puṟam eḻum.  avviṉiya ēḻici kēṭṭu eḻum vaṇṭukaḷiṉ rīṅkāram maṟṟoru puṟam olikkum.  kuḻaliṉ ici yōṭu tumpiyiṉ iciyum muḻaviṉ oliyum aruvi nīriṉ oliyum viṟaliyar (kūttiyar) nuṭakkattoṭu pūṅkoṭikaḷiṉ nuṭakkamum pāṭuvatil valla pāṭiṉikaḷ pālip paṇṇip pāṭiṉārkaḷ.  atil kiḻami, niṟi, kuṟikaḷiṉ tōṟṟattōṭu āṭum mayiliṉ arinta kural oliyum māṟuṟṟaṉa pōṉṟu etirniṉṟaṉa.  itaṉip paripāṭalil nallaḻuciyār eṉṉum pulavar perumakaṉ,
orutiṟam pāṇar yāḻiṉ tīṅkuraleḻa
orutiṟam yāṇar vaṇṭiṉ imiriciyeḻa
orutiṟam kaṇṇār kuḻaliṉ karipueḻa
orutiṟam paṇṇār tumpi paranticiyūka
orutiṟam maṇṇār muḻavi ṉiciyeḻa
orutiṟam aṇṇal neṭuvari yaruvi nīrttumpa
orutiṟam āṭaṉal viṟaliyar olkupu nuṭaṅka
orutiṟam vāṭi yuḷarvayiṟ pūṅkoṭi nuṭaṅka
orutiṟam pāṭiṉi muralum pāliyaṅ kuraliṉ
nīṭukiḷar kiḻami niṟikuṟi tōṉṟa
orutiṟam āṭucīr maññi yarikural tōṉṟa
māṟumā ṟuṟṟaṉapōṉ māṟetir kōṭaṉ
māṟaṭṭāṉ kuṉṟa muṭittu
eṉṟu aḻakuṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.  pāṭiṉiyiṉ miṭaṟṟup pāṭaliyum pāṇaṉ icikkum yāḻ oliyiyum, kuḻalōṉ vācikkum vaṅkiyap pāṭaliyum viṟaliyar āṭum āṭṭattiyum puṉattilum poḻililum kuṉṟattilum maṉṟattilum kaṭaṟkari yilum kāṉyāṟṟu maṇalilum tēvakōṭṭattilum aṟavōr paḷḷiyilum kēṭṭum kaṇṭum uḷam makiḻntu uḷḷam pūrittu iṉpak kaṭalāṭi vantaṉar paḻantamiḻ makkaḷ.  yāḻ oli avarkaḷ uyiri vaḷarttu vantatu.  ataṉ viḷakkamē ikkālattiya tamiḻar kaḷiṉ iciyuṇarvām.
yāḻiṉ piṟappiṭam
civalōkam eṉap pōṟṟappeṟum teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭi luḷḷa potiya maliyil iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻum piṟanta teṉpar, tamiḻ aṟiñar.  aṅkuttāṉ paḻam perum tamiḻ ilakkaṇam eṉṟu kūṟappeṟum olkāpperum pukaḻ tol kāppiyam tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar.  ippotiya maliyil allatu atit toṭarntu teṟkē nīṇṭirunta maliyucciyil tāṉ tamiḻaṉiṉ taṉip perum icikkaruviyāṉa yāḻum kuḻalum piṟantaṉa.  tēṉiṉum iṉiya teyvīka icikkaruviyāṉa yāḻik kuṟiñci nilak kuṟavarkaḷ kaṇṭaṉar.  akkaruviyē kuṟiñci yāḻ.  appāl maliyaṭivārattil vāḻnta tolkuṭi makkaḷāṉa āyarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ kaṇṭa vaṅkiyattōṭu yāḻiyum vaḷarttaṉar.  atuvē mulli yāḻ.  koṟṟavi kuṟiñci nila makkaḷukku vil yāḻi aḷittāḷ.  tīṅkuḻalit tirumāl mulli nila makkaḷukku uvantaḷittār.
viṇṇit toṭṭu niṟkum māmaliyiṉ aṭivārattilē kāṭum ceṭiyum kāṉyāṟum kāṭci tarum mulli nilattilē paccik kampaḷam virittāṟ pōṉṟu eḻiluṭaṉ tikaḻum pacumpuṟ ṟarilē āṭum māṭum mēyntu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.  kaṉṟukaḷ tuḷḷik kutittu viḷiyāṭukiṉṟaṉa.  kārkālam ākiyāl eṅkum kaviṉ peṟakkāṭci aḷikkiṉṟatu.  maraṅkaḷilum, ceṭikaḷi lum, koṭikaḷilum eḻil tarum eṇṇilā vaṇṇamalarkaḷ kottuk kottākap pūttuk kuluṅki niṟkiṉṟaṉa; kaṇṭōr kaṇ kaḷiyum karuttiyum kavarvaṉavāy kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  eṅkum kamakama veṉṉum naṟumaṇa nāṟṟam vīcikkoṇṭē irukkiṉṟatu.
māṭṭu mantiyiṉ mattiyil āyaṉ aṇipeṟa niṟkiṉṟāṉ.  avaṉ irupatu vayatum nirampappeṟāta kaṭṭiḷam vāynta kāḷi.  kaṭṭu vāynta kaviṉuṟu uṭaliyum tiraṇṭa tōḷkaḷiyum virinta mārpiyum uṭiya oru āṇaḻakaṉ.  kārmēkam pōṉṟa kaṟutta niṟattiyuṭiyavaṉ.  ciṟanta nōkkiṉaṉ.  ariyil kaṉatta āṭiyi iṟukap piṇittuḷḷāṉ.  avaṉatu paranta tiraṇṭa tōḷil mulli malarum elliyaṟṟa maṇantarum ēṉiya eḻil tarum pūkkaḷum kōkkap peṟṟa māliyoṉṟu aṇi ceykiṟatu.  ariyiṟ piṇittuḷḷa kacciyil oru vēyṅkuḻal cerukap paṭṭuḷḷatu.  oru kiyilē kōlum maṟṟoru kiyilē vilyāḻum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
katiravaṉ taṉ poṟkatirkaḷi vīcikkoṇṭu takatakaveṉak kiḻakkilē tōṉṟi mika virivāka uccikku nērāka vantuḷḷāṉ.  eḻil mika vāynta iḷam naṅki orutti kuṭuviyilēyuḷḷa kūḻi eṭuttut tali mītu vittukkoṇṭu oyyāramāka naṭantu varukiṉṟāḷ.  atip pārttatum āyaṉ mukattil iḷam naki polikiṉṟatu.  kārikiyik kaṇṭatum kāḷikkuk kaṭṭuk kaṭaṅkāk kaḷippu.  avaḷ koṇṭuvarum kūḻik kaṇṭatum kāḷik kaḷippeṉṉum kaṭalil mūḻkiṉāṉ.  eṉṟālum avaṉ avaḷik kaṇṭatum taṉ āruyirt tuṇiviyeṉa ōṭippōy, kaṭṭi aṇittu mārpuṟat taḻuvi muttamiṭavilli.  avaṉ avaḷ arukē vantatum mutaṉmutalāka veḷḷāṭu iru kuṭṭikaḷ īṉṟatiyum, kārām pacu oru kaṉṟu pōṭṭatiyum kūṟiviṭṭuk kūḻi vāṅkik koṇṭu avaḷatu karaṅkaḷip paṟṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu ālamarattaṭiyil amarntu akamakiḻntu akamuṭi yāḷōṭu aḷavaḷāvip pēcikkoṇṭē vayiṟu nirampak kūḻik kuṭittāṉ.  appāl makiḻcci poṅka maraniḻalil maṇal mītu amarntu iṭuppil ceruki irukkum vēyṅkuḻali eṭuttu vāyil vikkiṉṟāṉ.  arukil amarntirukkum avaṉatu akamuṭiyāḷ tirumālākiya mulli nilat teyvattik kaṇṇārak kāṇpatu pōlavē kaṇavaṉik kaṇṭu kaḷikkiṉṟāḷ.  kaṇavaṉ tīṅkuḻalil pālip paṇṇip pāṅkuṭaṉ icikkiṉṟāṉ.  iḷam pāvi nallāḷ iṉiya iciyil mūḻki iṉpakkaṭalil amiḻntu kiṭakkiṉṟāḷ.  ciṟitu nēram ceṉṟatum aḻaku mikka anta āycci, akatti nōkki avacaramāka naṭantu ceṉṟāḷ.
avaṉ ceṉṟapiṉ āyaṉ taṉ vilyāḻi eṭuttu mīṭṭukiṉ ṟāṉ.  inta vilyāḻ āyaṉē amittatu.  tuḷiyuṭiya kumiḻaṅ kompi villāka vaḷittu nāriṉālē kaṭṭiyuḷḷāṉ.  orē aḷavuḷḷa ēḻu vilkaḷ oṉṟāka iṇittuk kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  nāṇkaḷ ciṟitum peritumāy uyarttiyum tāḻttiyum kaṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  āyaṉ narampukaḷit toṭṭu yāḻi mīṭṭi ici yoppumi ceykiṉṟāṉ.  cōliyelām iṉṉoli, vāṉa melām iṉṉoli, āṭu, māṭu, eliyum, puliyum, mayilum, kuyilum, pāmpum, palliyum anta iṉṉoli kēṭṭu iṉpuṟṟu meymaṟantu niṉṟaṉa.  āyaṉ anta yāḻil peṇ iṉpattiviṭa periya pēriṉpattit tuykkiṉṟāṉ.  iṉpa mikutiyāl avaṉum ciṟitu nēram meymaṟantu niṉṟāṉ.  appāl māli nēram ākiviṭṭati aṟintu yāḻi vittuviṭṭuk kuḻali eṭuttuc cātārip paṇṇi icikkiṉṟāṉ.  piṉ āṭukaḷiyum māṭukaḷiyum kaṉṟukaḷiyum tiraṭṭikkoṇṭu vīṭu nōkki varukiṉṟāṉ.  appāl avi kaḷit toḻuvattil kaṭṭiyum paṭṭiyil maṭakkiyum viṭṭuviṭṭu illattiṟkuḷ pukukiṉṟāṉ.  avaṉatu akamuṭiyāḷ paṭikkum amuti aruntiviṭṭu veku oyyāramākat taṉ illak kiḻatti yōṭu arukarukē amarntu koṇṭu ici kūṭṭi vitta vil yāḻi eṭuttuk kuṟiñcip paṇṇi icikkiṉṟāṉ.  maṉivi kēṭṭu makiḻkiṉṟāḷ.  anta iravu iṉpa iravāka, tēṉ iravākat tikaḻntatu.  naḷḷiravil kuṟiñci, kuṟiñciyāyp puṇarntatu.
inta vilyāḻtāṉ tamiḻaṉ - ēṉ? mulli nila āyaṉ mutaṉ mutalākak kaṇṭa icikkaruvi.  iṉṟikku aiyāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukkumuṉ mutalāvatāka oru narampuṭiya vilyāḻ eḻuntatu.  itu caṅkak kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭatu.  itu paḻaṅ kaṟkālam.  irum pāṇiyō, cempāṇiyō, uḷiyō, cuttiyō illāta kālam.  eṉavē kiyāl kampukaḷi vaḷittu maranārkaḷāl villik kaṭṭi maralnāriṉit tirittu narampākak kaṭṭi yāḻi uruvākki ṉar.  appāl nāḷaṭivil atu vaḷarcci aṭintu mūṉṟu narampu kaḷum, aintu narampukaḷum piṉ ēḻu narampukaḷumāka eḻuntaṉa.  itaṟku ilakkaṇamum vakuttaṉar.  yāḻukku ettaṉi vilkaḷ?  ettaṉi narampukaḷ?  eṉṉa kompāl yāḻ ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭum, yāḻiṉ nīḷam, akalam, kuṟukkāka vittuk kaṭṭavēṇṭiya kompukaḷiṉ nīḷam akalam ataṉ eṇṇam muṟukkāṇikaḷiṉ eṇṇam, oṟṟuṟuppukaḷ amikkum muṟi ivikaḷukkellām ilakkaṇam vakuttaṉar.  avi mikavum virintaṉa.
piṉṉar vilyāḻiṉ amippu attuṇi ciṟantatāka illi eṉṟu kaṇṭaṉar.  mēlum yāḻ oliyi arukil iruppavarkaḷ tāṉ kēṭka muṭiyum, nīṇṭa tūram ivvoli kēṭka eṉṉa ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpatiyellām cintikka muṟpaṭṭaṉar.  eṉavē pala vakiyāṉa yāḻic ceyyavum, ēḻu, oṉpatu, paṉṉiraṇṭu, patiṉāṉku, patiṉāṟu, irupatu, nūṟu, āyiram eṉṟu narampukaḷik kaṭṭi pala vakiyāṉa vaṭivil pala yāḻ vakikaḷi uruvākkiṉar.
inta yāḻi amippataṟku maṭṭum appaḻaṅkāla makkaḷ ilakkaṇam kāṇavilli.  ati ici kūṭṭum muṟikkum ilakkaṇam kaṇṭaṉar.  muṟukkāṇikaḷi muṭukkavum, nekiḻttavum vaḻi kaṇṭaṉar.  muṟukkāṇikaḷip piṉṉē taḷḷa, muṟukkēṟṟi narampiṉi valittal, melittal ceytu piṉ atit taḷḷi iṟukkik koḷḷavum terintukoṇṭaṉar.  appāl narampukaḷukku uḻi mutalāka kikkiḷi īṟāka ēḻu peyarkaḷi vittup pala nāṭkaḷ āyntu iṟutiyāka ici kūṭṭa muṭipu kaṇṭaṉar.  tārattiṉ aintāmici uḻi eṉṟu koṇṭaṉar.
pallāṇṭākat tamiḻ makkaḷ yāḻip paṟṟi āyntu palvēṟu vitamāṉa yāḻkaḷik kaṇṭaṉar.  avikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ namakku atikamākak kiṭikkavilli.  namakkut terintavikaḷip paṟṟi viḷakkik kūṟuvōm.  avi kālattil muṟpaṭṭum piṟpaṭṭum irukkalām.
yāḻ vakikaḷ
1.	irāvaṇācura yāḻ
2.	kaccapi yāḻ
3.	kaḷāvati yāḻ
4.	kiṉṉari yāḻ
5.	kīcaka yāḻ
6.	cakōṭa yāḻ (14 allatu 16 narampukaḷiyuṭiyatu)
7.	cīṟi yāḻ (9 narampukaḷiyuṭiyatu)
8.	ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ (7 narampukaḷiyuṭiyatu)
9.	tīkkucci yāḻ
10.	tumpuṟu yāḻ
11.	nārata yāḻ
12.	pirakati yāḻ
13.	pēri yāḻ (21 narampukaḷiyuṭiyatu)
14.	makati yāḻ (peruṅkalam) (21 narampukaḷiyuṭiyatu)
15.	makara yāḻ (19 allatu 17 narampukaḷiyuṭiyatu)
16.	maruttuva yāḻ
17.	muḷari yāḻ (muṇṭaka nalyāḻ)
18.	vallaki yāḻ
19.	varāḷi yāḻ
20.	viṟkōṭṭi yāḻ
21.	vil yāḻ
22.	peruṅkalam (paravi yāḻ) 1008 narampukaḷ pūṭṭappeṟṟatu.
23.	āti yāḻ
24.	tantiri yāḻ
ici ivvāṟu iṉṉum pala uḷa eṉṟu tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.

8.  yāḻ vakikaḷum vaṭivaṅkaḷum
āti kālattil kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ vil yāḻik kaṇṭaṉar.  ammutu makkaḷ kaṇṭa vilyāḻ ātiyil oru narampuṭiyatākavē iruntatu eṉalām.  āṉāl mulli nilattu āyarē kuḻaliyum vil yāḻiyum kaṇṭaṉar eṉap pattup pāṭṭu kūṟukiṟatu.
appāl avarkaḷiṭam eḻunta oli āyvāl iraṇṭu mūṉṟu (nāṉku) aintu narampukaḷiyuṭiya yāḻkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa eṉalām.  iṉṟum irāvaṇātam eṉṟu oru yāḻ uḷḷatu.  atu oṟṟit tēṅkāy ōṭṭiṉāl vīṇātaṇṭam nuḻittu mūṉṟu tantikaḷ pūṭṭi muṟukkāṇi iṟukkappaṭṭa yāḻāka iruk kiṟatu.  itu irāvaṇaṉ taliyip pūṭṭit, taṉ atattit taṇṭāyamittut taṉ narampukaḷi nāṇkaḷākap pūṭṭi icittuc civapirāṉi ēṟṟip pōṟṟip pāṭiyatik kuṟippiṭum ciṉṉamāka aminta yāḻ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.1  iṉṟu itaṉi irāvaṇaṉ kiyāḻ eṉak kūṟulām.
itip paṟṟi nāṭṭiya mēti ār. irañcaṉ taṉatu, nāṭṭiyam eṉṉum tiṅkaḷ itaḻil kuṟippiṭuvatāvatu: irāvaṇātam, 7000-m āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ villiṉāl icikkappaṭṭa tantiravāttiyamāy nilaviyatu.  itu tōliṉāl mūṭṭappaṭṭa attimarak kuṭattiyuṭiyatu.  kuṭattiṉiṉṟu 22 aṅkulamuḷḷa marattaṇṭu oṉṟu cerukappaṭṭirukkum.  nuṉiyil iraṇṭu tuḷiyiṭṭu iru āṇikaḷ pukuttappaṭṭirukkum, āṇi kaḷi muṟukkiṉāl tantikaḷ iṟukum; curuti māṟutal peṟum.  itaṟku mukkāl aṅkula nīḷamuḷḷa kutiriyum uṇṭu.  itaṉ tantikaḷ māṉ narampukaḷāl ceyyap peṟṟavi.  iti mīṭṭa oru villum uṇṭu.
vil, eṉpatu mūṅkil pirampi vaḷittu iru nuṉikaḷilum iḻuttuk kaṭṭi iruppārkaḷ.  atu irāvaṇaṉ kaṇṭupiṭitta vāttiyam.2
itē pōṉṟu orē narampuḷḷa maruttuva yāḻum iruntatu. itaṉi viḷakki innūlil vēṟoriṭattil virivākak kūṟi yuḷḷōm.
kēraḷa nāṭṭic cērnta kaḷḷikkōṭṭikkup pakkatti luḷḷa vaḷḷuva nāṭṭil uḷḷa peruntal maṉṉā eṉṟa ūrukkup pakkattiluḷḷa kāṭukaḷil māṭukaḷ mēykkum puḷḷuvar eṉṉum iṉattavar tam kiyiluḷḷa oru narampuḷḷa vil yāḻil ici mīṭṭi varukiṟārkaḷ.
oru narampuḷḷa tuntināmā eṉṉum icik karuviyi namatu nāṭṭup piccikkārarkaḷ iṉṟum icittu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.
ivi ellām namatu yāḻukku mūlak karuviyāka irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
tiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷōṭu vil yāḻ campantamāy māṟu paṭṭa apippirāyaṅ koṇṭa tiru. ā.a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷ, oru narampuḷḷa vil yāḻilē kuṟiñcip paṇṇiṟkuriya ēḻu kēḷvikaḷiyum icittal kūṭum.  vil yāḻukku vipulāṉantar kūṟum ēḻu kōṭukaḷ avaciyam illi eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.
oru narampuḷḷa vil yāḻiṉiṉṟu 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 9, 11, 14, 16, 17, 19, 21, 100, 1000 narampukaḷ uḷḷa yāḻkaḷit tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭaṉar.  ovvoru yāḻum vevvēṟu uruvam peṟṟatāy amikkap peṟṟiruntatu.  ivikaḷip paṟṟi namakku ōraḷavu ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷtāṉ kiṭittuḷḷaṉavē yoḻiya cumēriyā, ekiptu nāṭukaḷip pōṉṟu acal uruvamō ōviyam ciṟpam pōṉṟavi kaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷō atikam kiṭikkavilli.  tamiḻakattil ippoḻututāṉ, tirumeyyattilum kumpakōṇattilum (tārā curattilum) cila uruvaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.  amarāvatiyilum, (āntirāvilum) vaṭa intiyāvilum cila yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ kiṭit tuḷḷaṉa.  avikaḷip paṟṟi, iṅkuc ciṟitum ārāyvōm.  tiru meyyam, kuṭanti kōyilkaḷil yāḻ, ciṟpaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa.
vilyāḻ
ici oli vakikaḷi (ēṭṭi), narampiṉ maṟi (vētam) eṉavum, paṇkaḷi, yāḻiṉ pakuti eṉavum eṭuttōtiṉār, pērā ciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār.  iti uṉṉippāka uṟṟu uṇarntāl paḻan tamiḻarkaḷ narampuk karuviyākiya yāḻiṉi vittē paṇ kaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ tiṟaṅkaḷiyum vakippaṭuttit tokip paṭutti avikaḷi ulakiṟku aḷittaṉar eṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākum.
ātikālattil makkaḷ vēṭṭiyāṭi uyir vāḻntu vantaṉar.  villil kaṇi ēṟṟi toliyil uḷḷa paṟavikaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ, kāy, kaṉikaḷik kuṟippārttu eyya, kaṇikaḷ virintu pāyum vaṇṇam villil nāṇi viṟippākak kaṭṭuvar.  nāṇiṉ iṟukkatti aṟintu ati viralāl teḷittup pārppar.  nāṇi liruntu kaṇīr eṉṟu oli eḻuntāl nāṇ viṟippāka iruppatāka eṇṇuvar.  nāṇiliruntu eḻum iṉiya ōciyi aṟinta vēṭarkaḷ pallāṇṭāka āyntu vil yāḻi amittaṉar.  vil yāḻi, nal yāḻākka nīṇṭa kālam muyaṉṟu, vil yāḻiṉ nīḷattik kūṭṭiyum kuṟittum, iṟutiyil tam muyaṟciyil veṟṟi kaṇṭaṉar.  appāl iraṇṭu vil yāḻiyum, mūṉṟu vil yāḻiyum, nāṉku vil yāḻiyum iṟutiyāka aintu vil yāḻkaḷiyum iṇittuk kaṭṭiṉar.  ovvoru vil yāḻiṉ narampiyum taḷarttiyum muṟukki yum palvēṟu melivum valivum uḷḷa olikaḷik kaṇṭaṉar.  iṟutiyāka ēḻu vil yāḻkaḷi iṇittu ēḻu narampukaḷi yuṭiya oru niṟivāṉa vil yāḻi amittaṉar1.  itaṉ payaṉāka ēḻu curaṅkaḷum piṟantaṉa.  ovvoru narampukaḷukkum muṟiyē tāram, kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari yeṉa ēḻu curaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ koṭukkap peṟṟaṉa.  appāl nāṉilaṅ kaḷum taṉakkut taṉakkeṉa vevvēṟu vakiyāṉa yāḻkaḷik kaṇṭaṉa eṉṟālum, oru nilam piṟa nilattu yāḻi viruppuṭaṉum mīṭṭivantatu.  āṉiri mēykkum āyaṉ makaṉ kumiḻa marak kompu kaḷi villāka vaḷittu maral nāriṉi narampākak kaṭṭi vil yāḻil kuṟiñcip paṇṇi icittāṉ.  intak kāṭci perum pāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyil peritum viyantu pēcappaṭukiṟatu.  mutaṟ caṅkak kālattiṟku muṉṉar tōṉṟiya vil yāḻi, kaṭiccaṅkap pulavarāṉa kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār eṭuttukkāṭṭiyatu yāḻiṉ toṉmiyiyum vaṉmiyiyum naṉmiyiyum naṉku eṭuttu viḷakkuvataṟkēyākum.
pēriyāḻ piṉṉu makarañ cakōṭamuṭaṉ
cīrpoliyuñ ceṅkōṭu ceppiṉār - tārpolintu
maṉṉun tirumārpa vaṇkūṭaṟ kōmāṉē
piṉṉum uḷavē piṟa
eṉṟa cilappatikāra uri mēṟkōṭ ceyyuḷāl pēri yāḻ, makara yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉṉum nālvaki yāḻkaḷ kaṭiccaṅkak kālattil iruntatākap pulappaṭukiṟatu.  iḵtaṉṟi tamiḻakattil palvēṟu vaki yāḻkaḷum iruntaṉa.  iṉṟikku eṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tōṉṟiyatākak kūṟappaṭum.  kallāṭam eṉṟa nūlil nārata yāḻ, kīcaka yāḻ, tumpuru yāḻ, maruttuva yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, makati yāḻ, kaccapi yāḻ eṉṟu cila yāḻ vakikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  tirunelvēli tiru. kaṅki muttuppiḷḷi avarkaḷ eḻutiya naṭanāti vāttiya rañcaṉam eṉṉum nūlil tīkkuccikki yāḻ, varāḷi yāḻ, vallaki yāḻ, irāvaṇēcura yāḻ pōṉṟa cila yāḻkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kuṟip piṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  caṅkīta rattiṉākaram eṉṉum nūlil, pirakati, kaḷāvati eṉṟa iru yāḻkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
vil yāḻ, pāṭṭāṇmi peṟṟa pāṇarkaḷ kik koṇṭa yāḻalla.  villāṇmi peṟṟa vēṭarkaḷākiya kuṟiñci nilamākkaḷ koṇṭa yāḻākum.  mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē oru narampu pūṭṭiya vil yāḻiliruntu 1008 narampukaḷ pūṭṭiya peruṅkalam eṉṉum pēriyāḻ vari iruntatu eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.  oru kālattil 21 narampukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭa pēriyāḻē peruvaḻakkil iruntu vantatu.  1008 narampukaḷ koṇṭa peruṅkalam (pēriyāḻ) icippataṟku mikavum iṭar tantatu.  eṉavē ēḻu icikaḷiyum melivu camaṉ, valivu eṉṉum mūṉṟu tāṉaṅkaḷilum icippataṟ kēṟṟavaḻiyil 21 narampukaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟa pēriyāḻāka maṟumalarcci aṭintatu.  makara yāḻ 19 narampukaḷip peṟṟatu.  cakōṭa yāḻ 14 narampukaḷiyum, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ 7 narampukaḷiyum peṟṟatu.  iḵtaṉṟi iraṇṭu mūṉṟu - ēṉ?  oru narampiyuṭiya ciṟiya yāḻ kaḷum akkālattil iruntu vantaṉa.
yāḻ mika vaḷivāka iruntatāl aḻakaṟṟum icippataṟku vacatiyaṟṟum iruntatu; eṉavē cakōṭa yāḻil uḷḷa vaḷivāṉa kōṭu eṉṉum uṟuppi nimirtti nērākac ceytu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ ceyyappeṟṟatu.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ civanta marattiṉāl ceyyappeṟṟatu.  ikkālattil uḷḷa vīṇiyip pōlavē itaṟkum ēḻu narampukaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.  itu mikac ciṟiyatāka iruntatāl cīṟi yāḻ eṉṟum kūṟappeṟṟatu..
vaḷarcci
yāḻil uḷḷa kōṭu nimirttappeṟṟatit tavira ataṉ uṟuppukaḷ etuvum māṟṟap peṟavilli.  ataṉ narampukaḷum peyarum māṟṟap peṟavilli.  kōṭu tiruttappeṟṟa yāḻ kōṭṭuvāttiyam (makā vīṇi) eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭatu.  atu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉa aḻikkappeṟṟatu. kōṭu tiruttap peṟṟu cemmiyuṟṟatāl cakōṭa yāḻiṉṟu tampurāvum, kōṭṭu vāttiyamum iṟutiyākac ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻum, tantiri yāḻum malarntirukkalām ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉṉum taṉip peyarip peṟṟatu.
iṉṟiyat tamiḻarkaḷ yāḻik kaṇṭatē illi.  ataṉ iṉṉoliyic cevimaṭukkum pēṟṟiṉip peṟṟatē illi.  yāḻ maṟintu viṭṭālum namatu kōyilkaḷ cilavaṟṟil cili vaṭivam peṟṟu iṉṟum ataṉ uruvam aḻiyātu ilaṅkukiṟatu eṉa ippoḻutu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  yāḻiṉ uruvaṅ kaḷip paṟṟi namatu akarātikaḷil kūṭak kāṇamuṭiyavili.
pattuppāṭṭu, eṭṭuttoki ākiya caṅka nūṟ ceyyuṭ kaḷil tamiḻ muṉṉōrkaḷ icitta paḻiya yāḻkkaruviyiṉ uruvaccāyalum ataṉ uṟuppukaḷākiya pattar pōrvit tōl, kōṭu, āṇi, tivavu, narampiṉ toṭarcci, unti eṉpaṉavaṟṟil amippum, viṉi, mey, payaṉ, uru eṉṉum nālvaki uvamaṅ kaḷiṉ tuṇi koṇṭu naṉku viḷakkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  paṇṭi nāḷil vaḻaṅkiya narampukkaruviyākiya yāḻ eṉpatu maruppu eṉṉum kōṭākiya uṟuppu vaḷiyappeṟṟatāyt tōṟṟattuṭaṉ ceviyāṟ kēṭṭu makiḻaṟku iṉiya ici nalaṅkaḷit tōṟṟuvikkum ciṟappuṭiyatāy viḷaṅkiyatu.
yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ
yāḻiṉi iṉṟu kāṇamuṭiyāviṭṭālum ciṟpa vaṭivil ataṉ uruviṉit tamiḻakattil tirumeyyam (tirumayam), tiruerukkat tampuliyūr, tārācuram mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa kōyilkaḷil iṉṟu kāṇamuṭikiṟatu.  tiruerukkattampiliyūril tiru nīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar ciliyil oru yāḻi avar tāṅki niṟpatik kāṇa muṭikiṟatu.  iḵtaṉṟi āntirāvil uḷḷa amarāvatiyil uḷḷa kōli eṉṉum iṭattil ki. pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻunta ciṟpa vaṭivaṅkaḷil vaḷinta paḻiya cīṟiyāḻ pōṉṟa yāḻiyum kōṭu, nimirnta putiya ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ pōṉṟa aḻakiya yāḻiyum kāṇa muṭikiṟatu.  mēlum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷākiya tirāviṭar kaḷiṉ piṟappiṭamāṉa mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟum cumēriyā nāṭṭiṉ (mecapottāmiyāviṉ) talinakarākiya ūr eṉṉum nakaril akaḻntu kaṇṭa puti kuḻikaḷil 5000āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar vaḻakkilirunta poṟṟakaṭṭāl potiyappeṟṟa nalyāḻ uruvaṅ kaḷip putiporuḷ ārāycci nipuṇarkaḷ kaṇṭeṭut tuḷḷārkaḷ.1  atu tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa yāḻoṭum, mokañcatā rōvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa muttirikaḷil poṟikkappeṟṟa yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷōṭum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyatāyk kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu.1
muṟkālattil tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa yāḻiṉ kōṭu vaḷintu iruntatu; aḻakaṟṟiruntatu.  itu nām kūṟum kūṟṟaṉṟu; namatu pakivarkaḷ kūṟum coṟkaḷumaṉṟu; uṇmiyē atuvākum.  ataṉāl teyvap pulavar tiruvaḷḷuvar tirukkuṟaḷil,
kaṇikoṭi tiyāḻkōṭu cevvitāṅ kaṉṉa
viṉipaṭu pālāṟ koḷal
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār.  itaṟkup poruḷ kaṇṭa parimē laḻakar, ampu, vaṭivāṟ cevvitāyiṉum ceyalāṟ koṭitu.  yāḻ kōṭṭāl vaḷintatāyiṉum ceyalāṟ cevvitu.  avvakiyē tavam ceyvōriyum koṭiyar, cevviyar eṉpatu vaṭivāṟ koḷḷātu avar ceyalpaṭṭa kūṟṟāṉē aṟintu koḷka eṉṟu uri kūṟiyiruppatu yāḻ vaḷintu aḻakaṟṟiruntatu eṉpati meyppippatākum.
vil yāḻ, kuṟiñci nilattilē vāḻnta villarkaḷ kaṇṭa yāḻ eṉat teriya varukiṟatu.  vil yāḻ mulli nilattilē tōṉṟiya yāḻ eṉṟu perumpāṇāṟṟappaṭi kūṟukiṟatu.  enta nilattiyum viṭa kuṟiñci nilattilētāṉ vil yāḻ tōṉṟum cūḻnili atikam. vēṭṭiyāṭi vayiṟu vaḷarkkum vēṭar iṉam kuṟiñci nilattiltāṉ tōṉṟiyatu.  kuṟiñci nilak kuṟavarkaḷukkuttāṉ vēṭṭuvat toḻil uriyatu.  vēṭarkaḷtāṉ mutaṉmutalil villik kaṇṭaṉar.  villit tiṟampaṭap payaṉpaṭuttavum muṟ paṭṭaṉar.  eṉavē avarkaḷ villar eṉum peyariyum peṟṟaṉar.
mēlum, cēra nāṭṭilētāṉ kuṟiñci nila makkaḷiṟ palar kuṭiyēṟiṉar.  cēranāṭṭu makkaḷ kuṟiñci nila makkaḷiṉ nēraṭiyāṉa cantatikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvatu poruttamāṉatu.  cērarkaḷ villarkaḷ eṉa aḻikkappaṭuvar.  cērar āṭci, villik koṭiyākavum muttiriyākavum ēṟṟukkoṇṭatu.  cērarkaḷ iṉṟum, maliyāḷikaḷ, villarkaḷ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.  eṉavē maliyāḷikaḷ kaṇṭa icikkaruviyākiya vil yāḻ, maliyāḻ eṉṟu tiripuṟṟu iṟutiyil yāḻ eṉṉum peyarip peṟṟirukka lām eṉṟu koḷḷalām.
kēraḷa nāṭṭil uḷḷa cāṇār eṉṟa oru vakuppār ēkatantiri vīṇiyip pōṉṟa oru vaki vil yāḻi iṉṟum payaṉpaṭutti varukiṟārkaḷ.  atu kēraḷa nāṭṭup pāmara makkaḷiṉ pāṭalkaḷōṭu (nāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷōṭu) cērttu eṇṇappaṭu kiṟatu.  pīkār, cōṭṭā nākappūr ākiya iṭaṅkaḷilum itu pōṉṟa vilyāḻ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.  tāylāntu, ci-cāṉ-lōvō, lāvō, piyā, pōrṉiyō, pūcōy eṉṟa nāṭukaḷil vil yāḻ pōṉṟa icikkaruvikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṟu intiyāvil pala malippakutikaḷil vāḻum tirāviṭa iṉattic cērntavarkaḷākak karutappaṭum vēṭṭuvarkaḷ vil yāḻip payaṉpaṭutti varukiṟārkaḷ.  inta vil yāḻ cermaṉ nāṭṭilum cupeyiṉ (Spin) nāṭṭilum mūr eṉṉum paḻuppu niṟa makkaḷiṭamum nalla vaḷarcci peṟṟa icik karuviyāyt tikaḻ kiṉṟatu.  itaṉip paḻamiyāṉa piṭil eṉṟu mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.  inta icik karuvi cermaṉ nāṭṭu ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil kūṭa iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.1
vil yāḻum ataṉ aṭippaṭiyil uruvākkap peṟṟa pala yāḻkaḷum tamiḻakattilum vaṭa intiyāvilum piṟa nāṭukaḷilum paraviyuḷḷatu.  iṉṟu anta vil yāḻkaḷ toṭakkak kālattuṭaṉ ulakameṅkumuḷḷa nākarikam peṟāta makkaḷiṭamum palvēṟu peyarkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  teṉ intiyāvil uḷḷa kāṭuri eṉṟa iṉattavarkaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭum orē nāṇiyuṭiya kiṉṉari eṉṉum yāḻ namatu paḻiya vil yāḻuṭaṉ oppu nōkkaṟ kuriyatu, eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.  tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ kūrmā nāṭṭiyattil mūṅkili uṭittuc ceyyap peṟṟa oru vita icik karuvi iṉṟum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.  itu vil yāḻiṉ iṉattic cērntatē yākum.
kēraḷa nāṭṭic cērnta vaḷḷuva nāṭṭu vaṭṭattil uḷḷa peruntal maṉṉā eṉṟa ūrukku aṇmiyil uḷḷa kāṭukaḷil āṭu māṭukaḷi mēyttuk koṇṭum picci eṭuttuk koṇṭum uyir vāḻum nāṭōṭikaḷāṉa puḷḷuvar eṉṉum iṉattavarkaḷ kiyil vil yāḻi vittirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.  inta vil yāḻil oru nuṉiyil maral kayiṟṟiṉ oru nuṉiyik kaṭṭi maṟu nuṉiyi1 vil yāḻiṉ maṟu kōṭiyil uḷḷa taṭuppil kaṭṭātu atik kōttu villiṉ oru pakkamāka iṭakkiyāl iḻuttup piṭittuk koṇṭu valakkiyil oru cilli (piḷācci)k koṇṭu nāṇit teṟikkiṟārkaḷ.  uṭaṉē atiṉiṉṟum ariya nātam eḻukiṟatu.  avarkaḷiṉ iṭatu kiyāl anta nāṇ iḻupaṭuvataṟku ēṟpa oli melintum valintum eḻukiṟatu.  inta vil yāḻi mīṭṭi avarkaḷ miṭaṟṟāl pāṭuvārkaḷ.  avarkaḷatu miṭaṟṟup pāṭṭil perum pakuti tamiḻc coṟkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  inta vil yāḻiṉ nāṇi iṟukak kaṭṭi villākavum payaṉpaṭuttukiṟārkaḷ.2
vil yāḻiṉ toṉmi
vil yāḻ mikamikat toṉmi vāynta tamiḻar kaṇṭa icik karuvi. itutāṉ tamiḻarkaḷ mīṭṭiya āti icikkaruvi.  inta vil yāḻiṉ aṭippaṭiyilē mēṉāṭṭiṉar kaṇṭa ārp (Harp) eṉṉum icikkaruviyum amintirukkiṉṟatu eṉa icip pulavarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.
inta vil yāḻ tamiḻar kaṇṭa yāḻā?  itu caṅka nūṟkaḷil atikam kāṇappaṭavilliyē eṉṟu cila tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ kūṭa viṉavukiṟārkaḷ.  caṅkam maruviya pattup pāṭṭukaḷil oṉṟāṉa, perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyil,
centīt toṭṭa karuntuḷik kuḻaliṉ
iṉṟīm pāli muṉiyiṟ kumiḻiṉ
puḻaṟkōṭṭut toṭutta maraṟpuri narampiṉ
vilyāḻ icikkum viraleṟi kuṟiñcip
palkāṟ paṟavi kiḷicet tōrkkum
pullār viyaṉpulam pōki
eṉṟu kaṭic caṅkap pulavarākiya kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār, toṇṭamāṉ iḷantirayaṉip pukaḻntu pāṭi inta vil yāḻik kuṟippiṭṭiruppatu kūrntu nōkkutaṟkuriyatu.  itil pēriyāḻiṟ pulami vāynta pāṇar, iḷantirayaṉip pāṭip paricu peṟa virumpiyatākavum maṟṟoru pāṇaṉ kāñciyil āṇṭa piṟitoru iḷantirayaṉiyum avaṉ vaḷḷaṟṟaṉmiyiyum kuṟiñci mutaliya aintiṇip pakuppiyum avaṟṟil vāḻvōr vāḻkki muṟiyiyum virittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.  itil pēriyāḻiyum ataṉ uṟuppukaḷiyum kāñci vaḻiyil kāṇap pōkum vil yāḻiyum ataṉ uṟuppukaḷiyum viritturittuḷḷār.  itil, vil yāḻiṟku oru kōṭum oru nāṇum uṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  ataṟkup pattal, kavikkaṭi, vaṟuvāy, puri narampukkōl, tivavu oṟṟuṟuppu mutaliyaṉa illātatāl kuṟippiṭavilli eṉṟum terikiṟatu.  eṉavē ātiyil vil yāḻiṟku oru kōṭum, oru nāṇum iruntatu eṉṟa namatu karutti ippāṭṭu araṇ ceyvatāka miḷirkiṟatu.  inta oru kōṭum oru narampum uḷḷa vilyāḻilē paḻaṅkāla āyaṉ kuṟiñcip paṇṇi mīṭṭi irukka vēṇṭum.  orē narampil ēḻu kēḷvikaḷiyum vācittirukkakkūṭum eṉṟum iṉṟiya aṟiñarkaḷ cilar karutukiṟārkaḷ.  
vilyāḻ, yāḻkaḷiṉ tāy yāḻ
pāṇar, kivaḻi eṉṟa nūlil, tiru. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ aṟiñar vipulāṉantar, yāḻ nūlil, vil yāḻ eṉṟumē ēḻu narampukaḷip peṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu aṟiñar vipulāṉantar karuti ṉāl atu tavaṟākum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.  atēpōl, vil yāḻ eṉṟumē orē narampittāṉ peṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu aṟiñar varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ karutiṉāl atuvum tavaṟē eṉpatu eṉatu karuttu.  toṭakkak kālattil vil yāḻ oru kōṭum oru narampum peṟṟu appāl paṭippaṭiyāy nāḷaṭivil oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, ēḻu kōṭukaḷiyum, ēḻu narampukaḷiyum peṟṟu vilyāḻ virutti aṭintatu eṉpatu eṉatu karuttu.  atōṭu vilyāḻ tāṉ, mutaṉ mutalil kuṟiñciyil tōṉṟiya āti yāḻ eṉpatu eṉatu muṭivāṉa karuttu.  ataṉi aṟiñar vipulā ṉantar tamatu yāḻ nūlil ēṟṟukkoṇṭuḷḷār.1  āṉāl aṟiñar varakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷ pāṇar kivaḻi eṉṉum tam nūlil yāḻ eṉṉum palavitak karuvikaḷ tōṉṟiyapiṉ vil yāḻ arum piyatu eṉṟu kūṟum kūṟṟi eṉṉāl ēṟṟukkoḷḷa muṭiya villi.2  tiruvarakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ kūṟṟukkuc ciṟitum ātāram kiṭiyātu eṉpatu eṉatu muṭipu
villiṉiṉṟē mutaṉmutalāka vilyāḻ arumpi, appāl atu oru narampiṉiṉṟu aintu narampip peṟṟup piṉ ēḻu narampip peṟṟu vaḷarcciyuṟṟup pēri yāḻ, makara yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ mutaliyavikaḷum āti yāḻ eṉṉum āyiram narampuṭiya peruṅkalamum eḻuntaṉa eṉpatit teḷivāka tiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷiṉ māṇavarkaḷil oruvarākiya tiru. veḷḷivāraṇaṉ paṉṉiru tirumuṟi varalāṟu eṉṉum tam periya nūlil naṉku viḷakkiyuḷḷār.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
muṟkālattil vēṭṭit toḻili mēṟkoṇṭa makkaḷ ampukaḷi eyyuṅkāl avi neṭuntūram virintu celluva taṟku ēṟṟavaṇṇam villiṉkaṇ nāṇi iṟukak kaṭṭiṉar.  vil nāṇiṉ iṟukkattit terintu koḷvataṟku nāṇiṉi viralāl teṟittu atiliruntu eḻum ōciyi aḷavu karuviyākak koṇ ṭaṉar.  vil nāṇiṉ ōciyik kēṭṭu ataṉ ici nuṭpatti uṇarnta nam muṉṉōrkaḷ icit tōṟṟattiṟku iṭaṉāyuḷḷa vil nāṇiṉ nīḷattik kuṟittuṅ kūṭṭiyum, palvēṟu iṉṉicic curutikaḷit tōṟṟuvittaṉar.  ivvāṟu vevvēṟu iṉṉicic curutikaḷit tarum pala viṟkaḷi oṉṟākac cērttu neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉ nam muṉṉōrkaḷāl amittuk koḷḷap peṟṟa narampuk karuvi, vil yāḻ eṉpatākum.
mikap paḻaṅkālattil tōṉṟiya vil yāḻ ākiya ik karuviyi aṭippaṭiyākakkoṇṭē ici vaḷarccikku iṉṟiyamiyāta pēri yāḻ, makara yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉṉum nālvaki yāḻum āyiram narampukaḷiyuṭiya peruṅkalam eṉṉum ātiyāḻum piṟa narampuk karuvikaḷum nuṇṇuṇarvuṭiya perumakkaḷāl āyntuṇarntu amikkappeṟṟaṉa.
villiṉiṉṟu oru narampuṭiya vil yāḻum piṉ 5 narampukaḷi yuṭiya vil yāḻum appāl 7 narampukaḷiyuṭiya vil yāḻum piṉṉar cīṟi yāḻ, pēri yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, makara yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ, āti yāḻ pōṉṟa palavakiyāṉa yāḻkaḷum piṟantaṉa eṉṉum namatu karutti vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷiṉ ciṟanta māṇava rākiya tiru veḷḷi vāraṇaṉār avarkaḷ tamatu nūlil teḷi vāka viḷakkik kūṟiyiruppatu kaṇṭu kaḷippuṟukiṉṟōm.  atu, uḷḷatu ciṟattal eṉṟa pariṇāmavāta cittāntattiṟkum aṟiviṟ kum ici nūlkaḷukkum ēṟpuṭittāy irukkiṟatu.
piṟa nāṭukaḷil vil yāḻ
mēṉāṭṭup pērāciriyar pāṉum eṉpavar ekiptiyac cuvar ōviyaṅkaḷil vil yāḻ, kiṟittava ūḻi eḻuvataṟku 3000-m āṇṭu mutal 4000ām āṇṭu variyuḷḷa kāla elliyi uṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.  tamiḻakattil kiṭikkum cāṉṟukaḷ mūlam yāḻ eṉṉum toṉmiyāṉa icik karuviyē ekiptiyac cuvar ōviyaṅkaḷil oḷirkiṟatu eṉpati nām naṉku meyppikka muṭiyum.  mēlum āciriyar pāṉum ariṭāṭṭilum kuṟippiṭum ki. mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭiluḷḷa curuti vāttiya iciyic cērnta icic karuvikaḷum aṭiyiliruntu nuṉivari nāṇkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭatāka iruntaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  ittakiya tamiḻaka icik karuvika ḷāṉa ēka tantiri vīṇi paṇṭik kāla vil vaṭivamāṉa icik karuviyiṉiṉṟu piṟkālattil tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  mēlum, ekiptiyarkaḷ paṇṭu eṉṟa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantu ekiptil kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ eṉṟu taṅkaḷik kūṟi varukiṟārkaḷ.  paṇṭu nāṭu eṉpatu pāṇṭiya nāṭu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ.1  itaṉ mūlam ekiptiyarkaḷukkum tamiḻarkaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu naṉku terikiṟatu.  itaṉāl tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu vil yāḻ ekiptiṟku pōyirukkalām eṉṟum eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
aitarāpāttil uḷḷa ceñcu makkaḷiṭam kiṉṉari eṉṟa icik karuvi uḷḷatu.  atu mūṉṟu curak kuṭukkiyiyum oli parappum palakiyiyum iru tantikaḷiyum uṭiyatu.  atu tiṟanta mūṅkil nuṉikaḷi uṭiyatu.  ivvicik karuvi iru narampukaḷi yuṭiya yāḻ iṉattit cērntatu.  itaṉi vīṇi eṉpārumuḷar.  itit tuvi tantirikā eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.  ceñcu iṉattavarkaḷ eppoḻutum inta yāḻiyum villiyum ampiyum tāṅkit tirikiṉṟaṉar.  villāl ciṟu paṟavikaḷi eytu koṉṟu pucippar.  vil taṟkāppiṟkum payaṉ paṭuttappaṭum.  ivarkaḷatu vil yāḻip pārttāl itu vil yāḻiṉiṉṟu kiḷitta kiṉṉari yāḻ eṉṟu naṉku teriyum.  kiṉṉari yāḻ vaḷarcci aṭintu pattu meṭṭu kaḷum mūṉṟu curak kuṭukkikaḷum peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉṟu pulappaṭum.1
aṟiñar, eyc pāl pōr eṉpavar tam nūlil kuṟiñci nila vēṭarkaḷiṉ villē ellā vitamāṉa tantik karuvikaḷukkum muṉṉōṭiyāka mūlakkaruvāka irukkiṟatu.2  eṉṟu kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār.  itu nam karutti naṉku araṇ ceyvatāka irukkiṟatu.  vil yāḻ piṟkālattil eḻuntatu eṉṟu karutum tiru. ā. a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷ tam pāṇar ki vaḻiyil kūṟum kūṟṟiṟku nalla maṟuppākat tikaḻkiṟatu.3
mutaṉmutalil oliyi eḻuppum karuvi oṉṟu tōṉṟi yiruttal vēṇṭum.  ikkaruvi evarāl ekkālattil evvuru vōṭu?  evvāṟu?  epporuḷāl ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉat taṟkālat tavarākiya  nām kūṟutaṟkiyalātu.  eṉiṉum orumūlap poruḷ iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatu tuṇipu.  tunti nāmā eṉṟa karuvi yāḻiṟku mūlakkaruviyāka iruttal kūṭum eṉac cila ārāycci yāḷar karutukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu tiru. ā. a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.4  tunti nāmāvik kuṟippiṭuvatu enta ārāycci yāḷar?  ataṟku eṉṉa ātāram?  vilyāḻ ellā narampuk karuvi kaḷukkum aṭippaṭiyāka  irukkiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇaṟṟa ātāraṅ kaḷuṭaṉ piṟar kūṟum karutti ivar ēṉ maṟukkavēṇṭum?  eṉpatu eṉakkup pulappaṭavilli.  tiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ vilyāḻ mutalil tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu kūṟuvati eppaṭiyum maṟuttuk kūṟavēṇṭum eṉṟa orē eṇṇattiltāṉ intat tavaṟāṉa muṭivik kaṇṭuḷḷār eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ.
vilyāḻ, mutalil oru narampōṭu tōṉṟi, appāl aintu narampiṟku vantu, iṟutiyil ēḻuvil iṇikkappaṭṭu ēḻu narampip peṟṟatu eṉṟa karuttip pala ici aṟiñarkaḷum ēṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  namatu tamiḻk kalikkaḷañciyattilum intak karuttu ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
villiṉ kaṇṇē kaṭṭappaṭṭa nāṇiṉ nātattik kēṭ ṭuṇarnta muṉṉōrkaḷ palvēṟu ōciyamiya nāṇ kaṭṭappaṭṭa viṟkaḷi oṉṟāyc cērttu mutaṉmutalil vācitta narampukkaruvi yāḻākum.1
kaṭiccaṅkak kālattilum ataṟku muṉṉum tōṉṟiya cāṉṟōrkaḷiṉ ceyyuṭkaḷilē vil yāḻum cīṟi yāḻum, pēri yāḻum kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭiyil pēriyāḻum vilyāḻum kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  cilappatikārattilē cakōṭa yāḻum makara yāḻum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻum kuṟikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
ivi, maṇimēkali, cīvakacintāmaṇi peruṅkati mutaliya vikaḷilum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻarkaḷ ātiyil kaṇṭa vil yāḻi yotta icik karuvikaḷ mēṉāṭeṅkum paraviyuḷḷaṉa.  iṉṟu avikaḷi ārp (Harp), layar (Lyre), lūṭ (Lute) eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar.  itip paṟṟic ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakap pērāciriyarāy irunta tiru. vi. ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ maṇimalar eṉṉum kaṭṭurit tokutiyil paṇṭit tamiḻar perumi eṉṟa uriyil, teṉṉāṭṭiliruntu tamiḻar nākarikam urōmāpurikkum kirēkka nāṭṭiṟkum paraviyatu.  iṉṉum takkaṇa pīṭapūmiyic cērnta vēṭṭuvar eṉṟa kuṟiñci makkaḷ yavaṉa nāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu aṅku taṅkaḷ araci nilināṭṭit taṅkaḷ peyariyum an nāṭṭiṟkuk koṭuttārkaḷ.  atuvē kirīṭ nāṭu (crete).  itaṉ nākarikattiṟkum tamiḻ nākarīkattiṟkum oppuvami mikutiyāy uḷḷatu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  cilappatikārattil vaṉcol yavaṉar vaḷanāṭāṇṭu eṉṟum patiṟṟuppattu iraṇṭām patikattil, nayaṉil vaṉcol yavaṉar piṇittu eṉavum kūṟap paṭuvatik kāṇumpoḻutu cērarkaḷ yavaṉa nāṭu pōntu, aṅkup pōrāṭi, yavaṉa nāṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyil veṟṟi peṟṟu āṭci naṭatti irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
villarkaḷ kaṇṭatu vilyāḻ
cērarkaḷ villarkaḷ, maliyāḷikaḷ eṉṟu paṇṭu toṭṭu aḻikkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.  kuṟiñci nila makkaḷiṉ nērāṉa vaḻiyil vanta maliyāḷikaḷ kaṇṭa yāḻ, mali yāḻ vil yāḻ eṉṟu tiripuṟṟu iṟutiyil yāḻ eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ nilaviyatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ cāṉṟu kāṭṭi uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar.
vil yāḻiṉ amippiṉi yoṭṭi ekiptilum papilōṉi lum icikkaruvikaḷ eḻuntuḷḷaṉa.  iṉṟu aṅku naṭipeṟṟa akaḻ ārāycciyil palavita yāḻkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ekiptu nāṭṭiluḷḷa kallaṟikaḷilum kaltēyā (cālṭiyā) nāṭṭiluḷḷa ūr eṉṉum iṭattiluḷḷa aracu kuṭumpattār kaḷiṉ putikuḻiyilum iruntu yāḻkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa.  intap paḻamperum yāḻkaḷ ki. pi. 2600āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷaṉa.  ivi iṉṟu īrāk nāṭṭiṉ talinakarākiya paktāt nakarat tolporuḷ kāṭcicāliyil vikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.1  mēlum ūr nakaril naṭipeṟṟa akaḻ ārāycciyil kiṭitta maṟṟoru yāḻiṉ uruvappaṭamum iṅku veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  itil pattar nīṇṭa caturappeṭṭi pōl iruk kiṟatu.  iru kōṭukaḷum 7 narampukaḷum uḷḷaṉa.  yāḻiṉ nuṉiyil oru erutiṉ tali ēṟṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  atu poṟṟakaṭṭāl ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ekiptil mīṭṭappeṟṟa vilyāḻ naṅkā eṉappaṭum.  inta yāḻ iṉṟu piriṭṭiṣ tolporuḷ kāṭcicāliyil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.  ataṉ varipaṭamum iṉṉum pala ekiptil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa palvēṟu vakiyāṉa paṭaṅkaḷum nūṟkaḷil cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟāl vācakarkaḷ yāḻiṉ vaḷarcciyi yum atu veḷināṭṭilum pērum perumiyum peṟṟa viparamum naṉku aṟiyalām.  ūr nakaril kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa maṟṟoru yāḻ māṭṭiṉ talivikkappaṭṭa muntiya yāḻ uruvattiṉiṉṟu muṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭatu.  itu tamiḻaka yāḻiṉ aṭippaṭiyil naṉku amintuḷḷatu.  pattar peṭṭi pōl ceyyappeṟṟu, ataṉ naṭuvē kaṉṟiṉ tali oṉṟu poṉṉum maṇiyum iḻittu aḻakuṟac ceytuvikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  pattariṉiṉṟu mēlē nīṇṭa kōṭu oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  itil patiṉoru narampukaḷ pūṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  itu ūr nakariluḷḷa aracarkaḷiṉ kallaṟikaḷiṉiṉṟu eṭukkappeṟṟatu.  (ki. mu. 26, 27 nūṟṟāṇṭil uḷḷatu) inta yāḻ uruvamum iṅku iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.1
vil yāḻiṉ amippu
4500 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ cumēriya nāṭṭilum ekiptu nāṭṭilum kiṭitta palvēṟu vakiyāṉa yāḻkaḷip pōṉṟu mokañcatārōvilum aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muntiya yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻakattil ātiyil arumpiya vil yāḻ 6-cāṇ 54 aṅkula nīḷamum 6 viral atāvatu 4½ aṅkulattiṟkuk kuṟiyāta cuṟṟaḷa vuḷḷa nērāṉa 7-kumiḻaṅ kompukaḷ koṇṭu amikkappeṟṟatu.2  atōṭu mēṟkuṟitta cuṟṟaḷavuḷḷa 5-cāṇ 45 aṅkulattil 2, iraṇṭu cāṇ 4 viral 21 aṅkula nīḷattil 1, iraṇṭu cāṇ-18 aṅkula nīḷattil 16, oru cāṇ 4  viral 12 aṅkula nīḷattil 2 kompukaḷumāka 21 kompukaḷ koṇṭu amikkappeṟṟatu.
cuttamāṉa maraliṉ melliya nāri nūlākki narampu pōl tirittu vēṇṭiya parumaṉil ceytu koḷvar.  kampukaḷik kaṭṭu vataṟkut taṉiyākac caṇaṟkayiṟṟit tirittu vittukkoḷvar.  āṟu cāṇ-54 aṅkula nīḷamuḷḷa kompi vil pōl vaḷittu iru maruṅkiṉum 45 aṅkula nīḷamuḷḷa kompiṉi vittu iṇittu ēṉiya ciṟu kompukaḷik kuṟukkāka vittuk kaṭṭi ēḻu viṟkaḷi iṇiyavittu iruppārkaḷ.  7 narampukaḷ kaṭṭi maṟu talippil muṟukkāṇikaḷi amittu oṟṟuṟuppum iṇip pārkaḷ.  muṟukkāṇiyāl narampiṉi melittal valittal ceyyap paṭum.3
tamiḻakattil mutaṉmutalāka eḻunta vil yāḻ viṟkōṭṭi yāḻāka malarntu, vēṟupala yāḻkaḷ eḻavum tuṇiyāka iruntatu.4   inta vilyāḻiṉ uruvatti namakku mutaṉ mutalāka yāḻ nūliṉ vāyilāka tiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉār.  avar kaḷukkut tamiḻ ulakam eṉṟum naṉṟi pārāṭṭak kaṭamip paṭṭuḷḷatu.
iṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu yāḻiṉ peyar kaḷiyum ataṉ uṟuppukaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭu kiṟārkaḷ.  āṉāl avikaḷiṉ uṇmiyāṉa uruvatti evarā lum kāṭṭamuṭiyavilli.  aṇmiyil tamiḻakattil tirumayatti lum, (tirumeyyattilum) tiruerukkattampuliyūrilum iruntu iru vitamāṉa yāḻiṉ ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  iṉṟu veḷivantuḷḷa pala yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷip paṭittu ataṟkēṟpak kaṟpaṉiyil uruvākkiya yāḻkaḷēyoḻiya acal yāḻ uruvam eṉṟu colvataṟkilli.  kaṭanta pattu āṇṭu kaḷāka namatu nāṭu muḻuvatum yāṉ cuṟṟiyalintu pala yāḻiṉ ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷip paṭam piṭittu vittuḷḷēṉ.  āṉāl avi kaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi eṉṉāl kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyavilli.  ici aṟiñarkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu avikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi aṟintu virivil oru nalla uyariya nūl veḷiyiṭa eṇṇiyuḷḷēṉ.  
2. peruṅkalam (pēriyāḻ)
pēriyāḻ eṉṉum peyari nammil palar kēṭṭē aṟiyōm.  cila tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil intap peyarip paṭit tiruppārkaḷ.  āṉāl itaṉ uruvattip pārttavarkaḷ tamiḻakat til aritāka iruppar.
pēriyāḻ piṉṉum makarañ cakōṭamuṭaṉ
cīrpoliyum ceṅkōṭu ceppiṉār-tārpolintu
maṉṉum tirumārp vaṇkūṭaṟ kōmāṉē
piṉṉum uḷavē piṟa
eṉṟu cilappatikāra uri kūṟukiṟatu.  itaṉ mūlam muṟkālattil pēri yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ mutaliya yāḻkaḷ cilappatikāra āciriyar kālattil iruntatāka aṟikiṉṟōm.
yāḻkaḷil peruṅkalam peruñciṟappip peṟṟa ariya yāḻākum.  muṟkālattil pēriyāḻ, peruṅkalam eṉṟum paravi yāḻ eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa.  itaṉ kōṭu 12 cāṇum vaṇar 1 cāṇum pattar 12 cāṇum ataṟkēṟpa āṇikaḷum tivavum untiyum peṟṟu āyiram narampu pūṭṭappeṟṟiruntatu.  itu oru vakip pēriyāḻ.
āyiram narampiṟ ṟātiyā ḻāku
mēṉi yuṟuppu moppaṉa koḷalē
pattara taḷavuṅ kōṭṭi ṉataḷavu
motta veṉpa virumūṉ ṟiraṭṭi
vaṇarcā ṇoḻitteṉa vittaṉar pulavar
eṉṟum,
talamuta lūḻiyiṟ ṟāṉavar tarukkaṟap
pulamaka ḷāḷar purinarap pāyiram
valipeṟat toṭutta vākkami pēriyāḻc
celavumuṟi ellāñ ceykiyiṟ ṟerintu
maṟṟi yāḻuṅ kaṟṟumuṟi piḻiyāṉ
eṉṟum cilappatikārattil aṭiyārkkunallār urip pāyirat tuḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
itaṉāl pēriyāḻ, kōṭum pattarum vaṇarūm tivavum (vārkkaṭṭum) kōlkaḷum (purinarampukaḷum) untiyum peṟṟirunta teṉa aṟikiṟōm.  inta yāḻ kuyiluvōrkaḷiṉ, vayiṟṟōṭu cērkkappaṭṭum iṭatu tōḷpakkam aṇikkap peṟṟum kuyilap peṟṟatu.
toṭiyami kēṉvi yiṭavayiṟ ṟaḻīi
iṭattōṭ pakkattē aṇittu
eṉṟu perupāṇāṟṟuppaṭip kūṟuvataṉāl pēriyāḻi eppaṭi vittu mīṭṭiṉar eṉṟu pulappaṭum.  peruṅkalam, mutaṟ caṅkak kālattil nilaviyatu.  appāl atu mutal ūḻiyilē aḻintu pōyiṟṟu eṉṟum terikiṟatu.
1000-narampukaḷiyuṭiya pēriyāḻ ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu maṟṟō riṭattiṟkut tūkkic cella muṭiyātiruntatu.  mēlum ati vittukkoḷḷa oru periya maṇṭapam tēviyāy iruntatu; kuyilavum payilavum aritāka iruntatu.  eṉavē iyaṟkiyiṉ kōpattāl mutal ūḻiyil maṟintu pōṉa peruṅkalam mīṇṭum maṟumalarcci peṟāmal maṟintu viṭṭatu.
1000-narampukaḷiyuṭiya peruṅkalam eṉṟa paravi yāḻ oru poḻutum iruntirukka muṭiyātu; atu aṟiñarkaḷ kaṟpaṉiyil kaṇṭa oru ceytiyēyoḻiya uṇmiyāka irātu eṉṟu mēṉāṭṭu nākarika mōkaṅkoṇṭa iṉṟiya iḷiñarkaḷ cilar navilkiṉṟaṉar.  itu tavaṟāṉa karuttēyākum.  cumār āyiram narampukaḷi koṇṭa yāḻ pōṉṟa icikkaruvi ittāli nāṭṭil iṉṟum icikkappeṟukiṉṟatu.  curamaṇṭalam eṉṉum inta ittāli vāttiyam iṉṟum iruppati nampum namatu naṇparkaḷ 1000-narampukaḷiyuṭiya peruṅkalam iruntati nampa maṟuppatu ēṉ?  eṉṟu terivilli.  itiviṭa vinti, iṉṟu ittāliyil 1000-narampukaḷiyuṭiya curamaṇṭala vāttiyam irukkalām; muṟkālattil vaṭintiyāvil 1000-narampukaḷi yuṭiya nāratap pēriyāḻum iruntirukkalām; āṉāl tamiḻakattil cumār 5000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ 1000-narampukaḷiyuṭiya peruṅkalam iruntatu eṉpati nampamuṭiyātu eṉṟu kūṟum tamiḻ iḷiñarkaḷ pōkkiṟku eṉ colvatu?
peruṅkalam eṉum ātiyāḻ mutal ūḻiyilē iruntatu.  arakkarkaḷ narampuk karuviyi icippatil nikaraṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉar.  avarkaḷ tarukkiṉi akaṟṟa nallaṟivuṭiya aṟiñarkaḷ 1000-narampukaḷiyuṭiya āti yāḻi amittaṉar eṉṟu peruṅkati kūṟukiṟatu.
inta yāḻ 12 cāṇ nīḷamum kōṭu vaḷiyoḻittu 12 cāṇ nīḷamum uṭiyaṉavām.  yāḻukkuriya ēṉiya uṟuppukaḷ mēṟkūṟiya aḷaviṟkēṟpa amikkappaṭuvaṉa veṉṟum aṭiyārkkunallār eṭuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
curutikaḷiṉ elli irunūṟu eṉṟum, avikaḷi meli viṟku melivu, melivu, camaṉ, valivu, valiviṟku valivu eṉṉum aintu tāṉaṅkaḷilum vikka narampukaḷiṉ toki 1000 eṉṟum, ittakiya nuṇṇiya curutikaḷi vācippataṟkup payaṉpaṭum muṟiyil tamiḻ makkaḷāl ātiyil amittukkoḷḷappaṭṭa ariya yāḻ karuviyē āyiram narampukaḷiyuṭiya ātiyāḻ eṉṟum peruṅkalam eṉṟum aruṭṭiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷār tam cīriya yāḻ nūlil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  iti maṟuttuk kūṟu vēṇṭiyatu avaciyamē illi.  atuvē uṇmi eṉa muṟiyāka icip payiṟciyuṭiyār palarum oppukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.
pēriyāḻ
peruṅkalam eṉṉum ātiyāḻ ōriṭattil vittu icip pataṟku ēṟṟatēyoḻiya pāṇarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ cella virumpum iṭaṅkaḷukkuk kiyil tāṅkic celvataṟku ēṟṟatāka illi.  eṉavē piṟkālattil pāṇarkaḷ eḷitil tūkkic celvataṟkum eḷitil ceyvataṟkum eḷimiyāka icippataṟkum ēṟṟavāṟu irupattoru narampukaḷiyuṭiya pēri yāḻ uruvākkap peṟṟatu.
intap putiya-kikku aṭakkamāṉa pēriyāḻip paṟṟic caṅka nūṟkaḷil kuṟippiṭap peṟṟu, aṟiñarkaḷāl uṇarappeṟum ceytikaḷ pala uḷa.  avi pēriyāḻ periya iciyiṉi eḻuppa vallatu.  itu ici ilakkaṇa viti tiṟampātu ceyyappeṟṟa ariya yāḻ.  pāṭiṉiyiṉ, pāl pōṉṟa veḷiṟiya kikaḷil iruntu pin tamiḻp paṇṇi olittu vantatu.  atu māṉ kuḷampu aḻuttiya iṭam pōṉṟu irupuṟamum tāḻntu naṭuvuyarnta pattari uṭiyatu.  iḷiya cūliyuṭiya cenniṟam cērnta cēyiḻiyār vayiṟṟi luḷḷa aitākiya muṭiyiṉip pōṉṟu irupuṟamum cērttuk kaṭṭap peṟṟa tōl pōrviyiyuṭiyatu.  naṇṭiṉ kaṇ pōṉṟa tuṟi kaḷiṉ vāyi maṟittaṟkuriya āṇikaḷ māṭṭap peṟṟatu.  eṭṭām nāḷ iravil eḻum tiṅkaḷ vaṭivil vaṟuvāy eṉṉum uṟuppu ciṟappuṟa amintirukkum.  taṇṭil aṭuttaṭuttu vārkaḷ (tilavukaḷ) karumi niṟam vāynta kārikiyar muṉ kikaḷil ilaṅkum aḻakiya vaḷiyalkaḷ pōl nekiḻattakkatāy ilaṅkum, tiṉi arici pōṉṟa viralkaḷāl iyakkum kuṟṟamaṟṟa poṉṉiṟam pōṉṟa aḻakiya narampukaḷāl pūṭṭappeṟṟirukkum.  putiya yāḻ aḻaku ceyyappeṟṟa iḷam maṇamakaḷiyottirukkum eṉṟel lām kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
pēriyāḻiṉ tōṟṟam
itu narampukaḷ muṟukkik kaṭṭappeṟṟu iṉṉoli eḻuppuvatāy amintirukkum.  vaṉappu vāynta varakuk katiril variciyāka varakukaḷ oḻuṅkuṟa amittu iruppatupōl yāḻiṉ nīḷattil neruṅkiya variciyākac ciṟiya tuḷikaḷ pōṭṭu cuḷḷāṇikaḷ aṭikkappaṭṭirukkum.  pattariṉ kuṟukkē tantattāṟ ceyyappeṟṟa yāppup poruttap peṟṟirukkum.  antap pattar piciṉāl oṭṭappeṟṟa poṉṉiṟamāṉa tōl pōrviyāl pōrttappaṭṭirukkum.  yāḻiṉ maruppu vaḷintu arattiṉāl arāvi, nimirntu oḷirum kaḷām paḻattiṉ karumiyāṉa kaviṉ perum vaṇṇampōl kāṭci aḷikkum eṉṟu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
peruṅkalattil muṟukkāṇi (māṭakam) amikkappaṭa villi.  narampukaḷit tuvakkum vārkkaṭṭākiya tivavu avi kaḷi melittal valittal ceyyap payaṉpaṭum.
māyōḷ muṉki āytoṭi kaṭukkum
kaṇkūṭu irukki tiṇpiṇit tivaviṉ
eṉṟu porunar āṟṟuppaṭi kūṟukiṟatu.  itaṉ poruḷ kariya niṟattiyuṭiya, tāyt teyvamākiya paḻiyōḷiṉ muṉkiyil uḷḷa vaḷi pōṉṟu tivavu viḷaṅkiyatu eṉpatākum.
neṭum paṇit tiraṭōḷ maṭanti muṉkik
kuṟun toṭi yēykkum melintu vīṅku tivavu
eṉṟu perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi kūṟukiṟatu.  neṭiya mūṅkilip pōṉṟa tiraṇṭa tōḷkaḷiyuṭiya makaḷir muṉkivaḷi pōṉṟu nekiḻa vēṇṭiya poḻutu nekiḻntum iṟuka vēṇṭiya poḻutu iṟukum vārkkaṭṭu eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷākum.
piṅka ṇūkam pāmpupiṭit taṉṉa
aṅkōṭṭuc ceṟinta aviḻntuvīṅku tivaviṉ
eṉṟu ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi aḻakuṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟatu.  itaṉ poruḷ:  paciya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya karuṅkuraṅku pāmpiṉ taliyip piṭittāl appāmpu kiyic cuṟṟikkoṇṭu oru kāl iṟukavum oru kāl nekiḻavum ceyvatu pōṉṟa aḻakiṉiyuṭiya yāḻiṉ taṇṭiṭattē ceṟiyac cuṟṟiṉ nekiḻa vēṇṭiya iṭattu nekiḻntum iṟukavēṇṭiya iṭattum iṟukiyum narampu tuvakkum vārkkaṭṭu eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
itaṉāl tivavukaḷ puri narampukaḷālāṉa tiraṇṭa tiṇmi yāṉa kōlkaḷik kaṭṭi caṟṟu uṟaḻcciyōṭu kūṭi kōṭṭuṟuppil kāṇappaṭum vārkkaṭṭukaḷ eṉpatu teḷivākkap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  māṭakattiluḷḷa āṇikaḷil kaṭṭappeṟṟa paṇmoḻi narampuka ḷallāta tiṇmiyāṉa puri narampākiya kōlkaḷik kaṭṭum vārkkaṭṭukaḷ tivavukaḷ eṉat terikiṟatu.  māṭakattil kaṭṭap peṟum narampukaḷ vēṟu; tivavukaḷil kaṭṭappeṟum narampukaḷ vēṟu eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
itōṭu tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi, malipaṭukaṭām āciriyar kaḷum uriyāciriyarkaḷum kūṟuvatiliruntu pēriyāḻ irupat toru narampukaḷik koṇṭatu eṉṟum, oṉpatu tivavukaḷ uṭiya oru kaviyum pēriyāḻ eṉṟa peyarāl viḷaṅkiyatiyum aṟiyalām.
mēlum yāḻ narampukaḷ melliyaṉavāka irukkum eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  ciṟitum kuṟṟamaṟṟavaṉavākavum aḻakākavum irukkum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  itiṉiṉṟu eḻum oli amiḻtu polintu irukkum eṉṟu terikiṟatu.
toṭittiri vaṉṉa toṇṭupaṭu tivaviṉ
kaṭippaki yaṉittuṅ kēḷvi pōkāk
kural orttut toṭutta cukirpuri narampiṉ
arali tīra urīi varakiṉ
kuralvārn taṉṉa nuṇṭuḷi irīic
cilampami pattal paciyoṭu cērtti
ilaṅkutuḷi ceṟiya āṇi muṭukkip
putuvatu puṉinta veṇki yāppamittu
putuvatu pōrtta poṉpōṟ pacci
vatuvi nāṟum vaṇṭukama ḻimpāl
maṭanti māṇṭa nuṭaṅkeḻi lākattu
aṭaṅkumayir oḻukiya avvāy kaṭuppa
akaṭu cērpu porunti aḷaviṉiṟ ṟiriyātu
kavaṭupaṭak kaliiya ceṉṟuvāṅ kunti
nuṇaṅkaram nuvaṟiya nuṇṇīr - māmik
kaḷaṅkaṉi yaṉṉa kataḻntukiḷar uruviṉ
vaṇarntutēntu maruppiṉ vaḷḷuyirp pēriyāḻ					malipaṭukaṭām.
itil tivavu, narampu,. pacci, unti, maṟuppu mutaliya uṟuppukaḷ pēriyāḻukku iruntaṉa eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭukiṟatu.  mēlum, toṇṭupaṭu tivavueṉpataṉāl tivavukku oṉṟāka narampum oṉpatu uḷa eṉṟu peṟappaṭukiṟatu.
innālvaki yāḻiṟkum narampu koḷḷumiṭattup
pēriyāḻiṟku irupattoṉṟu.............
eṉṟum,
oṉṟu mirupatu moṉpatum pattuṭaṉē
niṉṟa patiṉāṉkum piṉṉēḻum - kuṉṟāta
nālvaki yāḻiṟku naṉṉarampu coṉmuṟiyē
mēlvaki nūlōr viti
eṉṟa cilappatikāra urikkup pāṭapētam uṇṭu eṉṟum narampukaḷ kūṭiyum kuṟintum irukkalām eṉṟum vipulāṉantar kūṟukiṉṟār.
pēri yāḻ eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari iruntirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  itaṉic cīvaka cintāmaṇi āciriyar paravi yāḻ eṉak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.  itaṟku vaṟuvāy, kavik kaṭi, tivavu, taṇṭu (kōṭu), narampiṉ toṭiyami kēḷvikōl, pōrvi, pōrviyip pottaluṟu nuṇtuḷi iṟukkum āṇi, pattal muṟukkāṇi, unti, veṇkāyāppu mutaliya ellā uṟuppukaḷum iruntaṉaveṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu.
irupattoru narampukaḷiyuṭiya pēri yāḻum pattoṉpatu narampukaḷiyuṭiya makara yāḻum, patiṉāṉku narampukaḷiyuṭiya cakōṭayāḻum, ēḻu narampukaḷi uṭiya ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻum akkālattil tamiḻakattil celvākkup peṟṟiruntaṉaveṉa naṉkaṟikiṟōm.  cilappatikārattiṟ kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷa ceymuṟik kēḷvi eṉṟa yāḻkkaruviyiyē cakōṭa yāḻ eṉṟu kūṟappeṟṟa tākap peyarmāṟṟal kuṟittu vipulāṉantar, ariya karuttiṉi yāḻnūlil kūṟiyuḷḷār.
cakōṭa yāḻ
caṅkak kāla yāḻkaḷ nāṉkiṉuḷ cakōṭa yāḻum oṉṟu.  inta yāḻ mutaṟcaṅkak kālantoṭṭu tiruñāṉa campantappiḷḷiyār kālam vari nilavi iruntatu eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ uḷa.  cilappatikārattil,
īrēḻ toṭutta cemmuṟik kēḷvi	(cilap. araṅ. 70)
eṉṟum,
piḻiyā marapiṉīrēḻ kōvi	(cilap. vēṉiṟ 31)
eṉṟum,
aṇivuṟak kiṭanta yāḻiṉ ṟokutiyum
īrēḻ cakōṭamu miṭinilip pāliyum					(cilap. 10 nāṭu. kaṭṭuri vari 13-14)
eṉṟum cakōṭayāḻ pōṟṟappeṟukiṟatu.  ippāṭalukku uri kaṇṭa aṭiyārkkunallār, mayaṅkā marapiṉiyuṭiya ip patiṉāṟ kōviyākiya cakōṭayāḻ eṉṟu teḷivāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭic cakōṭayāḻ cilappatikārak kālattilē ciṟappuṟṟiruntatu eṉpati nilināṭṭukiṟār.  iti, tiruvarakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉ pāṇar kivaḻiyil cilappatikārattil cakōṭa yāḻ kuṟippiṭa villi eṉṟu avaciyamiṉṟic cila varikaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷār.
aruṭṭiru vipulānanta aṭikaḷ yāḻ nūlil pattarum vaḷinta kōṭum piṟa uṟuppukaḷum 14 narampukaḷum uṭiya cakōṭa yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ pala paṭam pōṭṭuk kāṭṭi cakōṭayāḻiṉ ilakkaṇattiyum ati amikkum muṟiyiyum icikkum vaḻikaḷiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭiyatōṭu iti marattāṟ ceytu itiyum iṉṉum mūṉṟu yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷiyum ceytu tirukkoḷḷamputūrt tirukkōyilil āḷuṭiya piḷḷiyār tiruuruviṉmuṉ kūṭiya pulavar pēraviyil 5-6-47il yāḻ nūli araṅkēṟṟumpoḻutu kāṭcikkum vittuk kāṭṭiṉār.
intac cakōṭa yāḻic camippataṟku avar enta ōviyatti ṉiṉṟu cāṉṟu kaṇṭār eṉṟu eṉakkut teriyavilli.  ituvum avaratu kaṟpaṉi yāḻ eṉṟē avaratu nūliṉiṉṟu nāṉ uṇar kiṟēṉ.  avar kaṇṭa cakōṭa yāḻ uruvattiyum innūlil paṭa māka varintu eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷēṉ.
āntira nāṭṭiluḷḷa amarāvati arukil uḷḷa kōli eṉṉum iṭattil cila ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap peṟṟiruntaṉa.  avi iṉṟum ilaṇṭaṉ tolporuḷ kāṭcicāliyilum uḷḷaṉa.1  avi ki. mu.  iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil tīṭṭappeṟ ṟuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  ivikaḷil cila yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivi tirāviṭa ciṟpak karuvūlamāka   aṟiñar kaḷāl pōṟṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivikaḷil yāḻ vācikkum aṇaṅku kaḷuṭiya aḻakiya ciṟpaṅkaḷ pala uḷa.  ivaṟṟi aṉittulakak kali aṟiñar āṉanta kumāracuvāmi avarkaḷ kaṇṭu āyntār.  cilappatikāra kālattilum ataṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum tamiḻ nāṭṭilē paḻamiyāṉa yāḻkaḷ vaḻaṅkivantaṉa.  paḻamiyāṉa ici marapu nilavi vantatu.  ati meyppippatupōl amarāvati kōlic ciṟpam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu mutaṉmutalāka ulakiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār aṟiñar āṉantakumāra cuvāmi.
aruṭṭiru vipulānanta aṭikaḷ yāḻ nūli yāttataṟku muṉpē, kali mēti āṉanta kumāracuvāmi avarkaḷ amarāvati yāḻc ciṟpattik kaṇṭu āyntu atip paṟṟi ariya oru kaṭṭuriyit tīṭṭiyuḷḷār.  vipulānanta aṭikaḷ atik kaṇṭu viyantu yāḻ nūliṟ pārāṭṭi eḻutiyuḷḷār, inta nūṟṟāṇṭil.  eṉavē yāḻ ārāyccikku mutaṉmutalāka aṭippaṭiyiṭṭavar tiru. āṉanta kumāracuvāmi avarkaḷēyākum.  niṟka, amarāvati ciṟpattil kāṇappaṭum oru yāḻ cakōṭa yāḻ.  atil tivavu, valittal, melittal, ceyyum māṭakam (muṟukkāṇi) pattarilē amikkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum.  yāḻ mīṭṭum peṇ uruvam pattari maṟittuk koṇṭirukkiṟatu.  māṭakam kāṇappaṭa villi.  kōṭum narampum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟiñar vipulā nantar kūṟiyuḷḷār.  mēlum avar tam nūlil oru varipaṭamum veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār.  āṉāl nāṉ ati acal uruvattiyē paṭam piṭittu innūlil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷēṉ.
intac ciṟpa uruvil yāḻ icikkum maṅkiyiṉ uruvatti yum yāḻ uruvattiyum oppiṭṭu ārāyntāl yāḻiṉ uruvam 42 aṅkulamum (3½ aṭiyum) kōṭṭiṉ uruvamum elip eṉṉum āyta vaṭṭa vaṭivākavum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  āyta vaṭṭattiṟku iru miyaṅkaḷ uḷa (itaṉūṭākac cellum nīṇṭa viṭṭam 42 aṅkulam eṉa mēlē kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.) inta vaṭṭatti liruntu nēr kōṇamākac cellum kuṟukku viṭṭam 28 aṅkula nīḷam uḷḷatu.  inta aṭippaṭiyil āyta vaṭṭam varintāl yāḻkkōṭu amintuviṭum.  ciṟpa uruvil kāṇappaṭāta pattarum āyta vaṭṭattil ellippaṭutti niṟkac ceyyalām.  kuṟukku viṭṭattiṟku 30 pāki cāyvākiya nērkōṭu pattariṉ naṭuvē pōrvit tōl mītu pollam pottappaṭṭa iṭam eṉak koḷḷamuṭiyum.  narampu kaṭṭiyirukkum tōṟṟattiyum piṟavaṟṟi yum paṭattiṟ pārkkaveṉṟu yāḻnūlil vipulānanta aṭikaḷ viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
āṉāl inta amarāvati ciṟpattil taliyuṭintu pōyirukkum peṇ oru kiyāl yāḻi vayiṟṟōṭu taḻuvi ataṉ aṭippakutiyi iṭukkik koṇṭum maṟṟoru kiyāl ataṉ nuṉip pakutiyip piṭittuk koṇṭiruppatumāṉa nīṇṭa kompuḷḷa yāḻi evvāṟu cakōṭa yāḻ eṉṉa kuṟippiṭṭār eṉṟu eṉṉāl yūkikka muṭiyavilli.  ivar tam yāḻ nūlil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa cakōṭa yāḻ vaḷinta kōṭukaḷiyuṭiyatu.  ataṟkum amarāvatic ciṟpattiṟkum immi aḷavum oppuvami kūṟamuṭiyātu.  ātiyil vaḷintirunta cakōṭa yāḻiṉ kōṭu nimirkkappeṟṟu amarāvati ciṟpattil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiyiruntāl oru ciṟitu oṟṟumi irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟalām.
aṭikaḷāriṉ intak karuttu, eṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷiṉ uḷḷak karutti kalakkiviṭukiṟatu eṉṟu iṅkuc collit tīravēṇṭiyava ṉāka irukkiṟēṉ.  ātiyil cakōṭa yāḻ vaḷinta kōṭukaḷip peṟṟiruntatu.  appāl kōṭu nimirkkappeṟṟatu.  yāḻ nūlil kuṟippiṭuvatu kōṭu nimirkkappeṟāta cakōṭa yāḻ.  amarāvatic ciṟpattil kāṇappaṭuvatu kōṭu nimirkkappeṟṟa piṟkālac cakōṭa yāḻ eṉṟu vipulānanta aṭikaḷ kuṟippiṭṭiruntāl teḷivāka irukkum.
putiya oḷi
iruḷil eṉṉa ceyvatu eṉṟu tayaṅki niṉṟa oruvarkku tiṭīreṉṟu oru putiya oḷi tōṉṟiyatu pōl, mutalāvatu yāḻ ciṟpa uruvam oṉṟu kiṭittatu.  atu tañci māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa tiru erukkattam puliyūril uḷḷa tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar tiru uruvac ciṟpam.  antac ciṟpa uruvil yāḻ vaṭivam oṉṟu kāṇap paṭukiṟatu.
inta yāḻ vaṭivam cakōṭa yāḻ vaṭivamēyākum eṉṟu eṉ naṇpar palarum aṟutiyiṭṭu uṟuti kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.  tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar, avar kālattil mīṭṭi vantatum pōṟṟi vantatum cakōṭa yāḻtāṉ eṉpatil eṉakku aiyamuṇṭu.  tirumuṟi varalāṟṟi naṉku ārāynta aṇṇāmalip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻ ārāyccit tuṟi virivuriyāḷarum vittuvāṉumāṉa tiru. ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉ avarkaḷ paṉṉiru tirumuṟi varalāṟu eṉṉum nūlil tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar tantiri yāḻi mīṭṭiya tākak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
tirunīlakaṇṭayāḻppāṇar piḷḷiyārit toḻutu tantiri yāḻiṉi vīkki iciyārāyntu iṟivarkkukanta pāṇi yiṉi ēḻici valla mataṅka cūḷāmaṇiyāruṭaṉ pāṭi, yāvarum viyantu pōṟṟa yāḻiliṭṭu vācittup pōṟṟiṉār.1 eṉpatākum.
tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar cīkāḻippatikku vantu ñāṉa campantarik kaṇṭu vaṇaṅki avar virumpiyavāṟē tamatu kiyi lirunta tantiri yāḻil iṟivaṉukkukanta pāṭalip pāṭiyirukkum poḻutu, avar ciliyil kāṇappaṭuvatu cakōṭa yāḻ eṉa eppaṭik kūṟuvatu eṉṟa aiyam ēṟpaṭṭatu.
āṉāl itē nūlil vēṟoru pakkattil tiru. veḷḷi vāraṇaṉ avarkaḷ,
tiruñāṉa campantap piḷḷiyār kālattavarākiya tiru. nīla kaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar, tam maṉiviyār mataṅka cūḷāmaṇiyāruṭaṉ cīkāḻip patikkuc ceṉṟu āḷuṭiya piḷḷiyāri vaṇaṅki avar pāṭiyaruḷiya tiruppatikaṅkaḷit tam yāḻil icittup piḷḷiyāruṭaṉ tamiḻ nāṭeṅkuñ ceṉṟu, nāḷum iṉṉicit tiṟattāl teyvattip pōṟṟiṉār eṉpatu varalāṟu.  avar vācitta yāḻk karuvi cakōṭa yāḻ eṉṉum peyariyuṭiya teṉpatu...... eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
ivar mikat teḷivāyk kuṟippiṭuvatiṉiṉṟu cakōṭa yāḻukku, tantiri yāḻ eṉṟa peyar oṉṟu irukkumō eṉṟu cantēkappaṭukiṉṟēṉ.  ēṉeṉil yāḻ nūlil aruṭṭiru vipulā nanta aṭikaḷ cakōṭa yāḻukku viḷakkam kūṟumpoḻutu tirunīla kaṇṭa yāḻppāṇa nāyaṉār kiyilirunta itteyvak karuvi muttamiḻ vittakarākiya tiruñāṉacampantap piḷḷiyārālē pārāṭṭappeṟṟatu.  ittakiya ciṟappuvāynta karuviyiṉ ici yiṉi mīṭṭu morumuṟi tōṟṟumāṟu ceytal tamiḻakattiṉ maṟumalaccikkuc ciṟantatō rētuvākum1  eṉṟu kūṟiyatu cakōṭa yāḻ oli mīṇṭum tamiḻakattil olikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu aṭikaḷ aḷapperum āval koṇṭiruntār eṉpatit terivikkiṟatu.
itaṉāl eṉakku ōraḷavu maṉaniṟivu ēṟpaṭṭatu.  tiru nīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar icittatu cakōṭa yāḻāka irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇiṉēṉ.  tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇaric cakōṭa yāḻt talivar eṉac cēkkiḻār aṭikaḷ pōṟṟiyatu oṉṟē tiru. nīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar, piṟanta tiru erukkattampuliyūril uḷḷa avaratu ciliyil kāṇappaṭum yāḻ uruvam cakōṭa yāḻ eṉṉum muṭipiṟku eṉṉi varac ceytatu.  ataṉi viḷakki vipulānantarum kūṟiyirukkiṟār.  āṉāl iṅkē oṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭum.  vipulānanta aṭikaḷ yāḻ nūl iyaṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭa piṟakē, pala tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ, yāḻ, yāḻ eṉṟu, kūvi tamiḻarkaḷ icikkaruvi yāḻē eṉṟum kūṟi, yāḻ paṟṟiya āyvil iṟaṅkat toṭaṅkiṉar eṉpatē maṟikkātu kūṟavēṇṭiya ceytiyākum.  avarukku muṉṉāl?  kuṭantiyic cārnta tārācurattilum tiru nīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar cili uru, cakōṭa yāḻit tāṅki niṟpatu pōl kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  
ivikaḷiyellām ārāyntu kāṇpōrkkup paṇṭiya tamiḻar mutal ūḻiyil uruvākkiya mutuperum yāḻ uruvil cakōṭayāḻ uruvamē takka cāṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu tiṭṭavaṭṭamākat teriya varum.
eṉatu karuttiyum aṟiñar veḷḷivāraṇaṉār karutti yum araṇceyyum muṟiyil taṉittamiḻp perum pulavarum kīḻkalit tērcciyāḷarumāṉa uyartiru. ñā. tēvanēyaṉ, em. ē. avarkaḷ,
narampuk karuvikaḷellām paṇṭik kālattil yāḻ eṉṟē kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.  ippōtuḷḷa vīṇi ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻāka allatu ataṉ tiruttamāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  ceṅkōṭu - nērāṉa taṇṭu.  ceṅkōl eṉpati itaṉoṭu oppunōkkuka.
icit tamiḻ vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉa piṟkālattil kampar mutaliyōr yāḻum vīṇiyum vēṟākak karutiṉar.  yāḻ eṉṉum teṉcoṟkup patilāka vīṇi yeṉṉum vaṭacol vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟateṉka
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.1  pala tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ vīṇi eṉṟa patam tamiḻē eṉṟu kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.  ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻiṉ tanti viṇ eṉṟa oliyi eḻuppiyatāl atu viṇ eṉṟu kūṟappeṟṟu iṟutiyil vīṇi eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu eṉpar.
vīṇi, yāḻ, calam, kiṉṉaram, kōṭāvati, kōṭṭam, taṇṭu, māṭam, vallaki, vipañciyam, tantiri yāḻ eṉṟellām aḻikkap paṭum.
cīṟiyāḻ
cilappatikāram, pēri yāḻ, makara yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉa nālvaki yāḻkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu.  mikap peritāṉa pēri yāḻi mutalil kūṟi mikac ciṟiyatāṉa ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻi iṟutiyil kūṟiyuḷḷatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.  nālvaki yāḻkaḷil ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ kōṭu cenniṟamāka iruntatāl itaṟkuc ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭu irukkalām eṉṟum eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
pēri yāḻukku irupattoru narampukaḷum makara yāḻukku pattoṉpatu narampukaḷum cakōṭa yāḻukkup patiṉāṉku narampu kaḷum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻukku ēḻu narampukaḷum kūṟuvatiliruntē, ēṉiya yāḻkaḷiviṭa ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ ciṟuyāḻ eṉpatu naṉku pulappaṭum.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ ciṟiya yāḻāka iruppatāl ciṟuyāḻ eṉṟum cīṟiyāḻ eṉṟum aḻittu vantaṉar.
iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ilakkiyac ciṟappu mikka cilappatikārattiṉ puṟañcēriyiṟutta kātiyil,
cenniṟam purinta ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻil	(cilap. puṟam 106)
eṉṟum, naṭukaṟkātiyil
vaṇarkōṭṭuc cīṟiyāḻ vāṅkupu taḻīi	(cilap. naṭukaṟ - 31)
eṉṟum, puṟanāṉūṟṟil,
kivaḻi maruṅkiṟ cevvaḻi paṇṇi	(puṟam-149)
eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  kiyakattu eppoḻutum irukkat takkatākiyāl kivaḻi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭatu.  pāṇarkaḷ kiyil eṭuttukkoṇṭu ūrūrāyc celvataṟku ēṟṟatāy eḷi tāy iruntatālum pāṇarkaḷ kiyil eppoḻutum irunta tālum kivaḻi eṉṟu peyar peṟṟatu.  iccīṟiyāḻ puṟanāṉūṟṟil
vāṅkiru maruppiṟ ṟīntoṭic cīṟiyāḻ	(puṟam-285)
eṉṟum,
miṉṉēr pacci miñiṟṟukkuraṟ cīṟiyāḻ 	(puṟam-308)
eṉṟum,
kival cīṟiyāḻ kaṭaṉaṟin tiyakka	(puṟam-398)
eṉṟum caṅkakkālap pulavarkaḷ palarum cīṟiyāḻip paṟpala iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  itupōl piṟa yāḻkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi avarkaḷ kuṟippiṭavilli.  itaṉāl cīṟiyāḻ mutaṟ caṅkak kālam mutal aḻiyātu ellōrālum pōṟṟappeṟṟu varuvatum kavaṉikkattakkatu.  itu aḻiyāmalum ēṟṟam peṟṟum iruppataṟku itaṉ ciṟiya uruvamē mukkiyak kāraṇamākum.
inta yāḻāṉatu muṟkālattil pāṇarkaḷ palarālum pōṟṟappeṟṟu vantatu.  inta yāḻē mātavi taṉ pañciṉum melliya pāl pōṉṟa aḻakiya karaṅkaḷil ēnti vācikkappeṟṟa aḻakiya yāḻ.  ituvē civaṉārkku ukantatu eṉṟum irāvaṇaṉ icittatu eṉṟum kūṟappeṟukiṟatu.  āṉāl itaṟku nammāl pōtiya cāṉṟu kāṇamuṭiyavilli.
aruḷtiru. vipulānanta aṭikaḷ cīṟiyāḻum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻum orē karuvi eṉpati ēṟṟukkoḷḷavilli.  mikat tiṭṭa vaṭṭamāy ivviraṇṭum iru vēṟu karuvikaḷ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟārkaḷ.  āṉāl tiru. ā. a. varakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷ mikat teḷivākac cīṟi yāḻum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻum orē karuvi eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
atōṭu, tiru. vipulānantar cīṟiyāḻukkum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻukkum iruvēṟu uruvappaṭaṅkaḷ kārikkuṭi kumaraṉ āṇṭu malaril tantuḷḷār.  āṉāl inta iraṇṭu uruvap paṭaṅ kaḷiyum avar etiṉiṉṟu kaṇṭār eṉṟu eṅkum kuṟippiṭa villi.  avar kuṟippiṭum cīṟi yāḻ uruvatti ā. a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ enta ātārattik kaṇṭu ati ēṟṟukkoṇṭiruk kiṟār?  eṉṟum emmāl aṟiyamuṭiyavilli.  ilakkiyap paṭippi vittukkoṇṭu tam kaṟpaṉiyil tiru. vipulānantar kaṇṭa uruvattiyē tiru. varakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉum ēṟṟukkoṇ ṭirukkiṟār eṉṟē karutukiṉṟēṉ.
aruḷā yātalō koṭitē yiruḷvarac
cīṟiyāḻ cevvaḻi paṇṇi yāḻaniṉ
kāretir kāṉam pāṭiṉē māka	(puṟam-144)
eṉṟa pā āyviṟkuriyatu.
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ pattar, kōṭu, tivavu, oṟṟu, tantirikaram, narampu eṉṉum aṟuvaki uṟuppukaḷiyuṭiyatu.  cīṟi yāḻil uḷḷa pōrvit tōl ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻil illi.  eṉavē ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻil aminta pattariṉ parappu vaṟuvāy ākkap peṟṟu pōrvit tōlāl mūṭappeṟātu vīṇiyiṉ kuṭam pōṉṟu iruttalvēṇṭum.  cīṟi yāḻil illāta oṟṟu tantirikaram ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻil uḷa.  cīṟi yāḻ vaḷinta kōṭuḷḷatu.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ vaḷiviṉṟi nimirntirukkum.  itaṉāl itu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉum peyarip peṟṟatu eṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar.  itaṉ oṟṟuṟuppu ici narampukaḷiṉ kīḻ, nel kaṉam iṭiveḷiyuḷḷatu.  itaṉ mēl narampiṉi aḻuttip piṭittu nīḷattip pātiyākki melivu narampilē uccameṉappaṭum valivu tōṉṟavum ceyvar.  itaṉi viḷakki, tiru. veḷḷivāraṇaṉ avarkaḷ kalikkaḷañciyattil teḷivākak kūṟiyuḷḷār.1  ellām vipulānantar yāḻ nūlip paṭitta aḷavil kūṟiyaviyē eṉpatil eṭṭuṇiyum aiyamiṉṟu.
makara yāḻ
caṅkak kāla yāḻkaḷ nāṉkiṉuḷ makara yāḻum oṉṟu.  inta yāḻ, mutaṟ caṅkak kālantoṭṭu eṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari tamiḻakattil iruntu vantatu.  inta nūl vaki yāḻkaḷukkum ellā uṟuppukaḷum kiṭṭattaṭṭa oruppōla iruntaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇap paṭukiṟatu.  āṉāl inta nālvaki yāḻum vevvēṟu eṇkaḷ koṇṭa narampukaḷiyuṭiyaṉavāy iruntaṉa.
kōṭṭiṉa tamitiyuṅ koḻuviya vāṇiyum
māṭṭiya pattariṉ vakiyu māṭakamun
tantiri yamitiyuñ cāṟṟiya piṟavum
muntiya nūliṉ muṭinta vakiyē
eṉṟa cilappatikāra uriyāciriyar kūṟṟāl ēṉiya yāḻkaḷiṉ uṟuppiṉip pōlavē makara yāḻiṉ uṟuppukaḷum atāvatu pōrvi tiṇ kōl, tivavu, āṇi, pattar, maruppu, kōṭu, māṭakam, narampu mutaliya uṟuppukaḷum uṇṭu eṉṟu aṟiñar kaḷāl kūṟappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.  makarayāḻiṉ vāṉkōṭu taḻīi - (maṇi 4: paḷik. vari 3-6) eṉṟu maṇimēkali kūṟukiṟatu.  itaṉāl makarayāḻ maṇimēkali āciriyar kālam vari tamiḻakattil iruntu vantatu eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu.
makara yāḻ 19 narampukaḷiyuṭiyatu eṉṟum 17 narampukaḷi yuṭiyatu eṉṟum collappaṭukiṟatu.  inta yāḻiṉ uṟuppukaḷ aṉittum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ uṟuppukaḷi ottaṉa eṉṟu cilar karutukiṉṟārkaḷ.
