makara yāḻkaḷik kuyiluvōr iṭattōṭpakkam aṇittuk koṇṭu kuyilumpoḻutu itaṉ tivavukaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟa kōṭum pattarum varuvāyum makara mīṉiṉ uṟuppukaḷi ottaṉavāy iruppatāl itaṉi makara yāḻ eṉṟaṉar eṉa varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ makara yāḻukkut taṉ kaṟpaṉiyil oru putiya uruvaṅ kaṟpittuḷḷār.
atōṭu niṟkātu, cilar makara maccam eṉpatu mutaliyiṉ taliyiyum nāyiṉ uṭaliyum nāṉku kālkaḷiyum peṟṟa ōr purāṇa kāla vilaṅkeṉṟum, avvilaṅkiṉ tōṟṟattip pōl ivyāḻum iruntateṉṟuṅ kūṟi, ikkaruttiṟkiṇaṅka ōr cittiramum varintuḷḷaṉar.  atiyum iṅku kaṇṭu aṟiñar cīrtūkki aṟintu koḷka eṉṟu pāṇar kivaḻiyil kuṟippiṭṭu aṟiñar vipulāṉantar kaṇṭa yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷukku maṟuppuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
aṟiñar vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ, tamiḻarkaḷ yavaṉa nāṭṭōṭu koṇṭa toṭarpāl, avarkaḷāl ceyyappeṟṟa makara yāḻ eṉṟu kaṟpaṉi ceytukoṇṭu uruvamikkum ōviyak kaliyi lum icikkaliyilum vallunarāyirunta yavaṉar aḻakupeṟac ceyta makara yāḻāṉatu avar nāṭṭu makaramīṉ uruvattatāka iruntateṉak koḷḷalām eṉṟu tam yāḻ nūlil kūṟiyuḷḷār.
avar etu kūṟiṉālum kūṟaṭṭum; makara yāḻukku avar kaṇṭa uruvam tamiḻakattilō allatu piṟanāṭṭilō eṅkum kāṇap paṭṭatākak teriyavilli.  yavaṉa nāṭṭilum attakiya uruvam kāṇappaṭavilli.  airōppāvil uḷḷa enta nāṭṭiṉarō kaṇṭa makara uruvatti maṉattiṟkoṇṭu kaṟpaṉiyil iru makara yāḻ uruvatti vipulānantar kaṇṭu tam yāḻ nūlil tantuḷḷār.  ivviru uruvaṅkaḷum paṇṭiya tamiḻar kaṇṭa makara yāḻ uruvam eṉṟu eṉṉāl ēṟṟukkoḷḷa muṭiyavilli.
tiru. vipulānantar kaṇṭa makara yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷum aṟiñar ā. a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ varinta makara uruvaṅkaḷum avar kaḷiṉ contak kaṟpaṉi uruvaṅkaḷēyākum.  ataṟku evvita ciṟpac cāṉṟukaḷō ōviyac cāṉṟukaḷō illi.
intiyā muḻuvatilum, ēṉ? parmā, cayām, cāvakam, kampōcakam pōṉṟa pala nāṭukaḷilumuḷḷa palayāḻ ciṟpap paṭaṅ kaḷik kaṇṭuḷḷēṉ.  avi etilum ivarkaḷ iruvarkaḷiṉ makara yāḻukkum cāṉṟu illi.
nāratap pēriyāḻ
mutaṟcaṅkak kālattilē vil yāḻum pēriyāḻum cīṟiyāḻum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa.  kaṭic caṅkak kālam vari vēṟu yāḻp peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli.  cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali, cīvakacintā maṇi, peruṅkati mutaliya nūlkaḷil cakōṭa yāḻum makara yāḻum ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  cumār 800āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ eḻunta nūlākak karutappaṭum kallāṭattilē pēri yāḻ, tumpuru yāḻ, kīcaka yāḻ maruttuva yāḻ pōṉṟa cila yāḻkaḷiṉ peyar vakikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  300āṇṭukaḷukkuḷ eḻunta pala nāṭaka nūlkaḷil varāḷi yāḻ, vallaki yāḻ, muḷari yāḻ, makati yāḻ, pirakati yāḻ, tīkkucci kiyāḻ, tumpuru yāḻ, kaḷāvati yāḻ, kaccapi yāḻ, kiṉṉari yāḻ, irāvaṇācura yāḻ pōṉṟa pala yāḻkaḷ kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ippoḻutu pala yāḻp peyarkaḷum, peyar teriya muṭiyāta pala ciṟpa yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷum kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.
mutal caṅkak kālattil iruntatu peruṅkalam eṉṉum paraviyāḻ, atu 1000-narampukaḷiyuṭiyatu.  itu mutal ūḻiyil aḻintu paṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  appāl 1000-narampukaḷi yuṭiya nāratappēri yāḻ eṉṟoru yāḻ talitūkkiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  itu 32 viral aḷavu akalamum 84 viral aḷavu nīḷamum uṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  kallāṭar kallāṭattil,
vaṭamoḻi vititta vicinūl vaḻakkuṭa
ṉaṭuttaṉa veṇṇāṉ kaṅkuli yakattiṉu
nāṟpatiṟ ṟiraṭṭi nālaṅ kuliyiṉuṅ
kuṟumiyu neṭumiyuṅ kōṭal peṟṟati
yāyiran tantiri niṟipotu vicittuk
kōṭi muṉṟiṟ kuṟittumaṇi kuyiṟṟi
yirunilaṅ kiṭatti maṉaṅkaraṅ katuva
vāyirat teṭṭi lamintaṉa piṟappup
piṟavi pētat tuṟiyatu pōla
vāriyap pataṅko ṇāratap pēriyāḻ
naṉṉarko ḷaṉpā ṉaṉimukam pulampa
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
itaṉ poruḷ vaṭamoḻi nūṟkaḷiṉ vitippaṭi 32 viral aḷavu akalamum 84 viral aḷavu  nīḷamum uṭiyatāy kuṟukalum nīṭṭalumāṉa narampukaḷ 1000-kaṭṭappeṟṟatāy mūṉṟu mūlikaḷi lum irattiṉamaṇikaḷ poṟikkappeṟṟatāy nilamītu kiṭattik kiviralkaḷāṟṟaṭavi 1008-vita ici olikaḷip piṟappikkak kūṭiyatāy uḷḷa icikkaruviyē nāratap pēriyāḻākum. 
ikkaruvi paṇṭiya peruṅkalam pōl 1000 narampukaḷi yuṭiyatāy iruntālum uruvattilum vēṟupaṭṭatāyk karutap paṭukiṟatu.  itaṉit tiru. ā. a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷ, itu mukkōṇa vaṭivāy mūṉṟu mūlikaḷiyuṭiyatāy tarimītu kiṭattappeṟṟu kuyilappaṭuvatāy uḷḷa karuviyeṉa nām aṟiyumpōtu tāḻipōl puṭittup parutta kalattiyum vaḷinta vaṇariyum tivavukaḷākac cuṟṟappeṟṟa nīṇṭa kōṭṭiṉiyum kavittu uyarntu vaḷinta untiyiṉiyum uṭiyatāṉa toṉ ṟamiḻp pēriyāḻiṉ tōṟṟattiṉi ottirātu eṉpataṉi aiyamaṟak kāṇkiṟōm.  toṉṟamiḻp pēriyāḻ nāratap pēriyāḻip pōl nilamītu kiṭattappaṭṭuk kuyilap peṟātu kuyiluvōriṉ vayiṟṟōṭu cērkkappaṭṭum iṭat tōṭpakkam aṇikkap peṟṟum kuyilappaṭum karuvi eṉap paṇṭiya nūlkaḷil kāṇkiṟōm eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.1
atōṭu avar kallāṭac ceyyuḷi ātāramāka vittut tam kaṟpaṉiyāl oru mukkōṇa nāratap pēriyāḻiyum varin tuḷḷār.  atiyum iṅkuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷōm.
inta nāratap pēriyāḻiṉ cariyāṉa uruvam etuvum iṉṟu kiṭikkavilli.  nāratap pēriyāḻum namatu paṇṭiya peruṅ kalatti yottup peritāka ellā makkaḷum payaṉpaṭutta muṭiyā tiruntatāl aḻintu pōyiṟṟu.
nāratap pēriyāḻ mukkōṇa vaṭivāy mūṉṟu mūlikaḷi yuṭiyatu.  itu mantara, mattiya, tāram eṉṉum mūṉṟu nilikaḷil curam paṭippaṭiyāy mēlē cellaccellat tīviramākiṟateṉṟum narampukaḷ kīḻē varavarak kuṟukip pōkiṟateṉṟum aṟiyak kiṭak kiṉṟatu.  mantira niliyil uḷḷa narampukaḷ nīḷamākavum kaṉamākavum irukkum.  atōṭu oliyum mantamāka eḻum.  narampukaḷ nīḷam kuṟintu kaṉamum kuṟintu iruppatāl pōkap pōka oliyum tīviramākiṟatu eṉṟu ici aṟiñarkaḷ eṇṇukiṟārkaḷ.
maruttuva yāḻ
maruttuva yāḻ tiruttam peṟṟatu; cevviya uruvam peṟṟatu; iṉiya olikaḷi eḻuppuvatu.  itu nāratap pēriyāḻiyum kīcaka yāḻiyum viṭac ciṟiyatu.  itaṉi aṟiñarkaḷ cīriya yāḻ eṉṟum teyva yāḻ eṉṟum aḻippar.  itu nalla vaṭṭamāṉa vaṭivuṭi yatāy irukkum.  pattar muyal tōliṉāl mūṭap peṟṟirukkum.  naṭuvē oṟṟi narampu iṭakkiyāṟ kaṭṭi iṭap pakkam mārc caṉiyi 26 viral aḷavuḷḷatāy pattariṉ mēl vittuk koṇṭu valakkiyiṉ mūṉṟu puṟa viralkaḷālum, nuṉi viralālum varuṭi iyakkappaṭum.  itaṉkaṇ tūṅkal ōci, tuḷḷal ōci eṉṉum ici vikaṟpaṅkaḷit taḻuvi 62 vaki ici tōṉṟum.  itu 80 viral nīḷam uḷḷatu.  itaṉik kallāṭaṉār tam ariya nūlākiya kallāṭattil,
niṟimati vaṭṭattu muyaluri vicittu
nāppa ṇoṟṟi narampu kaṭippamit
tannaram pirupat tāṟaṅ kulipeṟa
viṭakkaran tuvakki yiṭakkī ḻamittup
puṟaviraṉ mūṉṟi ṉuṉivira lakattu
maṟupat tiraṇṭici yaṉittuyir vaṇaṅku
maruttuvap peyarpeṟum vāṉak karuvi
tūṅkalun tuḷḷalun tulakki niṉṟicippa
nāṉmukaṉ mutalā mūvarum pōṟṟa
muṉivarañ caliyuṭaṉ mukama ṉiyampat
tēvarka ḷaṉivarun ticitici yiṟiñca
eṉa, mika aḻakāka maruttuva yāḻiṉ māṇpi aḻuttam tiruttamāka eḻutiyuḷḷār.
1008 narampukaḷ uḷḷa nāratap pēri yāḻum 100 narampukaḷuḷḷa kīcaka yāḻum icippataṟkum eṭuttuc celvataṟkum ēṟṟaṉavāka illātiruppati eṇṇip piṟkālattē ciṟiya yāḻāka maruttuva yāḻ amikkap peṟṟatu pōlum.
makkaḷiṭam puttuṇarcciyum putu vāḻvum eḻut toṭaṅkiya kālattil putiya yāḻum eḻuntatu.  iṟivaṉ taṉmiyi iṉṉoli yāl makkaḷukku uṇartta muṉvanta camayac cāṉṟōrkaḷ, kālattiṟ kēṟṟavāṟum vacatikkukantavāṟum icippataṟkut takka vaḻiyilum kuṟinta celavil niṟinta ciṟappuṭiya ciṟu yāḻi uruvākkiṉar, eṉavum kūṟalām.
kīcaka yāḻ
kīcaka yāḻ, pēriyāḻukkup piṉ eḻunta cīriya yāḻ, āṉāl itu pēriyāḻiṉ aṭippaṭiyilē amikkappeṟṟa periya yāḻākum.  yāḻ vakiyil itu peruṅkalam, nāratap pēriyāḻ ākiyavikaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyāy amikkappeṟṟa periya yāḻākum.  itu 80 viral nīḷamum kaṉamāṉa uṭal amippum uṭiyatu.  nāratap pēriyāḻiṉ aṭiyoṟṟi 100 narampukaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟatu itaṉuṭiya periya uṭal amippē itu kāla veḷḷattil nīnti vara muṭiyāmal kāṭēṟak kāraṇam eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  itaṉik kallāṭar,

veḻuveṉa vuṭampupeṟ ṟeṇpataṅ kuliyiṉ
ṟantiri nūṟu taḻaṅkiya mukatta
kīcakap pēriyāḻ kiḷiyuṭaṉ murala
eṉat tam cīriya nūlil teḷivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
tumpuru yāḻ
tumpuru yāḻ, nāratap pēriyāḻ, kīcaka yāḻ ākiyavikaḷi viṭac ciṟiyatu; aḻakiyatu; icippataṟku ēṟṟatu.  eṭuttuc celvataṟku eḷitāṉatu.  itu 12-viral cuṟṟaḷavum 94-viral nīḷamum uṭiyatu.  9-narampukaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟatu.  tōḷilum, kālilum tāṅka vittu icikkappaṭum.  itaṉi,
munnāṉ kaṅkuli muḻuvuṭaṟ cuṟṟu
mimpatiṟ ṟiraṭṭi yāṟuṭaṉ kaḻitta
vaṅkuli neṭumiyu mamittuṭ ṭūrntē
yoṉpatu tantiri yuṟuttinili nīkki
yaṟuvāyk kāyiraṇ ṭaṇitturi kaṭṭit
tōḷkāl vatintu toḻiṟpaṭat tōṉṟun
tumpuruk karuviyun tuṉṉiniṉ ṟicippa
eṉṟu kallāṭam, tumpuru yāḻiṉ amippi eṭuttuk kāṭṭu kiṟatu.  mēlum uriyāciriyar itaṟku oṉpatu narampukaḷ kaṭṭap peṟṟirukkum eṉṟum avaṟṟuḷ curuti narampu taṉittiruppa ēṉiya eṭṭu narampukaḷum iraṇṭiraṇṭāka iṇittuk kaṭṭap paṭṭirukkum eṉṟum itaṉi iyakkuṅkāl patumācāṉat tiliruntu toṭiyilum tōḷilum porunta vittu iyakkap peṟum eṉṟum viḷakkam tantuḷḷār.  ivviḷakkam mikavum ciṟappuṭiyatākum.

9. yāḻiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcci
tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ ici aṟiviṟkēṟpa yāḻum vaḷarcci peṟṟu vantatu.  paṇṭiya tamiḻarkaṇṭa paṇkaḷum yāḻkaḷum viṇ muṭṭa vaḷarntu uyarntaṉa.  paṇ kēḷvi eṉpavi ici, pāli (mūrccaṉi) eṉum iraṇṭiṉ cērkkiyil pallāyiramākap perukiṉa.  ātiyil oru narampōṭu uruppeṟṟa vilyāḻ paṭippaṭi yāy 5 narampukaḷiyuṭiya vilyāḻāka arumpip piṉ 7 narampu kaḷiyuṭiya viṟkōṭṭiyāḻāka malarntatu.  nāḷaṭivil yāḻ vakikaḷ perukiṉa.  narampukaḷiṉ eṇkaḷ atikappaṭṭaṉa.  uruvum tiruvam māṟivantaṉa.  oru narampuṭiya ātivil yāḻ iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, aintu, ēḻu, oṉpatu, patiṉāṉku, patiṉāṟu, irupattoṉṟu, nūṟu, āyiram eṉa eṇṇaṟṟa narampukaḷip peṟṟatu.  palvēṟu peyarkaḷiyum peṟṟu uyarntu peruki vaḷarntatu.
muṭattāmak kaṇṇiyār, peruṅkuṉṟūrk kiḻār, peruṅ kavucikaṉār, nallantuvaṉār, tiruvaḷḷuvar, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ pōṉṟa pēraṟiñarkaḷ ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkum muṟpaṭṭa varkaḷ.  ivarkaḷ kālattiṟku muṉpē yāḻ vevvēṟu peyarkaḷil narampukaḷiṉ eṇkaḷil māṟṟam peṟṟu viḷaṅkiyatu.  āṉāl ataṉ potu amippil atika māṟṟam etuvum eḻavilli.  atu vaḷintu aḻakaṟṟatāy irukkiṟatu eṉṟu eṇṇappeṟṟālum ati evarum periya māṟṟam ceyyamuṉvaravilli.  kāraṇam karuviyi viṭa atil eḻuppappaṭum ici muṟiyē avarkaḷukku mutaṉmiyāka iruntatu eṉalām.
civaṉeṟi pēṇiya appar perumāṉ kālattilō allatu avaratu kālattiṟkuc caṟṟu muṉṉarō tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ mikat tuṇivāka muṉvantu yāḻ uruvattilum ataṉ amippilum perum māṟṟam ceytuḷḷaṉar eṉavum kūṟalām.
patiṉmūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉ yāḻiṟku vēṟu peyarkaḷ etuvum taḻittatākat teriyavilli.  appāl yāḻiṟkum vīṇi eṉṟa peyar vaḻaṅkappeṟṟatu.  yāḻi vīṇi eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭum kālattil atāvatu ārampak kālattil yāḻ uruvattil māṟṟam peṟavilli; ātalāl akkaruviyil vaḷinta kōṭukaḷ māṟṟappeṟavilli.  āṉāl peyaraḷavil oru māṟṟam eḻuntatu pōlum.
aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa cīvaka cintāmaṇiyip paṭitta pulavar tiruttakka tēvar; ivaratu kālam ki. pi. 8m nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟuvar.  ivaratu cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil,
viruntāka yāḻ paṇṇi vīṇitāṉ tōṟpāṉ	(cin. 730)
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.  itaṟku uriyāciriyar mutalil iṉimi peṟa yāḻi icippittup piṉpu taṉ miṭaṟṟāṉum yāḻālum pāṭiṉāḷ eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
akkālattil vīṇi eṉṟa iṉṟiya icikkaruvi arumpa villi.  āṉāl yāḻum miṭaṟum uṭaṉnikaḻnta ici vīṇi eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭṭirukkalām eṉa eṇṇa iṭantarukiṟatu.  mēlum uriyāciriyar nacciṉākkiṉiyār,
vīṇi eṉṟa yāḻiyum pāṭṭiyum	(cin. 730)
eṉṟu teḷivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭuvatāl yāḻum miṭaṟum uṭaṉ nikaḻnta icikkē vīṇi eṉa akkālattil oru peyar urikkap peṟṟatu eṉṟu aiyamaṟat terikiṟatu.  atōṭu avar vīṇi eṉṟa patattiṟkut tāḷattōṭu kaṇṭattilum karuviyiṉum piṟakkum pāṭṭu eṉṟu cintāmaṇiyiṉ 411ām ceyyuḷ uriyuḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
veḷḷili vēṟkaṇāṉic cīvakaṉ vīṉiveṉṟāṉ 					(cin. 741)
eṉṟa cintāmaṇic ceyyuḷukku uriyāciriyar cīvakaṉ tattiyi yāḻum, pāṭṭum veṉṟāṉ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
cīvaka cintāmaṇi eṉum kāppiyam eḻunta kālattil vīṇi eṉpataṟku tāḷan tavaṟātu kaṇṭattālum yāḻālum piṟakkum pāṭṭum eṉṟē poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭatu.  cīvaka cintāmaṇi āciriyar vīṇi eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatu iṉṟuḷḷa vīṇiyāka iruntāl nacciṉārkkiṉiyār tam uriyuḷ ataṉi uṟutiyākak kuṟippiṭṭiruppār.  avar kuṟippiṭātu viṭṭiruntālum in nūli mutalmutalil nūl vaṭivil acciṭṭa tiru. u. cāmināta aiyar ataṉik kuṟippiṭātu viṭṭirukkamāṭṭār.
cilappatikāram eṉṉum kāppiya nūli eḻutiya iḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷ kālam ki. pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭāka irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  akkālattil vaḻakkil iruntuvarum vīṇi karukkuḻiyil uruppeṟuvataṟkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa kālam.  akkālattil vīṇi eṉṟa patam uruppeṟṟu viṭṭatu.  iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum yāḻiṟku vīṇi eṉṟa patatti upayōkittu vantuḷḷār cilappatikārattil,
nāṭakam uruppaci nalkā ḷāki
maṅkalam iḻappa vīṇi maṇmicit
taṅkuka ivaḷeṉac cāpam peṟṟa	(cin. kaṭalōṭu 6:21:23)
eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  itaṟku uriyāciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār vīṇi eṉpataṟku ippōtuḷḷa vīṇiyik kuṟip piṭātu uṭal eṉṟē poruḷ koṇṭuḷḷār.  virivuriyil nārataṉ itaṉi aṟivittal karutit taṉatu yāḻiṟkup paki narampu pāṭiṉaṉ.  ivaṟṟi ellām uṇarnta kuṟu muṉivaṉ nārataṉatu vīṇi maṅkalamiḻappa maṇṇilē maṇiyāyk kiṭakkumāṟum uruppaci puviyilē piṟakkumāṟum cayantaṉ pūmiyil mūṅkilāyt tōṟṟumāṟum capittāṉ eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
nārataṉ vīṇiyik kūṭa yāḻ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.  makkaḷ kurali (kaṇṭatti) cārīra vīṇi, carīra vīṇi, allatu kātra vīṇi eṉṟum piṟkālattil kuṟippiṭu vatik kaṇṭum cintikkalām.
yāḻum, kuḻalum cīṟum miṭarum	(cilap. araṅ 3: 26)
eṉṟac cilappatikārac ceyyuḷukku uriyāciriyar aṭiyārkku nallār miṭaṟu, miṭaṟṟup pāṭal; itaṉic cārīra vīṇi eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  miṭaṟākiya cārīrattiṟku vīṇi eṉṟu ak kālattil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatākavum terikiṟatu aṉṟō!
iḵtaṉṟic cīvakacintāmaṇiyil toṭṭiṉa muṭiya vīṇic ceviccuvi amirtam (cin. 204) eṉṟu varum ceyyuḷ aṭikaḷukku, kāmaṉ pāṭiṉa, oṟṟumiyuṭiya cārīra vīṇiyākiya, cevikku iṉimi tarum amirtatti eṉṟu uri kūṟi miṭaṟṟiṉi vīṇi eṉṟu valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṟatu.
caṅkak kālat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil peritum yāḻ eṉṟa patamē payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu. appāl ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟ ṟāṇṭu mutal vīṇi eṉṟa peyar miṭaṟṟuppāṭalukkum payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. piṉṉar yāḻukkum vīṇi eṉṟa peyar payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭirukkiṟatu eṉat terikiṟatu.
aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟāka viḷaṅkum maṇi mēkaliyil,
takarak kuḻalāl taṉṉōṭu mayaṅki
makarayāḻiṉ vāṉ kōṭu taḻīi	(maṇi. 4.55:56)
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  itil kuṟippiṭṭa makarayāḻ eṉṟa patattiṟku uriyāciriyarkaḷ makara vīṇi eṉṟē poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. iḵtaṉṟi cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil,
tīntoṭi makara vīṇit teḷviḷi
yeṭuppit tēṟṟi	(cin. 608)
eṉṟa ceyyuḷ aṭikaḷil varum makara vīṇikku uriyāciriyarkaḷ makara yāḻ eṉṟē poruḷ kaṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  maṇimēkaliyil maṟṟōriṭattu,
makara vīṇiyiṉ kiḷi narampu vaṭitta 	(maṇi 19:26)
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  itaṟku uriyāciriyar makara yāḻ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār .mēlum  makaravīṇi eṉṟu oru vīṇi eṅkum eppōtum iruntatilli.  makarayāḻē makaravīṇi eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu eṉpatu naṉku terikiṟatu.  koṅku vēḷir iyaṟṟiya peruṅ katiyil pala iṭaṅkaḷil yāḻ kuṟippiṭap paṭukiṟatu.
yāṇar kūṭṭat tiyavaṉak kiviṉi
māṇap puṇarntatōr makara vīṇi	(peruṅ 3: 15: 22-23)
eṉṟa ceyyuḷ aṭikaḷil kuṟippiṭum makara vīṇi eṉṟa patattiṟku uriyāciriyar makarayāḻ eṉṟē uri eḻutiyuḷḷār.
`teyvap pēriyāḻ kivayiṟ ṟirīi	(peruṅ 1: 48: 104)
eṉṟa ceyyuḷ aṭikku tiru. u. vē. cāminātiyar teyvap pēriyāḻ - kōṭavati; iḵtu utayaṇaṇuṭiya vīṇiyiṉ peyar eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār (peruṅ. pak 251).  maṟṟōriṭattil tīn toṭip pēriyāḻ tivavoṭu koḻīi (peruṅ. 1-52: 85) eṉṟa aṭikkup pēriyāḻ eṉpatu iṉiya narampiṉiyuṭiya kōṭavati yeṉṉum periya yāḻ; eḻuvāy; tīn toṭi makara vīṇi `(civa. 608) eṉṟu eḻutiyuḷḷār.
peruṅ kati vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu koṅku vēḷir eṉṉum kuṟunila maṉṉarāl tamiḻākkam ceyyappeṟṟatu.  innūl irāyāmaṇam pōl vaṭa moḻiyiṉiṉṟu moḻiyākkam ceyyap peṟṟatu eṉṟālum kampa irāmāyaṇam pōl ciṟappuṭiyatu.  kālattāl mikap piṟpaṭṭa nūl eṉṟu karutappaṭṭālum cintā maṇi, cilaptikāram, maṇimēkali pōṉṟa peruṅ kāppiyaṅ kaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyāy vittu aṟiñarkaḷāl pōṟṟappaṭuvatu.
innūliṉ mutaṉūlāka viḷaṅkiya pirukatcamkiti eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi nūlil kāṇappaṭum vīṇiyit tamiḻākkam ceyta koṅkuvēḷir eṉṉum tamiḻ aṟiñar, vīṇi eṉṟu  kuṟippiṭātu, yāḻ eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  itu, yāḻ iṭikkālattiltāṉ vīṇi eṉṉum peyarip peṟṟiruntatu; ātiyil yāḻiṟku vīṇi eṉṟa peyar iruntatu eṉpatiyum niṉippūṭṭuvatāka irukkiṟatu.  innūl, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, aṭiyārkku nallār, mayili nātar, nēmināta uriyāciriyar, yāpperuṅkala virutti yuriyāciriyar, vīracōḻiya uriyāciriyar, takkayākap paraṇi uriyāciriyar, ilakkaṇa viḷakka uriyāciriyar mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ urikaḷil innūl eṭuttāḷap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  itaṉ perumiyi yum itaṉ kālattiyum aṟiyat tuṇi ceyyum.
teyvan taḻuviya cīriya nalyāḻ mutaṟ caṅkak kālattiṟku muṉpē makkaḷ kuṟiñci nilattil vēṭṭuva vāḻkki naṭattiya tol paḻaṅkālattilē tōṉṟiyatu.  atu ātiyil vil yāḻāka arumpiyatu eṉpatē ataṉ toṉmiyi uṇarttap pōtiya cāṉṟāka miḷirkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟalām.
vilyāḻ, viṟkōṭṭiyāḻākavum, pēriyāḻ ākavum, cakōṭa yāḻākavum, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻākavum makara yāḻākavum vaḷarcci peṟṟatu eṉṟālum atu, ki. pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari ataṉ kōṭu māṟṟam peṟavilli.  atu oru narampuṭaṉ tōṉṟi āyiram narampukaḷ uṭiyatāka vaḷarntatu.  atu vil vaṭivamāka ātiyil arumpip piṉ pattariyum vaḷinta kōṭṭiyum peṟṟatu.  appāl atu makara mīṉiṉ uruvattiyum peṟṟatu eṉṟālum atu tamiḻ ici peṟṟa vaḷarccikkoppa ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkavilli.  atu miḷiṟṟum paṇ tēṉākat tittikkum ceṅkarumpāy amutāy viḷaṅkiṉālum ataṉ vaṭivam aḻakuṭiyatāy illi.  itaṉi maṉattiṟ koṇṭa vaḷḷuvar,
kaṇikoṭitu yāḻkōṭu cevvitu āṅkuaṉṉa
viṉippaṭu pālāl koḷal.	(tiruk. 279)
eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.  itaṉ poruḷ kaṇi kaviṉuṟak kāṇappaṭṭā lum ceyalāṟ koṭiyatu.  yāḻ kōṭāl vaḷintu aḻakaṟṟa tāyk kāṇappaṭiṉum ceyalāl cevviyatu.  avvāṟē tavam cey vōriyum koṭiyar cevviyar eṉpatu vaṭivāl koḷḷātu avar ceyalpaṭṭak kūṟṟāṉē aṟintu koḷka eṉpatākum.  itaṉi muṉ attiyāyattilum eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷōm.
tamiḻ ici ātikālattil kuḻantiyiṉ maḻali moḻi pōl tōṉṟip piṉ ilakkaṇa ilakkiyac ceṟivu  mikkatāy kalliyum urukkum āṟṟal vāyntatāy iṟivaṉ kōpattiyum taṇikkum taṉmi vāyntatāy ēṉ?  iṟivaṉiṉ vaṭivāy iṉimiyāy eḷitāy, amutāy, cuviyāy, cuviyiṉ payaṉāyp polintatu.  ataṟkēṟpa yāḻ vaḷarcci peṟavilli pōlum.
yāḻ vaki
ātiyil vil yāḻtāṉ tōṉṟiyatu.  appāl peruṅkalam eṉṉum ātiyāḻ tōṉṟiyatu.  itu mutaṟcaṅkak kālattil eḻuntu mutal ūḻiyilē maṟintu pōyiṟṟu.
kaṭic caṅka kālattilē pēriyāḻ, makara yāḻ, cakōṭa yāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ pōṉṟa yāḻkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.  inta yāḻkaḷ cilappatikārattil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  itaṉic cilappatikāra araṅkēṟṟuk kātiyiṉ uri meyppikkiṟatu.  inta yāḻkaḷ nāṉkum nāṉku vitamāka ēṟṟattāḻvuḷḷa eṇkaḷuṭiya narampu kaḷiyuṭiyaṉavāy iruntaṉa.
piṟkālattil pala yāḻ vakikaḷ tamiḻakattil iruntaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ kallāṭaṉār, nārata yāḻ, kīcaka yāḻ, tumpuru yāḻ, maruttuva yāḻ, makara yāḻ, kaccapi yāḻ mutaliyaṉa iruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.  19ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil 
tiru. kaṅkimuttup piḷḷi avarkaḷ tīkkucciki yāḻ, varāḷi yāḻ, vallaki yāḻ, vilyāḻ mutaliya cila putiya yāḻ vakikaḷiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
yāḻiṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kaṇṭa mūtaṟiñarkaḷ yāḻiṉ toḻil kaḷi viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:-
(1) paṇṇal (2) parivaṭṭaṇi (3) ārāytal (4) kivaral 
(5) celavu (6) viḷiyāṭṭu (7) kiyūḻ (8) kuṟum pōkku.
ivikaḷi viḷakki aṟiñarkaḷ tam nūṟkaḷil naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
(1)	paṇṇal - pāṭa niṉitta paṇṇukku iṇi, kiḷi, paki, naṭpu narampukaḷ peyarum taṉmi, māttiri aṟintu vīkkutal.
(2)	parivaṭṭaṇi - avvāṟu vīkkiṉa narampi aka viralālum karaṇañ ceytu taṭavip pārttal.
(3)	ārāytal - arōkaṇa avarōkaṇa vakiyāl iciyit terital.
(4)	tivaral - curuti ēṟṟutal
(5)	celavu - āḷattiyilē nirampap pāṭutal
(6)	viḷiyāṭṭu - pāṭa niṉitta vaṇṇattil cantatti viṭuttal
(7)	kiyūḻ - vaṇṇattil ceyta pāṭalkaḷ ellām iṉpa mākap pāṭal
(8)	kuṟumpōkku - kuṭakac celavum tuḷḷaṟ celavum pāṭutal
iḵtaṉṟi, vārttal, vaṭittal, untal, uṟaḻtal, uruṭṭal mutaliyaṉavum uḷa.
vārttal - cuṭṭu viralāl toḻil ceytal
vaṭittal - cuṭṭu viralum peru viralum kūṭṭi narampi akamum puṟamum ārāytal
untal - narampukaḷit teṟittu valiviṟ paṭṭatum meliviṟ paṭṭatum niral paṭṭatum aṟital
uṟaḻtal - oṉṟiṭiyiṭṭum iraṇṭiṭiyiṭṭum narampu kaḷit teṟittal.
uruṭṭal - iṭak kic cuṭṭu viral uruṭṭalum valak kic cuṭṭu viral uruṭṭalum cuṭṭōṭu peruviraṟ kūṭṭi uruṭṭalum iru peru viralum iyintu uṭaṉ uruṭṭalum eṉap pala.
yāḻiṉ irukki oṉpatu vakippaṭum. atāvatu (1) patumam (2) uṟkaṭitam (3) oppaṭi irukki (4) campuṭam (5) cuyamukam 
(6) cuvattikam (7) taṉippuṭam (8) maṇṭilam (9) rakapātam eṉpaṉa.  nāṭaka nūlōr yāḻiṉ irukkiyi aimpatu eṉpar.
yāḻiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷ pala vakippaṭum. aviyāvatu 
(1) cempaki (2) ārppu (3) atirvu (4) kūṭam eṉappaṭum.
(1) cempaki - paṇṇōṭuḷarā
(2) ārppu - ōṅka icittal
(3) atirvu - narampic citaṟa untal
(4) kūṭam - taṉ pakiyākiya āṟa narampiṉ iciyiṟ kuṉṟit taṉṉōci maḻuṅkal.
yāḻiṟkup pala vaḻikaḷiṟ kuṟṟam uṇṭākum.  yāḻiṉ maram nīrilē niṟṟal, aḻukutal, vētal, nila mayakkup pārilē niṟṟal, iṭi vīḻntiruttal, nōy, marappāl paṭal mutaliyavām.
muṟkālattil vāḻnta tamiḻ nāṭṭu ici vallunarkaḷ kuḻaliyum yāḻiyum tuṇiyākak koṇṭē kural mutaliya ēḻicikaḷiyum kuṟṟamaṟa āyntu perum paṇkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ vaḻip piṟakkum tiṟaṅkaḷumākiya ici nuṭpaṅkaḷi iṉimiyuṟa icittuk kāṭṭiṉārkaḷ.  kuḻalum yāḻum paṇṭiya tamiḻ makka ḷukku iṉpan tantaṉa; avarkaḷ cevikku uṇavāy iruntaṉa; avar kaḷukku amutāka viruntaḷittaṉa.  ivikaḷi īṭilāta iṉpan tarum iṉṉicit tiṟaṅkaḷit tōṟṟuvikka valla ciṟappamintaṉa eṉṟu vaḷḷuvar pōṉṟa aṟavōrkaḷum pōṟṟi vantaṉar.
yāḻiṉ maṟumalarcci
paṇṭik kālat tamiḻ makkaḷ uruvākkiya tīntamiḻ yāḻ patiṉāyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷāy ataṉ uruvilum tiruvilum māṟṟamiṉṟi viḷaṅkiyatu.  makkaḷ nākarikam uyarntatum yāḻ uruvamum māṟṟam uṟṟatu.  ici vallunarkaḷ ēḻiciyi yāḻil iṉimiyuṟa icikkavum kamakam mutaliyavikaḷi mīṭṭavum, yāḻ eḻil peṟṟilaṅkavum yāḻil oru perum māṟṟam ceytaṉar.  māṟṟa muṟṟa yāḻiṟkuk kōṭu nimirntu cemmiyuṟṟatāl ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉṟaṉar.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻē piṉ vīṇiyeṉap peyar ēṟṟu maṟumalarcciyuṟṟatu.  yāḻiṉ kōṭu oṉṟutāṉ nimirntatē yoḻiya ataṉ uṟuppu oṉṟum māṟṟam peṟavilli.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ yāḻiṉ maṟumalarcci eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ, yāḻ vaḷarcciyil oru tiruppam eṉṟu icikkaruvikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.
yāḻ, ātiyil villil iruntu piṟantatu.  atu mutalil ari vaṭṭavaṭivil arumpiyatu.  ataṉ uṇmi arappā, mokañca tārō muttirikaḷil naṉku kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  arappā muttiri kaḷil kāṇappaṭum yāḻ uruvam ari vaṭṭa vaṭivamākavē irukkiṟatu.  atuvē namakku 5000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ irunta yāḻ vaṭiviṟku nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka miḷirkiṟatu.  ataṟku aṭuttapaṭiyāka aruṭṭiru vipulānanta aṭikaḷār tam yāḻ nūlil naṉku tīṭṭikkāṭṭiya vil yāḻ vaṭivam ati iṉṉum meyp pippatāka irukkiṟatu.  ivikaḷil 7 nāṇkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. (paṭam 2, 3, 4 aip pārkka)
ataṟku aṭuttapaṭiyāṉa māṟṟam mokañcatārō nākarikattil kaṇṭa muttirikaḷil kaṇṭa mukkōṇa vaṭivamāṉa yāḻ uruvamākum.  itu ari vaṭṭamāṉa yāḻ vaṭṭattil eḻunta māṟṟattik kāṭṭuvatāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.  (paṭam 5-aip pārkka). itē mukkōṇam pōṉṟa vaṭivattil uḷḷa yāḻ ekiptiya ciṟpaṅkaḷilum cumēriya ciṟpaṅkaḷilum naṉku kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.  itil 10 nāṇkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  inta nāṇkaḷi oru kutiri taṉ kālāl mīṭṭuvatu pōl ciṟpam tīṭṭappeṟṟirukkiṟatu 
(paṭam 6). ivvariya yāḻ ki. mu. 4000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa ciṟpa uruvil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.  iḵtaṉṟi ki. mu. 3000ām āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa yāḻ uruvam oṉṟu kaltēyā (chaldea) nāṭṭil uḷḷa araca kuṭumpattārkaḷiṉ kallaṟi oṉṟiṉiṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  itu aḻakākap palvēṟu vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  atiluḷḷa erutut tali poṟṟakaṭṭāl potiyap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  inta yāḻ vaṭivam iṉṟum īrāk nāṭṭil uḷḷa tolporuḷ kāṭcicāliyil uḷḷatu.  itu ari vaṭṭavaṭiva yāḻiṉiṉṟu ciṟitu māṟutal peṟṟaḷḷatāyk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  mēlum itu mukkōṇa vaṭivam vāyntatu eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyā viṭṭālum nuṉi mukkōṇa vaṭivamāka amikkap peṟṟu nērāka nimirttivikkap peṟṟa icikkaruvi eṉṟu naṉku terikiṟatu. (paṭam 8, 9 aip pārkka)
mokañcatārōvilum kaltēyāvilum kāṇappaṭṭa yāḻkaḷ iru vēṟu aṭippaṭikaḷik koṇṭaṉavāy amin tirukkiṉṟaṉa.  inta iruvita aṭippaṭikaḷum tamiḻaka yāḻkaḷiṉ mūla amippit taḻuviyaṉavākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  oṉṟu ari vaṭṭamāṉa aṭippaṭiyiliruntu vaḷarcci aṭintatu.  maṟṟoṉṟu mukkōṇa vaṭiva aṭippaṭiyiliruntu vaḷarcci aṭintatu.  mukkōṇa amippi viṭa ari vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉa amippē vaḷarcci peṟṟup paṭukkik kōṭṭu vaṭivamāṉa ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻāka atāvatu iṉṟiya vīṇiyiṉ uruvamāka vaḷarcci peṟṟatu.  kōṭu vaḷintatu yāḻ. kōṭu nimirntatu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉpatu cemmiyāṉa - atāvatu nērāka nimirkkappeṟṟa kōṭṭip peṟṟatu eṉpatē poruḷākum.
ari vaṭṭamāṉa āti vilyāḻ nīṇṭakālattiṟkup piṉ caṟṟu nimirntu, iru nuṉikaḷum aṭiyi viṭa virintu mūṉṟām piṟic cantiraṉ vaṭivamāy 10 mutal 17 eṇkaḷ uḷḷa narampu kaḷip peṟṟum vevvēṟu peyarkaḷit tāṅkiyum nam nāṭṭil nilavivantatu.  inta māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷa vil yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ tamiḻakattil pala iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  amarāvati, cāñci ciṟpaṅkaḷilum kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ekiptu nāṭṭi luḷḷa paḻam perum paṭimaṅkaḷilum ciṟpaṅkaḷilum ōviyaṅkaḷi lum peritum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  cilpaciriyiṉ ciṟpa uruviṉ kikaḷil iṭampeṟṟa yāḻ itē vaṭivilē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  āṉāl ataṉ kōṭukaḷil iru ciṟu kuṭaṅkaḷ miḷirkiṉṟaṉa.  ivi ciṟitu aḻaku peṟa amikkappeṟṟut tamiḻ aṉṉiyiṉ tirukkaraṅkaḷil tikaḻvatu pōl camīpattil oru ōviyam tīṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatu. arivaṭṭam ciṟitu kuṟintum vāy akaṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  tiru. vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ tīṭṭiya pēriyāḻ vaṭivilum amarāvati kōlic ciṟpaṅkaḷilum inta māṟṟaṅkaḷi nām ōraḷavu kāṇa muṭiyum.  inta vaḷarccitāṉ aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ eḻunta ciṟpaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭuvatu.  itaṉi arivaṭṭa vaṭivayāḻ peṟṟa mūṉṟāvatu vaḷarcciyiṉ nili eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟalām.
mēṟkūṟiya arivaṭṭa amippuḷḷa āti yāḻkaḷi viṭa atika māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷatu vipulāṉantar kāṭṭum cakōṭa yāḻē yākum.  cakōṭa yāḻi viṭat teḷivāṉa māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷa putiya yāḻ uruvam oṉṟu camīpattil namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷatu namatu tavappayaṉēyākum.  intap putiya māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷa ariya yāḻ karuvi iṉṟu parmāvil uḷḷatu.  itu intiya nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu parmāviṟku putta neṟiyip parappuvataṟkuc ceṉṟa putta tuṟavikaḷāl koṇṭu cellappeṟṟatākak kūṟappeṟukiṟatu.  parmā nāṭṭil uḷḷa inta yāḻ uruvam iṉṟum mīṭṭappeṟṟu varukiṟatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  iṉṟu ivvulakil kāṇappaṭum orē intiya yāḻ uruvam parmāvil kāṇappaṭum ippaḻamperum yāḻ uruvamēyākum.  itaṉip parmīya makkaḷ cavuṇ eṉṟu aḻik kiṟārkaḷ.  itu ki. pi. iraṇṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkuḷḷāka uruvākkappaṭṭatākak karutappaṭukiṟatu.  itaṉ aṭip pakuti tōṇi pōl tōṉṟukiṟatu.  itu marattāl ceyyappeṟṟu mēlē poṟṟakaṭṭāl mūṭappeṟṟu vira maṇikaḷ patikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  kompu ciṟitu vaḷintu ataṉ nuṉiyil viram, marakatam, māṇikkam pōṉṟa kaṟkaḷ patikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  inta yāḻiṟku 14 narampukaḷ pūṭṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  itu ōr ācaṉattiṉ mītu vikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  itaṟku oru nallapaṭi eṭuttu ceṉṉit tolporuṭkāṭciyil vikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  iṉṟu pala yāḻ ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.  āṉāl marattiṉāl ceyyappeṟṟa paḻiya yāḻ uruvam oṉṟum namakkuk kiṭikkavilli.  inta nūṟṟāṇṭil mutaṉmutalāka nām kāṇum oru acal yāḻ uruviṉpaṭi ituvēyākum.  inta yāḻ kōṭu ceṅkuttāy nimirntuḷḷa tirumayam ciṟpattilum kaltēyā vilum kāṇappaṭṭa yāḻkaḷip pōl uḷnōkki vaḷintuḷḷa tāyk kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  ēṉiya iraṇṭu yāḻkaḷum nērākac ceṅkuttāy niṟkum kompukaḷiyuṭiyaṉa (paṭam 1, 18, 19-aip pārkka).
piṟa yāḻkaḷi viṭa tiru. ā. a. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ kāṭṭum nāratappēri yāḻ (1000-puri narampukaḷiyuṭiyatu) teḷivāṉa mukkōṇa amippiyuṭiyatākum.  (paṭam 20).  inta amippil kaltēyā nāṭṭiṉ kallaṟiyil kaṇṭeṭukkappeṟṟa maṟṟoru kāḷikkaṉṟu mukantāṅkiya maṟṟoru yāḻ uruvam kaṇṭu eṭukkappeṟṟuḷḷatu (paṭam 21). inta yāḻ ki. mu. 27, 28ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil eḻuntatāka aṟiñarkaḷāl eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  itaṉ aṭippakutiyil uḷḷa peṭṭi pōṉṟa pakutiyiṉ nuṉiyil tamiḻaka yāḻi aṉucarittu oru kāḷikkaṉṟu tali vaṭivam amikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  itil 11 mutal 15 vari nāṇkaḷ pūṭṭappeṟṟu aḻakiya ariya vēlippāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  itaṉ kīḻē kāṇappaṭum caturamāṉa peṭṭi pōṉṟa vaṭiva amippil iruntu kōṭu caṟṟu vaḷintu piṉ nērāka ceṅkuttāy nimirntu niṟkiṟatu.  inta amippiṉit taḻuvi-atāvatu ṭakara amippil (ṭaṉ pōṉṟu) ekiptilum, cumēriyā nāṭṭilum ōviyattilum ciṟpattilum pala yāḻ vaṭivaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa.  kaltēyā nāṭṭiṉ yāḻ uruvil tamiḻakat taccarkaḷiṉ kivaṇṇam oḷirkiṟatu.  inta yāḻiṉ kompu amippu tirumeyyam eṉṉum ūriluḷḷa ciṟpa uruviṉ amippit taḻuviyatākavē amintuḷḷatu.  aṭippakuti ciṟitu vēṟupaṭṭirukkiṟatu (paṭam 22-24 varip pārkka).
iṟutiyāka, cīṟiyāḻ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum yāḻē nalla māṟṟattip peṟṟatāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.  pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka yāḻkaḷ peṟṟirunta uruvaṅkaḷil oru perum māṟṟam eḻuntatu.  cīṟiyāḻiṉ vaḷivuṟṟirunta kōṭu peritum nimirkkappeṟṟatu.  cīriya yāḻē ciṟitāka, kikku aṭakkamāka eḷitil eṅkum eṭuttuc cellattakkatāy amintatu.  itiyoṭṭip pala vil yāḻkaḷ kūṭa māṟṟam peṟṟeḻuntaṉa.  inta yāḻ mīṭṭuvataṟku eḷitāṉa tāyum pāṇarkaḷ inta yāḻip paṭṭu uṟiyuḷ aṭittu ōriṭattiṉiṉṟu maṟṟōriṭattiṟku eḷitil eṭuttuc celvataṟku ēṟṟatāyum iruntatu (paṭam 25, 26).
cīṟiyāḻiṉ kōṭu nērāka nimirntatāl ataṉ aḻaku uyarntatu; icikkum vacati mikuntatu.   kamakam pōṉṟa kavarccikaramāṉa iṉṉolikaḷi eḻuppa muṭintatu.  itaṉ nalatti aṟinta aṟiñarkaḷ itaṉ kōṭu muḻuvatum nimirttappeṟṟāl, yāḻ nēriyatāyum cīriyatāyum ciṟantatāyum viḷaṅkum eṉṟu eṇṇi kōṭu nimirttiya yāḻi uruvākkiṉar.  inta yāḻ cemmiyāṉa kōṭṭip peṟṟatāl ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṟum poruḷ tarum perum pēṟṟip peṟṟatu.  itu ceṅkōlpōl nērāka nimirttappeṟṟatāl ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṟu kūṟiṉālum poruntum.  (paṭam 27) ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ iṉṟiya tampurā allatu vīṇi vaṭivatti aṭintatu; nām yāḻkaḷiṉ uruvaṅ kaḷi variciyāka kāla amippil varici oḻuṅkupaṭutti vittup pārttāl yāḻiṉ uḷḷatu ciṟattal eṉṟa pariṇāma vaḷarcciyilē iṟutiyākac ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ ataṉ vaṭivil vīṇiyum iṇintu niṟpatu eḷital pulaṉākum.  ceṅkōṭi yāḻ uruvāku muṉṉarē tuntiṉā pōṉṟa oru narampuḷḷa icikkaruvikaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.  itu yāṉ kaṇṭu kūṟum putiya muṭipaṉṟu.  eṉakku muṉṉar pala aṟiñarkaḷ inta muṭipiṟku vantuḷḷārkaḷ.  āṉāl avarkaḷ yāḻkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷi oḻuṅkupaṭutti vittu avikaḷiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyi yellām teḷivāka viḷakki eṭuttukāṭṭi inta muṭipip kūṟavilli eṉa eṇṇukiṟēṉ.  ghisa§ nfh£il, jÄœ¥ bgU«òyt® Mãufh« mUs¥g‹ ã.V., avarkaḷ eṉ vīṭṭil uḷḷa cīṟiyāḻip pārttu, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻukku viḷakkam tarumpoḻutu, mēṟkaṇṭa karuttukkaḷi ātarittup pala cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭiṉārkaḷ.
cakōṭa yāḻkūṭa, kōṭu nimirttappeṟṟāl iṉṟiya vīṇiyiṉ vaṭivattip peṟum eṉṟu iṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷa pala ciṟpac cāṉṟukaḷ uṟutippaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻtāṉ yāḻ vaḷarcciyiṉ iṟutikkaṭṭamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu eṉalām.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉpatu yāḻ amippil oru ciṟiya māṟṟattittāṉ-atāvatu kōṭu nimirttap peṟṟa māṟṟattittāṉ peṟṟatu eṉalām.  iḵtaṉṟi, atu vēṟu oru ciṟu māṟṟattiyum ēṟṟattiyum peṟavilli.  āṉāl eḻil ciṟappip peṟṟatu.  aḵtōṭu kōṭṭiṉ nuṉiyil oru putiya uruvam iṭam peṟṟatu eṉalām.  atāvatu putitākap purāṇa kāla vilaṅku eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum yāḻiyiṉ vaṭivam peṟṟatu.  tamiḻar kaṇṭa narampukkaruviyākiya yāḻil nīṇṭa kālamāka evvitamāṉa paṟavi allatu vilaṅku vaṭivaṅkaḷum iṭam peṟātu iruntu vantatu.  appāl ataṉ toṉmi marapu nili eṉpatu māṟi, makara mīṉ vaṭivam peṟṟatu. tamiḻarkaḷōṭu utiracampantamāṉa toṭar puṭiyavarkaḷākak karutappeṟum kaltēyarkaḷiṉ yāḻkaḷiṉ tamiḻnāṭṭuc civaneṟiyāḷarkaḷukku viruppamāṉa kāḷimukamum kaṉṟumukamum iṭam peṟṟaṉa.  ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ kōṭṭiṉ iṟutiyil yāḻimukam iṭam peṟṟatu.  piṉṉar eḻunta vīṇi kaḷil mayil mukamum kiḷi mukaṅkūṭa iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  camīpattil yāḻi mukavaṭivamum mīṉ vāl uruvamum amintuḷḷa vaḷinta kōṭukaḷ intiya yāḻ vaṭivam oṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷatu. itaṉip pōṉṟa vaṭivamuḷḷa yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ ekiptilum papilōṉilum kiṭippatākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  āṉāl paḻiya yāḻ vaḷarcci peṟṟu putturuvam peṟṟu nīṇṭa kōṭukaḷit tāṅkumpoḻutu kōṭṭiṉ nuṉiyil yāḻiyiṉ uruvattip peṟuvatu poruttamāṉatum ciṟappāṉatumākum.  iḵtaṉṟi yāḻ nimirnta kōṭṭiṉip peṟumpoḻutu atil oru ciṟitum māṟṟam ceyya evarum tuṇiyavilli eṉpatu iṅkuk kuṟippiṭat takkatu.  uṟuppukaḷiṉ peyarkaḷilkūṭa evarum māṟṟañ ceyya tuṇiyavilli.  yāḻiṉ uṟuppukaḷākiya 1. kavikkaṭi, 2. oṟṟu, 
3. unti, 4. nuṇtuḷiyāṇi, 5. pattal, 6. pōrvi, 7. paṇpoḻi narampu, 8. vaṟuvāy, 9. yāppu, 10. kōṭu, 11. tantirikaram, 12. kōl, 
13. tivavu, 14. takippu āṇi, 15. māṭakam, 16. vaṇar, 17. puri narampuk kōl mutaliya uṟuppukaḷ aṉittum kōṭu nimirkkap peṟṟa ceṅkōṭṭu yāḻil appaṭiyēyuḷḷaṉa.  cila uṟuppukaḷ peyar māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.1
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉiṉṟē iṉṟiya vīṇiyum ataṉ pirivukaḷāka iṉṟu malintu kiṭakkum pala tantik karuvikaḷum piṟantaṉa eṉpatu eṉatu uṟutiyāṉa muṭipu.  yāḻiṉ uṟuppukaḷ palavum vīṇiyil iruppatē yāḻiṉiṉṟu vīṇi piṟantatu eṉpati uṟutippaṭuttum.  yāḻiṉ nērāṉa pariṇāma vaḷarcci yilē - uḷḷatu ciṟattal eṉṟa niyatiyil tamiḻakattil, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷāl kāṇappeṟṟa uyariya icikkaruvi vīṇi eṉpatil immiyaḷavum aiyamiṉṟu.  mēlum iṉṟum yāḻiṉ 18 uṟuppukaḷiyum ovvoṉṟākac ceytu poruttiṉāl vīṇi uruppeṟṟuviṭum.
itaṉi emmatippiṟkuriya aṟiñar tiru. āpirakām paṇṭita riṉ arumi makaṉār tiru. varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉ naṉku āyntu pāṇar kivaḻi eṉṉum paṇ ēṭṭil pāṅkuṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭi, em karutti araṇ ceytuḷḷār.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
ēḻu narampukaḷiyum oṉpatu icikaḷukkuriya tavavukaḷip peṟṟa kōṭṭiṉiyum, narampukaḷi valittal melittal ceyyum māṭakattiṉiyum vaṇariyum āṇikaḷiyum, piṟavaṟṟiyum uṭiyatāyirunta nam paṇṭic ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻē taṟkālam nam kiyakattil vīṇi eṉap peyar māṟṟam peṟṟu ulavukiṟatu eṉṟa muṭivāṉa uṇmiyi nām ēṟṟukkoḷvatit tavirttu vēṟu vaḻiyilli eṉa nām aiyamaṟak kāṇkiṉṟōm eṉṟu kuṟip piṭṭuḷḷār.1
yāḻiṉ patiṉeṭṭu uṟuppukaḷil, taṇṭu, cuḷḷāṇi, pōrvi, vaṟuvāy, māṭakam, takippu, unti, vaṇar, pattar eṉṟa oṉpatu uṟuppukaḷum taṇṭu, muḷḷāṇi, mūṭi, palaki, kaṇ māṭam, mēṭu, meṭṭu, vaḷivu, pāḷi allatu kuṭam pōṉṟa 9 uṟuppukaḷum vēṟu tamiḻ peyarkaḷip peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  yāppu, kavikkaṭi, oṟṟu, muṟukkāṇi, puri narampu allatu paṇmoḻi narampu, tantiri karam, pōṉṟaṉa ṭakāri nākapācam, vaḷivurēkku piraṭi, curameṭṭu, tanti, kāṭic cakki pōṉṟa vēṟṟumoḻip peyari iṉṟu tāṅki irukkiṉṟaṉa.  tivavu, purinarampu, (kōlkaḷ) pōrvi, narampu eṉpaṉa muṟiyē meḻuku, veṇkalam, marap palaki, eḵku eṉap poruḷkaḷil māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  taṉit tamiḻp peyarkaḷi ayalavarkaḷ māṟṟit tam moḻip peyari vaḻaṅkiyatāl itu ayalavarkaḷiṉ icikkaruviyāki viṭa muṭiyumā?  murukaṉi, cuppiramaṇiyaṉ eṉṟu vaṭamoḻip peya rāl aḻittāl murukaṉ vaṭavarkaḷiṉ teyvam ākiviṭa muṭiyumā?  atu orupoḻutum ākamuṭiyātu.
paṇṭit tamiḻt teyvaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi vaṭa moḻi yil ākkiyavarkaḷ paṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyum uruvum, piṟappiṭa mum kāṇamuṭiyātavāṟu piṟamoḻip peyari vaḻaṅkiyati āyntāl inta yāḻ uruvil uḷḷa uṟuppukaḷukkup peyar māṟṟam ceytatil viyappu oṉṟum illi.  āṉāl, yāḻiṉ uṟuppukaḷiṉ peyarkaḷil oṉṟu iraṇṭit tavira maṟṟavikaḷi māṟṟātu appaṭiyē vittiruppatu kaṇṭu tamiḻaka ici vallōrkaḷ kaḻipēruvaki aṭikiṉṟārkaḷ.  pattar, vaṟuvāy eṉum iru uṟuppukaḷ ciṟitu māṟṟam peṟṟuḷḷatāl yāḻkkaruvi maṟintuviṭātu, aḻintu viṭātu, vīṇi yāḻukkut toṭar pillāta ayallavarkaḷiṉ icikkaruviyāki viṭamuṭiyātu.
yāḻ muṟkālattil vīṇi eṉṟa peyarip peṟṟiruntatu. yāḻkkulam, vīṇiyip peṟṟapiṉ maṟintu viṭṭatu.  ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku piṉṉarum ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟku muṉṉarum eḻunta iṭikkālattil, yāḻiṉ kōṭu nimirntu vēṟṟuruvam peṟṟa piṉṉarum cila kālam ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉṟum pañcaki yāḻ eṉṟum taṉ paḻampeyari vittuk koṇṭiruntatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷāl kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  atu māṟṟam peṟṟu nīṇṭa kālam ceṉṟa piṉṉarē vīṇi eṉṟa taṉippeyarit tāṅkiyatu.  atu yāḻ eṉṟa peyarit tuṟantu vīṇi eṉṟa peyarip peṟṟu oṉṟiraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil yāḻ eṉṟa tāykkaruviyi amiḻtuṟaḻ teyvat tīñcuvi nalkiya tirukkaruviyi aḻittuviṭṭatu.  atu, naṇṭu  ippi vēykatali nācamuṟuṅkālam, koṇṭa karuvaḻikkum eṉṟa āṉṟōr mutumoḻikkēṟpa vīṇi taṉ tāykkulatti (yāḻi) aḻitta tōṭu ataṉ peyariyum tuṭittuviṭṭatu.
appar kālattil yāḻ eṉṟa icikkaruvikaḷ iruntu vantaṉa.  avar kālattiṟku muṉṉarē yāḻukku vīṇi eṉṟa peyar vaḻaṅkappeṟṟu vantatu.  avar kālattilē yāḻiṉiṉṟu vīṇi eṉṟa icikkaruvi pirintatu.  muṟkālattil kōyilkaḷil yāḻtāṉ iṭam peṟṟiruntatu.  appāl yāḻum vīṇiyum iṭam peṟ ṟiruntaṉa.  iṟutiyāka yāḻ akaṟṟappaṭṭu vīṇiyē anta iṭattic contappaṭuttikkoṇṭatu.  yāḻmurinātar kiyil irunta yāḻum, vīṇātara taṭcaṇāmūrtti kiyil irunta yāḻum akaṟṟappeṟṟu nīṇṭa kompuḷḷa yāḻ eṉṟa vīṇi (taṟkāla vīṇi) iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.  vīṇi eṉpatu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ vaḻivanta oru narampukkaruvi eṉṉum namatu karuttip pala aṟiñarkaḷ ēṟṟukkoṇṭu irukkiṟārkaḷ.  aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakat tamiḻ āyvuttuṟi virivuriyāḷar vittuvāṉ tiru. ka. veḷḷivāraṇaṉ avarkaḷum ēṟṟukkoṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  atu aṭiyil vaṟumāṟu:1
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉi aṭiyoṟṟi amintatu vīṇi eṉṉum icikkaruviyākum.  yāḻil narampu kaṭṭappeṟuvataṟku iṭamāyuḷḷa kōṭu eṉṉum uṟuppiṉ vaḷiviṉi māṟṟi ataṉkaṇ oru narampilēyē ēḻicikaḷiyum toṭuttu vācittaṟ kēṟṟa vakiyil narampukaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyik kuṟittu amikkap peṟṟa narampuk karuviyē vīṇi eṉpatākum.  ovvoru curattiṟkum taṉittaṉi narampu kaṭṭappeṟṟatu paṇṭiya tamiḻar payiṉṟa yāḻākum.  oru narampilēyē pala curaṅkaḷiyum vācittaṟkēṟṟa tāṉa nilikaḷik  koṇṭatu iṉṟiya vīṇik karuviyākum, iṉṟiya vīṇi narampiṟkup patilāka melliya eḵkuk kampi kaḷip peṟṟuḷḷatu.  paṇṭiyat tamiḻar vācitta yāḻākiya icik karuviyum ataṉ aṭiyoṟṟi amikkappeṟṟa, vaṭa nāṭṭiṟ peruka vaḻaṅkiya vīṇiyeṉum icikkaruviyum tamiḻmakkaḷāl tiṟampeṟa vācikkappeṟṟaṉa.
yāḻ uḷḷatu ciṟattal eṉṟa iyaṟki niyatiyiṉ paṭi, vīṇi eṉum icikkaruviyāy malarntatu eṉpatit tamiḻ icikkup perumpaṇiyāṟṟiya takicāl  periyār tiru. mu. āpirakām paṇṭitar avarkaḷum ēṟṟukkoṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  atu aṭiyil varumāṟu:
ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉpatu civanta marattāṟ ceyyappeṟṟu ēḻu tantikaḷuṭaṉ atāvatu - ici narampukaḷ nālum pakka  narampukaḷ mūṉṟum uṭiyatāy taṟkālattil nām vaḻaṅki varum vīṇi eṉpatākat terikiṟatu.  yāḻi eṉṟa oru pūrvakāla mirukattiṉ taliyip pōl mēruviṉ pakkam ceyyappaṭṭiruntatiṉāl yāḻ eṉṟa peyar vantirukkalām eṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu.  vaṭa nāṭṭil yāḻ vakikaḷ vaḻaṅki varukiṟati atikamākak kāṇamāṭṭōm. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷilum micūr irācciyattilum āyiram āyiramāka vaḻaṅkivarum ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṟa ciṟanta vāttiyam taṟkālattil vīṇi eṉṟa peyarāl aḻikkappaṭu kiṟatu.  āṉāl avikaḷi vaṭanāṭṭil kāṇpatu aritu.2
9ām nūṟṟāṇṭilē vīṇi tōṉṟi tirukkōyilkaḷi lellām ciṟappāṉa iṭattip peṟṟukkoṇṭatu.  kōyil kaḷil yāḻum vīṇiyum mīṭṭappeṟṟu varalāyiṉa.  eṉṟālum vīṇi eḻuntatum yāḻkkulam maṟintu viṭavilli: yāḻ, palvēṟu peyar peṟṟum, vaṭivam peṟṟum eḻuntu vantaṉa.  ki. pi. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iruntavarākak karutappeṟum kallāṭar kālattil nāratap pēriyāḻ, kīcaka yāḻ, tumpuru yāḻ, maruttuva yāḻ pōṉṟavi oṉṟu mutal āyiram vari nāṇkaḷip peṟṟu nāṭu muḻuvatum paravip pērum perumiyum peṟṟut tikaḻntaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭut teyvaṅkaḷākiya civaperumāṉum tirumālum yāḻi virumpiṉar.  civaperumāṉ yāḻi mīṭṭiṉār.  appar,
naṅkiyip pākam vittār ñāṉatti navila vittār
aṅkiyil aṉalum vittār āṉiyiṉ urivi 					vittār
taṅkiyil yāḻum vittār tāmari malarum vittār
kaṅkiyic caṭiyil vittār kaḻippālic cērppaṉārē
eṉap pāṭi iruppati eṇṇiyum, iṟivaṉ yāḻmuri nātar eṉṟum, iṟivi yāḻip paḻitta moḻi, yāḻiṉum meṉmoḻi eṉṟa peyarkaḷit tāṅki iruppati eṇṇiyum, yāḻtāṉ tamiḻar kaṇṭa icikkaruvi civaperumāṉukkukantatu eṉṟum nampalām.  vīṇi putiya icikkaruvi tamiḻar kaṇṭa karuviyaṉṟu; vaṭanāṭṭu icikkaruvi eṉṟu cilar kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.  atu tavaṟu.
vēyuṟu tōḷipaṅkaṉ viṭamuṇṭa kaṇṭaṉ
mika nalla vīṇitaṭavi
eṉṟa campantapperumāṉ pāṭalukku, vīṇitaṭavi eṉṟa patat tiṟku yāḻi icittu eṉṟu poruḷ koṇṭu civaperumāṉuk kukantatu yāḻtāṉ vīṇi aṉṟu eṉac cilar vitaṇṭāvātam ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar.
emmiṟi vīṇi vācikkumē
eṉṟa pāṭalukkum yāḻukku vīṇi eṉṟoru peyar iruntatu eṉṟu mīṇṭum poruḷ tarukiṉṟaṉar.  appar, campantar kālattiṟku muṉ yāḻ vīṇi eṉṟa peyarāl aḻikkappaṭṭirukkalām.  āṉāl appar kālattilē yāḻum, vīṇiyum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa.  yāḻ eṉṟoru icikkaruviyum vīṇi eṉṟoru icikkaruvi yum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa.   iṟivaṉ vīṇātarataṭcaṇāmūrtti eṉum peyaruṭaṉ tam kikaḷil kōṭu nīṇṭa iṉṟiya vīṇiyit tāṅki iruppatiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉālum cilar ēṟṟuk koḷvatilli.  itu avarkaḷiṉ muraṭṭup piṭivātamē yoḻiya vēṟilli aṟiviyum uṇmiyiyum ēṟṟukkoḷḷātavar kaḷukku eṉṉatāṉ coṉṉālum payaṉ taruvatilli.
vīṇi, appar aṭikaḷ, aruḷ peṟuvataṟku muṉpē atāvatu ēḻāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉpē uruppeṟṟu viṭṭatu.  atōṭu yāḻip pōṉṟu, teyvīka icikkaruviyāy tēṉ piliṟṟum naṟkaruviyāy iṟivaṉ kikaḷil ēṟiviṭṭatu.  tamiḻakak kōyilkaḷilum pukuntu tēvarkaḷ cuvittumakiḻum amutuṟaḻ tamiḻicik karuviyāy ēṟṟamuṟṟuḷḷatu.  appar aṭikaḷ,
vētaṅkaḷ ōtiyōr vīṇiyēnti viṭiyoṉṟu
tāmēṟi vēta kītā
eṉṟum, maṇivācakap perumāṉ,
paṇṇārnta vīṇi payiṉṟa viṟalavaṉē
ēḻici yāḻ, vīṇi muralak kaṇṭēṉ
paṇṇōṭi yāḻ vīṇi payiṉṟāy pōṟṟi
aṟikalaṅ kuḻala monti vīṇiyākum
eṉṟum tamiḻmaṟi eṉappaṭum tiruvācakattil tiruppaḷḷi eḻucci yil, tirukkōyilil yāḻ, vīṇi ākiya iru icik karuvikaḷ iṭam peṟṟiruntatik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  poruḷit tirittuk kūṟumuṭiyā vakiyil,
iṉṉici vīṇiyar, yāḻiṉar orupāl
eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  itaṉāl yāḻ vīṇiyiṉiṉṟu piṟanta oru icikkaruvi eṉpatum yāḻum vīṇiyum taṉittaṉi icik karuvi eṉpatum naṉkaṟiyalām.  yāḻum vīṇiyum muṟkālattil namatu tirukkōyilkaḷil icikkap peṟṟaṉa. namatu teyvaṅkaḷ ivviru icik karuvikaḷil eḻum ici yolikaḷiyum virumpic cuvittaṉa.  tam kikaḷil ivi iraṇṭiyum taṉit taṉiyākat tāṅki icittaṉa eṉṟu namatu paḻamperum camaya ēṭukaḷ kuṟip piṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭut teyvaṅkaḷ vīṇiyi yāḻip pōl uvantu kikaḷil ēntik kaṉivuṭaṉ mīṭṭi vantaṉa eṉṟu nāyaṉ mārkaḷ navilkiṉṟaṉar.  vīṇi, teyva nalyāḻiṉ tiṟam mikka tiruk kuḻantiyākat tōṉṟiyatāl teyvaṅkaḷ ati virumpi icittu vantaṉa. vēṟu nāṭṭiliruntu vanta icik karuviyāy iruntāl eḷitil namatu teyvaṅkaḷ ati ēṟṟirukka muṭiyātu.  nam nāṭṭu camayac cāṉṟōrkaḷ piṟa nāṭṭu icik karuviyi yāḻōṭu oppiṭṭu matittup pōṟṟi irukka māṭṭārkaḷ.
yāḻ, toṭariciyuṭaṉ palarum kūṭip pāṭum niliyil aminta otticip paṇkaḷi vācippataṟkēṟṟa amippiṉi yuṭiyatu; narampukaḷ atikamākap pūṭṭap peṟṟatu.  āṉāl oru narampilēyē pala icikaḷiyum mīṭṭuvataṟku ēṟṟatāṉa nili kaḷi yāḻ peṟṟirukkavilli.  vīṇi atip peṟṟiruntatu.  atōṭu vīṇi narampukaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyik kuṟivākap peṟṟiruntatu.
nāḷaṭivil vīṇi cemmippaṭutta peṟṟatu; atu nallamippum eḻilum ēṟṟamum peṟṟatu; yāḻi viṭa eḷitāka icikka vallatu.  vīṇiyil narampukaḷukkup patilāka eḵkuk kampikaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟaṉa.  nāṭṭiṉ taṭpa veppanili yāḻiṉ narampukaḷip pātittatupōla vīṇit tantikaḷip pātippa tilli.  eḵkuk kampikaḷ avaciyattiṟkēṟpa muṟukkāṇikaḷ mūlam melittal valittal mutaliyavikaḷic ceyyakkūṭiyavi kaḷāyt tikaḻntaṉa.  itil eḷitil paṇkaḷiyum kamakaṅkaḷiyum vācikkamuṭiyum.
itaṉāl, vīṇi, yāḻiviṭa makkaḷiṉ uḷḷattik kavarntatu.  vīṇiyi vaṭa nāṭṭārum piṟa nāṭṭārum mīṭṭak kaṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar.  tamiḻakattil civamum viṇavamum vīḻcci peṟṟaṉa.  tamiḻar āṭci māṟiyatu.  tamiḻ makkaḷ aṭimi vāḻvu naṭattiṉar.  vaṟumiyum ciṟumiyum avarkaḷi vāṭṭi vatittaṉa.  nōy pēy pōl piccup piṭuṅkit tiṉṟatu.  inniliyil yāḻkaḷ tīṇṭuvāraṟṟuk kiṭantu maṟintaṉa.  vīṇiyi nāṭuvōr kuṟin taṉar.  ataṉāl vaṭa intiyāvil vīṇi celvākkup peṟṟatu.  makkaḷ atip pōṟṟi mīṭṭuvārāyiṉar.
puttarkaḷ vīṇiyi virumpavilli.  yāḻip peritum virumpiṉar.  puttam vaḷarnta īḻakam, kāḻakam, kampōcakam, cāvakam, campā, cappāṉ, cīṉam, parmā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil ellām yāḻ iṭam peṟṟatu.  atu āṅkāṅkē camaya icik karuviyākac ciṟappuṟṟatu.
inniliyil 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum 20ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭilum tamiḻakattil āriyar celvākku ōṅkiyatu.  vīṇi, āriyar icikkaruvi eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟappaṭṭatu.  ataṉit tamiḻar allātār pōṟṟavum payilavum muṟpaṭṭaṉar.  tamiḻaṟiñar kūṭa iti nampavum eḻutavum muṟpaṭṭaṉar.  caravati kiyilum vīṇi ēṟṟappaṭṭatu.
yāḻ nūli yātta aruṭṭiru vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ pōṉṟōrkaḷik kūṭa, intak kūṭṭam mayakki viṭṭatu.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka aṭikaḷār tam yāḻ nūlil pala viṭaṅkaḷil vīṇi āriyar icikkaruvi eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  itaṟkuc cilariṉ mēṟkōḷkaḷiyum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  intiyak kali mutu nitiyam eṉṉum āṅkila nūliṉiṉṟu oru mēṟkōḷiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār. atu, varumāṟu;
vētattiṟ kūṟap peṟṟatum, pañcacikaṉ eṉṉum ici vallōṉ ēḻu curaṅkaḷiyum irupattoru mūrccaṉikaḷiyum oru narampilē icittuk kāṭṭuvataṟku icintatum, paratamuṉi kiyāṇṭatum vīṇiyē
putta camayamāṉatu evviṭattum paravat toṭaṅkiya kālattilē accamayat talivarkaḷ vēta oḻukkaṅkaḷiyum avi tamakkuriya kuru vakuppiṉariyum paḻiya āriya moḻiyiyum otukki viṭṭup potu makkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya moḻiyit taḻuvip putiya kuru vakuppiyum oḻukka muṟikaḷiyum vakuttuk koṇṭārkaḷ.  atu pōlavē āriya vīṇiyiyum otukkiviṭṭup potu makkaḷ kik karuviyākiya yāḻiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum tiruviḻāk koṇ ṭāṭṭaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkumpaṭi ceytārkaḷ.  āṉāl āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭilē putta camayam nāṭṭilē oḷi kuṉṟiyatōṭu paḻiya yāḻum maṟintu pōyiṟṟu.1
tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kavaṉak kuṟivālum kāla veḷḷattālum tamiḻakatti viṭṭu yāḻ maṟintu viṭṭatu.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka oṉṟiraṇṭukūṭa eñci irukkavilli.  iṉṟu tamiḻakattil yāḻ uruvik kaṇṭavarkaḷ illi eṉṟu kūṟum aḷaviṟku yāḻ maṭṭumaṉṟi atu irunta iṭam kūṭat teriyātu maṟintuviṭṭatu.  vīṇi vaṭa nāṭṭu icikkaruvi eṉṟa mayakkattilō allatu piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉālō tamiḻnāṭṭut tirukkōyilkaḷil mīṭṭap paṭṭu vanta vīṇiyum niṟuttappaṭṭuviṭṭatu.  iṉṟu kōyil kaḷil vīṇi icikkappaṭuvatu aritākiviṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭu kiṟatu.
āyiṉum ikkālattilum vīṇik karuvikaḷil tañcāvūr vīṇi eṉpatu ciṟappāṉatākum.  tamiḻakattiṉ ciṟappaminta tañci mānakaril vīṇikaḷ aḻakākac ceyyappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  akkaruvikaḷic ceyyum ki vaṇṇam vāynta toḻilāḷarkaḷ tañci māvaṭṭattil iruntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  vaṭanāṭṭiṟkum piṟa nāṭṭiṟkum tañciyiliruntu vīṇikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭu aṉuppappeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.
yāḻiṉ iṉimiyum āṟṟalum
yāḻ, iṉimiyāṉa iciyi eḻuppum icik karuvi maṭṭumaṉṟu koṭiya uḷḷam vāynta arakkarkaḷiyum aṉpum aruḷum uṭiyavarkaḷāy māṟṟum āṟṟalum peṟṟuḷḷatu.  aṉpē, aruḷē amitiyē uruvāy aminta āṇṭavaṉ kōpaṅ koṇṭāl akkōpatti evvāṟṟāṉum taṇikkamuṭiyātu.  āṉāl aḷappariya āṟṟal vāynta araṉāriṉ kōpattiyum taṇikka vallatu yāḻ eṉpataṟku namatu purāṇak katikaḷil eṇṇaṟṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ uṇṭu.  avikaḷil oṉṟu irāvaṇaṉ kati.
irāvaṇaṉ ilaṅki vēntaṉ.  avaṉ arakkaṉ eṉṟum, koṭiyavaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉatu pakivarkaḷum vaṭa nāṭṭārum eḻutiya nūṟkaḷ kūṟum.  āṉāl tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ irāvaṇaṉ civapaktaṉ; ici vallunaṉ.  avaṉ cellum iṭaṅkaḷuk kellām oru pūcip peṭṭiyum ataṉuḷ oru poṉṉāṟ ceyta civaliṅkattiyum vittuc ceṉṟāṉ.  nāṭōṟum civa pūci ceytu vantāṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvar.  ivaṉ nīṟaṇintavaṉ; piṟa uyir kaḷukku ūṟu ceyyātavaṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
irāvaṇaṉ mēlatu niṟu, vaṇṇan taruvatu nīṟu
eṉṟu campantar tam tirunīṟṟuppatittil irāvaṇaṉip pōṟṟu kiṉṟār.  irāvaṇaṉ taṉ celvap perukki eṇṇātu tiru nīṟṟi avamatikkātu aṇintu civaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu, avaṉ aruḷi aṭintatu eṇṇat takkatākum.
irāvaṇaṉ nākar kulattōṉum ciṟpavallunaṉumākiya mayaṉ makaḷ maṇṭōtari eṉṉum eḻilmikka civapaktiyi maṇantu koṇṭavaṉ.  avaṉi maṇivācakar,
ērtarum ēḻulakētta evvuruvun taṉṉuruvāy
ārkalicūḻ teṉṉilaṅki aḻakamar vaṇṭōtarikkum
pēraruḷ iṉpam aḷitta peruntuṟi mēyapirāṉ					(tiruvācakam: kuyil 18. 5-7)
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu ārāyattakkatu.  irāvaṇaṉ maṭṭumallāmal avaṉ maṉivi kaṟpukkaraci maṇṭōtariyum civaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu avaṉatu aṉpiyum aruḷiyum peṟṟavarkaḷākak karutappaṭuvar.
irāvaṇaṉ kayili maliyik kaṭantu ataṟkappāluḷḷa nāṭukaḷi yellām vella eṇṇit taṉ vimāṉattil ēṟic ceṉṟāṉ.  viṇmuṭṭa niṟkum kayili mali vimāṉattit taṭi ceytatu.  eṉavē avaṉ vimāṉattiṉiṉṟu iṟaṅki, maliyit takarttu vaḻiceytu ati kaṭakka muyaṉṟāṉ; muṭiyavilli; taṉatu talivaṉākiya civaṉi niṉintāṉ; taṉ yāḻi eṭuttu mīṭṭi ṉāṉ; iṉṉiciyi icittāṉ.  avaṉi niṉintu aḻutu aruḷtara vēṇṭiṉāṉ; araṉār avaṉukkuk kāṭci aḷittu varampala aruḷiṉār.  avaṉ akamakiḻntu ilaṅki mīṇṭāṉ.  itaṉāl, pukaḻēntip pulavar
neṟṟit taṉikkaṇ neruppik kuḷirvikkum
koṟṟat taṉiyāḻ
eṉṟu pāṭiyatu mikap poruttamāṉatākum.  civacamaya kuravar kaḷil oruvarāṉa appar aṭikaḷ,
pattilaṅku vāyālum pāṭalkēṭṭup parintavaṉukku
irāvaṇaṉeṉ ṟīnta nāmatattuvaṉi
eṉṟu pāṭi iruppati ārāyntāl irāvaṇaṉukkum civa perumāṉukkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa aṉpum toṭarpum naṉku teriyum.
nāṉmukam, tirumāl mutaliya tēvarkaḷ, paṉṉūṟāṇṭu tavam purintum civaperumāṉ tiruuruvattiyō allatu tirumuṭiyiyō allatu tiruvaṭiyiyō kāṇātu tāḻvuṟṟu niṉṟaṉar.  arum perum muṉivar pallāṇṭu pala ariya tavam iyaṟṟiyum iṟivaṉ tiruuruvik kāṇamuṭiyātu pōyiṉar.  civapaktiyil ulakilē oppārum mikkārumaṟṟu viḷaṅkiya peṇṇiṉallāḷ peruṅ kuṭiyiṟ piṟanta uttami tavaniṟi tamiḻc celvi kārikkāl ammiyār iṟivaṉik kāṇa nāṭi nōṉpu nōṟṟu aṟam pala purintu tavam pala iyaṟṟik kāṭumali aṉittum tirintu, uṭalellām nintu nontu melintu uyiraṉittum vāṭi, kayilamalikkup pōy ataṉ uyarnta koṭumuṭiyil civaṉ iruppār eṉṟu nampi ataṉmītu ēṟa muṉintār.  iṟivaṉ vāḻum intap paṉimalimītu kāl vikkakkūṭātu eṉṟu eṇṇik kālāl naṭavātu taliyāl naṭantu ceṉṟu tam uṭampil uḷḷa ūṉ ellām karintu elumpellām tēyntu malimītu ēṟic ceṉṟum iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷik kāṇa muṭiyavilli.  ataṉāl avar uḷamnontu,
aṉṟut tiruuruvam kāṇātē āṭpaṭṭēṉ
iṉṟun tiruuruvaṅ kāṇkilēṉ - eṉṟuntāṉ
evvuruvō empirāṉ eṉpōrkaṭ keṉṉurikkēṉ
evvuruvō niṉṉuruva mētu?
eṉṟu pāṭiyiruppatu ārāyattakkatu.  irāvaṇaṉ imayamaliyiṉ kīḻ akappaṭṭu varunti iṟivaṉi utavikku aḻittu, yāḻ mīṭṭiṉāṉ.  avviṉṉoli kēṭṭu iṟivaṉ maṉam iḷaki ōṭōṭiyum kayili malimītiṉiṉṟu kīḻē iṟaṅki vantu irāvaṇaṉ icitta paṇṇoliyic cevimaṭuttu iṉpuṟṟu makiḻntu avaṉukkut tiruuruvik kāṭṭi avaṉ kēṭṭa varaṅkaḷum vāḷum aḷittār.
irāvaṇaṉ orumuṟi taḻiḻakattiṉ mītu paṭiyeṭuttuvara muṟpaṭṭāṉ.  valimi vāynta irāvaṇaṉit tamiḻnāṭṭut tārvēntarkaḷ pōriṭṭu veṟṟi peṟuvatu eḷitāṉatā?  iti aṟinta tamiḻaka maṉṉarkaḷ kalaṅkiṉar.  āṉāl irāvaṇiṉ uyariya paṇpiyum avaṉatu aṟaneṟiyiyum aṟinta akattiyar irāvaṇaṉi aḻittu irāvaṇēcuvarā!  nī tamiḻakatti āḷavēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟāyā?  ām eṉṟāl tamiḻakattōṭu pōriṭṭup pala makkaḷik koṉṟu nāṭṭiyum kāṭṭiyum aḻittu pāḻ paṭṭuk kiṭakkum maṇṇi āḷavirumpukiṟāyā?  uṉatu valimi vāynta ampukaḷiṉ oṉṟiyum ataṉ uṟiyiṉiṉṟu eṭukkāmal oru uyiriyum vatikkātu oru poruḷiyum aḻikkātu uṉ kiyiluḷḷa yāḻikkoṇṭē tamiḻakatti veṉṟu ati 
āḷa virumpukiṟāyā?  eṉṟār.  irāvaṇaṉ makiḻntu yāḻāl tamiḻakatti veṉṟu āḷavirumpukiṟēṉ eṉṟāṉ.  appaṭi yāṉāl, nī yāḻāl tamiḻaṉi veṉṟu viṭṭāl tamiḻakatti uṭaṉaṭiyākap peṟuvataṟkut taṭi illi eṉṟār.  ēḻulakilum iṇiyaṟṟa icivallōṉāy viḷaṅkiya irāvaṇap periyār (Ravana the Great) iṟumpūteyti inta icippōṭṭikku iṇaṅki yāḻik kiyil eṭuttār.  uṭaṉē akattiyar akamakiḻntu nī icip pōṭṭiyil eṉṉi veṉṟuviṭṭālē pōtum eṉṟār.  irāvaṇaṉ nallatu, ākaṭṭum eṉṟār.  pintamiḻ piṟanta pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiluḷḷa potiki malimītu icip pōṭṭi toṭaṅkiṟṟu.  kuṟumuṉivar tīntamiḻ payiṉṟa teyva nalyāḻit tam tirukkarattil ēnti ati mīṭṭiṉār.  appēriyāḻiṉiṉṟu paṇṇoli eḻuntatu.  arukē irunta kaṭumpāṟi kaṉivuṟṟu nīrp poruḷāki iḷaki ōṭiyatu.  irāvaṇaṉ kaṇṇāl kaṇṭu kaḷittāṉ.  appoḻutu akattiyar yāḻ oliyāl iḷakiyōṭum kaṟkuḻampil tam kiyilirunta yāḻi vittār.  pākāy ōṭiya pāṟi uṭaṉē kallāy, yāḻip paṟṟikkoṇṭu iṟukiyatu.  akattiyar puṉmuṟuval koṇṭu irāvaṇēcuvarā, inta yāḻi eṭuttu mīṭṭu eṉṟār.  pāṟiyi nekiḻac ceytu patintu kiṭakkum yāḻi eṭuttu mīṭṭa eṇṇiya irāvaṇaṉ muyaṟci oṉṟum palikkavilli.  nīṇṭanēram taṉatu āṟṟal aṉittiyum cela viṭṭup pārttum taṉ icittiṟaṉiyellām kāṭṭip pārttum, muṭiyavilli.  vēṟu yāḻi eṭuttu mīṭṭiyum pāṭiyum kal nekiḻavilli; yāḻik kalliṉiṉṟu acikkavē muṭiya villi.  irāvaṇaṉ taṉ tōlviyi eṇṇit talikuṉintāṉ.  akattiyar vēṟu oru yāḻi eṭuttu mīṭṭiṉār.  anta pāṟi mīṇṭum nekiḻntu pākāy iḷaki nekiḻntu ōṭiyatu.  akattiyar atiṟkiṭanta tam yāḻit tam kiyāl eṭuttār.  irāvaṇaṉ kaṇṭu iṟumpūteytiṉāṉ.  tamiḻakattiṉ icittiṟaṉi uḷamāra, vāyārap pukaḻntāṉ.  atu mutal tamiḻakattik kavaravēṇṭum eṉṟa karutti maṟantāṉ.  taṉ vākki iṟutivarik kāppāṟṟi ṉāṉ.  taṉ kulateyvamākiya civaṉuṟiyum tamiḻttirunāṭṭiṉ - teṉṉakattiṉ icic ciṟappik kaṇṭu kaḷittu akattiyariṭam viṭi peṟṟu, avaratu vāḻttiyum tamiḻakattiṉ nēcattiyum civaperumāṉiṉ aruḷiyum aṉpiyum peṟṟu īḻam mīṇṭāṉ.
itu oru paḻiya purāṇakkati.  itil kūṟappeṟṟa irāvaṇaṉ arakkarkulat talivaṉ.  ilaṅkāpuriyiṉ iṇiyilā vēntaṉ.  teṉṉavaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭam peṟṟavaṉ.  ivaṉ nākarkula naṅki maṇṭōtari eṉṉum maṅki nallāḷi maṇantavaṉ.  iruvarum civaperumāṉukku āṭpaṭṭavarkaḷ.  tirāviṭa kulattōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpu koṇṭavaṉ.  ivaṉ civavaḻipāṭṭic ceytuvarupa vaṉ eṉpati vaṭamoḻi nūṟkaḷkūṭa valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.
ivaṉ iṟivaṉ icivaṭiviṉaṉ eṉṟu eṇṇiyavaṉ.  iciyil vallōṉ; iciyāl iṟivaṉ aruḷippeṟṟavaṉ.  iciyiṉāl iṟivaṉ tiruuruvik kaṇṭāṉ; avaṉ tiru varuḷip peṟṟavaṉ.  taṉatu koṭiyil yāḻip poṟittavaṉ.  ivaṉukku aruḷkūrntu iṟivaṉ irāvaṇaṉ eṉṟu peyar aḷittāṉ.  ataṉāl iṟivaṉ irāvaṇa aṉukkiraka mūrtti eṉṉum ciṟappup peyarum peṟṟāṉ.  civaperumāṉuṭiya 64 mūrttaṅkaḷil irāvaṇa aṉukkiraka mūrttamum oṉṟu.
ici, makkaḷ uḷḷattip paṇpaṭuttukiṟatu.  atu arakkar uḷḷattiyum iḷakac ceykiṟatu.  kalliyum iḷakkum; uruk kattiyum urukkum.  teyvaṅkaḷiṉ kōpattiyum karivikkum.  irāvaṇaṉ arakkaṉtāṉ; āṉāl ici, avaṉ uḷḷam aṉpum aruḷum niṟiyumāṟu ceytatu.  irāvaṇaṉ iyaṟṟiya ici nūl irāvaṇīyam.  avaṉatu yāḻ irāvaṇātam eṉṟu vaṭamoḻi yāḷar kūṟuvar.1  avaṉ maruttuva nipuṇaṉ.  avaṉ iyaṟṟiya nūl iṉṟum ciṅkaḷa moḻiyil irukkiṟatu.

10.  yāḻum vīṇiyum
tamiḻar kaṇṭa narampuk karuvikaḷ palappala.  avikaḷil cīrum ciṟappum peṟṟavi yāḻum vīṇiyumākum.  yāḻ ātiyil eḻunta teyvat taṉmi vāynta icik karuviyākum.  vīṇi ataṟkup piṉṉar.  aṭuttapaṭiyākac ciṟappuṟṟu eḻunta icik karuviyākum.  vīṇi yāḻ vakikaḷil oṉṟāka viḷaṅkiya ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ aṭiyoṟṟi amikkappaṭṭa cīriya narampuk karuviyākum.
yāḻil narampu kaṭṭappeṟṟirukkum kōṭu, eṉṉum uṟuppi nimirttic cemmi peṟac ceytu, atiluḷḷa oru narampilēyē ēḻu icikaḷiyum toṭuttu mīṭṭum vakiyil narampukaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyik kuṟittu ēḻākac ceyyappeṟṟa narampuk karuviyē vīṇi.
vīṇi, cevikku iṉimiyum uḷḷattiṟku uṇarcciyum uyirukku uvakiyum ūṭṭavallatu.  itu yāḻ pōṉṟa icik karuviyātaliṉ yāḻiṉ perumiyiyum pēṟṟiyum peṟṟatu.  yāḻip pōlavē teyvaṅkaḷiṉ tirukkaraṅkaḷilum iṭam peṟṟatu.
miṭaṟṟukkum vīṇi eṉṟa peyar uṇṭu.  paṇṇiṟ polinta pākkaḷi makkaḷ, teḷivāy aṟintu koḷḷum vaṇṇam kuyiluvōriṉ miṭaṟṟolikkuc caṟṟu iṇiyākac ciṟu karuviyāy miḷirvatāl vīṇi virivil ciṟappeytiyatu.  vīṇiyilē ēḻu kēḷvikaḷukkum (captacuraṅkaḷukkum) uriya paṉṉiru pālikaḷip (12 icittāṉaṅkaḷip) piṟappikkaveṉa ovvoru pālikkum ovvoru iṭamāka (tāṉamāka) paṉṉi raṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷ nilipeṟa amintirukkiṉṟaṉa.
oppaṟṟa mutuperum camayakuravarum toṇṭarumāṉa appar aṭikaḷ tam tēvārat tiruppatikattil iṟivaṉuṭiya tiruvaṭi niḻali, vīṇiyiṉ iṉṉolikku oppiṭṭuḷḷār.  atu varumāṟu:
mācil vīṇiyum māli matiyamum
vīcu teṉṟalum vīṅkiḷa vēṉilum
mūcu vaṇṭaṟip poykiyum pōṉṟatē
īca ṉenti iṇiyaṭi nīḻalē
inta oru pāṭalē vīṇiyiṉ ēṟṟatti eṭuttukkāṭṭu vataṟku ēṟṟa cāṉṟākum.
ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻiṉ aṭiyoṟṟi eḻunta, vīṇi eṉum ariya icikkaruvi caṅka kālattil illi.  caṅkat toki nūṟkaḷil yāḻit tavira, vīṇi eṉṟa narampuk karuvi kūṟappaṭavē illi.  ki. pi. iraṇṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉṉarē vīṇi tōṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu cila aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṟārkaḷ.
vīṇi, ki. pi. 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tañci irakunāta nāyakka maṉṉariṉ kālattiltāṉ, iṉṟu peṟṟirukkum eḻil vaṭivip peṟṟatu.  itaṉāl vīṇi tañcāvūr vīṇi eṉṉum tuñcāp perumiyip peṟṟatu.  mutaṉmutalākat tamiḻakattil yāḻ ārāycciyit tuvakki vitta aṟiñar vipulāṉantar 9, tantikaḷi yuṭiya tumpuru yāḻum orē tantiyiyuṭiya maruttuva yāḻum, vīṇiyip pōṉṟa karuvikaḷē eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
vīṇi piṟkālattil tōṉṟiya narampuk karuviyāy irun tālum tamiḻc cāṉṟōr vīṇikku ēṟṟam aḷittu vantaṉar.  cēkkiḻār aṭikaḷ,
kōtiyar kuḻalcūḻ vaṇṭiṉ kuḻāttoli yoru pāṟkōla
vētiyar vētavāymi mikumoli yorupāṉ mikka
vētamil vipañci vīṇi yāḻoli yorupā lēttu
nātamil kalaṅkal kīta nayappoli yorupālāka.					(periya purāṇam 1200)
eṉṟu periya purāṇattil tiruñāṉa campantap perumāṉ tiru maṇattil vipañci vīṇi mīṭṭappeṟṟa ciṟappi eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār.
vīṇi piṟanta kati
yāḻ piṟanta katiyi ituvari, nām naṉkaṟiyavilli.  āṉāl vīṇi piṟanta kati namakkut teriyum.
kayilāca maliyil malimakaḷākiya umātēvi ayarntu tūṅkik koṇṭirukkiṟāḷ.  avaḷ vaḷikaḷ oḷirum tam ki kaḷit tam mārpiṉmītu vittapaṭiyē tuyilkiṟāḷ.  avaḷ tūkkat til mūccuviṭum poḻutu oru iṉṉoli arumpiyatu.  avaḷatu parutta iru koṅkikaḷiṉmītu vittirunta avaḷatu kikaḷil aṇintirunta vaḷikaḷ mūccu viṭumpoḻutu vimmit tāmum iyakkattāl mella oli ceytaṉa.  ampikiyiṉ aḻakoḷirum intak kōlamum avaḷatu mūkkiliruntu eḻum melliya oliyum civaperumāṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷiṟpaṭṭaṉa; kātukaḷil olittaṉa.  attirukkōlam oru nittiya icic ciṉṉampōl iruntatu.  atiṉiṉṟu civaperumāṉ tēviyiṉ tirucciṉṉatti niṉip pūṭṭum oṉṟi uruvākka niṉittār.  avar ampikiyiṉ ayarntu tuyilum ariya kāṭciyik kaṇṭu kaḷikūrntu mōṉat tiyāṉak kōlam pūṇṭār.  appoḻutu avarkoṇṭa niliyi vittu avari, yōka taṭcaṇā mūrtti eṉṟaṉar.  intat tiyāṉa niliyilum iṟiviyiṉ eḻiṟkōlam avaruṭiya akattiṉiṉṟu akalātu kāṭci aḷittu vantatu.  ataṉ viḷivāka ampikiyiṉ aḻakiya peruntaṉaṅkaḷ  curikkāykaḷākavum ataṉ mītu vit tirunta avaratu ki vīṇit taṇṭākavum eḻuntaṉa.  iṟiva ṉuṭiya intak kaṟpaṉiyiṉ viḷivāka uruttira vīṇi uruvā yiṟṟu.  ivveḻiṟ kōlattil eḻunta iṟivaṉuṭiya tōṟṟam vīṇātara taṭcaṇāmūrttam eṉṟu aḻikkap peṟṟatu.
vīṇiyiṉ nīṇṭa vaṭivam melliyalāḷākiya ampiki yiṉ tiruuruvattik kuṟikkiṟatu.  avaḷatu tirukkaraṅkaḷiyum atu niṉippūṭṭi varukiṟatu.  vīṇiyiṉ iru kuṭaṅkaḷum iru taṉaṅ kaḷiyum, vīṇiyiṉ iṉṉoli umātēviyiṉ melliya mūcci yum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivvāṟu iṟiviyiṉ eḻil uruvattik kāṭṭum tōṟṟamum oliyum uḷḷa vīṇi ilayam uṭiyatu.  ilayam umipaṅkaṉukku uriyatu.  ataṉāl vīṇi civam, cakti eṉṟa iruvarukkum uriya ciṉṉamāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu.  nāṉku tanti kaḷiyuṭiyatum vīṇiyil mukkiyamāṉatum ākiya vācikkum pakuti curutikkut teyvamākiya umā tēviyiṉ amcam.  arukiluḷḷa ēṉiya mīṭṭalukkuriya mūṉṟu tantikaḷum tāḷam allatu ilayat tiṟku amintavi.  antap pakuti ilayattiṉ āti teyvamākiya civapirāṉuṭiya amcam.  inta icik karuviyi iṟivaṉ taṉṉuṭiya tiyāṉattiṉāl uruvākkiṉāṉ.  piṟaku nāṉmukaṉukku ikkaruviyi aḷittāṉ.  piramatēvaṉ piṉ taṉ maṉiviyākiya kalimakaḷukku aḷittāṉ eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ1.
vīṇikku ēṉiya icik karuvikaḷukkillāta ēṟṟamum tōṟṟamum cīrum ciṟappum uṇṭu.  atu eḻil mika vāyntu iṉitāyk kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu.  ataṉ iṉṉoli uḷḷattiṟku uṇarcciyūṭṭi uyirukku uvaki aḷikkiṟatu.  perum muḻakka miṉṟi (valivu niliyilum, tāratāyiyilum, mantāra tāyiyilum, melivu niliyilum) iṉitāka icikkum ēṟṟam vāyntuḷḷatu.  vīṇiyi mīṭṭi ati niṟuttiviṭṭāl ataṉ iṉiya nātam uṭaṉē niṉṟu viṭuvatilli.  ataṉ iṉṉoli alikaḷ nīṇṭa nēram olittu melliya alikaḷippōl aṭuttaṭuttup paravi niṟkum.  valatu kiyāl mīṭṭiya oli iṭatu kiyāl icikkum pōtum iṭiyaṟātu iṉpamūṭṭum.  ēṉiya icik karuvikaḷ mīṭṭi, tantiyiṉiṉṟu kiyi allatu villi eṭuttatum oli ōyntu viṭum.  vayaliṉiṉiṉṟu villi eṭuttuviṭṭāl uṭaṉē oli niṉṟu viṭum.  vīṇi avvāṟaṉṟu.
vīṇikku oli eḻuppum tantikaḷ ēḻu.  avikaḷil nāṉku vīṇiyiṉ mārpil uḷḷa meṭṭukaḷiṉ mītuḷḷaṉa.  vīṇit taṇṭiṉmītu porutti irukkum ulōkat tuṇṭukaḷē meṭṭu kaḷākum.  immeṭṭukaḷ vīṇiyil 24 uḷḷaṉa.  mēlum 4 kampikaḷukkum 96 iṭaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa.  meṭṭukaḷ mītu cellum kampikaḷi iṭatu kiviralkaḷāl aḻuttit tēyttu curaṅkaḷi mīṭṭuvar, vīṇiyi icippōr.  itil taṉiyāka uḷḷa 3 kampikaḷ tāḷak kampikaḷ eṉappaṭum.  ivaṟṟi valatukic cuṇṭu viralāl, mēl nōkki mīṭṭuvar.  itiṉiṉṟu eḻum oliyik kuṭam taṇṭu ākiya pākaṅkaḷ perukki aṉuppukiṉṟaṉa.1
vīṇi, iṉṟuḷḷa uyarnta, tantik karuvikaḷil oṉṟu.  itu mika nuṭpamāṉa kēḷviyi (curutiyi) aḷakkum oru ciṟanta karuviyāka matikkappaṭukiṟatu.  inta icik karuviyiṉ cīriyum ciṟappiyuṅ kaṇṭu vaṭa intiyarkaḷ iti atikamākap payaṉ paṭutta muṉvantuḷḷaṉar.  tamiḻarkaḷ vīṇiyi maṭṭumaṉṟi yāḻ, kuḻal, muracu mutaliya icik karuvikaḷiyum tamiḻp paṇkaḷiyum nīṇṭa kālamākak kiviṭṭu viṭṭaṉar.  tamiḻarkaḷiṉ āṭci maṟinta tōṭu avarkaḷiṉ icip payiṟciyum, piṟa, kalip payiṟcikaḷum maṟintuviṭṭaṉa.  tamiḻar taṅkaḷiṉ pala icikkaruvikaḷiyum pala tamiḻp paṇkaḷiyum nīṇṭa kālamāka maṟantuviṭṭaṉar.  tamiḻar kaḷiṉ icikkaruvikaḷ palavaṟṟi, piṟa iṉattavarkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭiya tāka eṇṇi vaḷarkka muṟpaṭṭaṉar.  tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ icik karuvikaḷākiya palavaṟṟi, piṟa iṉattavarkaḷākiya yāḻ, vīṇi, mattaḷam, timili, kaṇappaṟi, kuḻal, kimaṇi pōṉṟavikaḷip piṟa iṉattavarkaḷiṉ icik karuvikaḷākakkūṭa eṇṇat talip paṭṭuviṭṭaṉar.  cātāraṇap pāmarat tamiḻarkaḷil cilar intap pāpattic ceytāl tamiḻaṉṉi maṉṉikkalām; āṉāl muṉṉēṟṟam aṭintavarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭum makkaḷ ip pāvattic ceytu vantatāl, tamiḻaṉṉi eppaṭi maṉṉippāḷ?  ipperum pāvattiṟku kaḻuvāy tēṭat tamiḻ icik kaliñarkaḷ iṉṟē muṉvaravēṇṭum.  ēṉ?  tamiḻaka aracē muṉ varavēṇṭum, eṉpatu ematu uḷamārnta āci.
namatu yāḻiṉip paṟṟi vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷa ici vallunarkaḷ oru ciṟitum aṟiyārkaḷ.  āṉāl vīṇiyip paṟṟi naṉku ārāyntirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.  vīṇi, yāḻiṉiṉṟu, yāḻiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyāl eḻunta oru icikkaruvi eṉpatu avarkaḷ cevikaḷil viḻuntatē illi.  āṉāl namatu ici nipuṇarkaḷ vīṇiyip paṟṟi aṟintiruppati viṭa avarkaḷ atikam aṟintirukkiṟārkaḷ.  vīṇi mikat toṉmiyāṉa icikkaruvi.  narampuk karuvikaḷiṉ tāy vīṇi.  āriyarkaḷ kaṇṭa cīriya icik karuvi.  ātikālattil iṟivaṉ vētakāla muṉivarkaḷukku aḷit tāṉ.  vētaṅkaḷil itaṟku ātāram uṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟi varukiṉ ṟaṉar.  ippoḻutu etaṟkum cāṉṟukaḷ kēṭkum kālamāka iruppa tāl irukku, yacur, cāmam, atarvaṇam mutaliya āriyarkaḷiṉ vētaṅkaḷil ātāraṅkaḷ tēṭiṉar; kiṭikkavilli.  ōviyaṅkaḷ, ciṟpaṅkaḷ, cācaṉaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa ātāraṅkaḷum kiṭikkavilli.  viññāṉa rītiyāka vīṇiyiṉ tōṟṟattiyum ataṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyiyum avarkaḷāl kāṇavē muṭiyavilli.  āriyarkaḷ pārata pūmikku varumpoḻutu vīṇiyik koṇṭuvantārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟku immi aḷavum ātāram illi.  vīṇi tirāviṭarkaḷiṭamiruntu, allatu avarkaḷ vaḻivanta tamiḻar kaḷiṭamiruntu āriyarkaḷ peṟṟa icikkaruvi eṉṟō allatu aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ arumpiya arappā nākarikatti ṉiṉṟu pariṇamittatu eṉṟō kūṟa avarkaḷ nāṇukiṟārkaḷ.  āṉāl cila vaṭanāṭṭu icivallunarkaḷ vīṇi, aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar eḻunta intiyap paṇpāṭṭiṉiṉṟu eḻuntatu eṉṟu kūṟa maṉamiṉṟi vīṇi eṉum cīriya icikkaruvi piṉlāntu, cārcciyā kākacup pakutikaḷil tōṉṟiya icikkaruvi eṉṟu tayakkamiṉṟi kūṟiyum eḻutiyum varukiṉṟaṉar.
nām, vīṇi, tirāviṭap perumakkaḷiṉ paṇpāṭṭil arumpiya ariya icikkaruvi eṉṟu kūṟukiṟōm.  itaṉi mēṉāṭṭu viññāṉa viṟpaṉṉarkaḷ aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar eḻunta arappāp paṇpāṭṭiṉ ariya mūlattiyuṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  āṉāl vīṇiyiṉ tōṟṟamum ataṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyum, varalāṟum ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷum ēṉiya cāṉṟukaḷum tamiḻakattil maṭṭumē kāṇamuṭiyum eṉṟu nām tiriyamākak kūṟamuṭiyum.  vīṇiyiṉ tāyk karuviyāy viḷaṅkiya yāḻiṉ ilakkaṇam tamiḻakattit tavira vēṟu eṅkum kāṇamuṭi yātu.  āṉāl aiyāyiram āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar ceyyappeṟṟa vīṇiyiṉ tāyk karuviyākiya yāḻiṉ acal uruvam tamiḻakattil ituvari kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭavilli.  itu tamiḻarkaḷiṉ peruntavak kuṟivu.  āṉāl yāḻk karuviyi otta icik karuvikaḷ cumār āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ, mēṟku āciyāvil arumpi cumēriya nāṭṭil tōṉṟi ṭikkirīcu, yūpraṭṭīcu āṟṟaṅkarikaḷil eḻunta nākarikaṅkaḷil vaḷarcci aṭintatu.1  ekiptil ōṭum nīla āṟṟaṅkariyil eḻunta nakaraṅkaḷi luḷḷa māḷikikaḷil maḻali moḻi miḻaṟṟiyatu.  kirēkkar, urōmar pōṉṟa yavaṉar nāṭukaḷil ariyaṇi ēṟiyiruntatu eṉṟu pala mēl nāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  mēlum itu an nāṭukaḷil tēciya icik karuviyāy ārp (Harp) peṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ makōṉṉata niliyil māṇpuṟṟu viḷaṅkiyatu.  kiṟittava ūḻi arumpuvataṟku 9 nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar ōmar (Homer) eṉṉum kaviñar eḻutiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷilellām namatu yāḻ ciṟappuṟṟu oḷirntatu.  kirēkka nāṭṭil itu tēcīya icik karuviyāy uyarttappeṟṟatu.  iti urōmarkaḷum pōṟṟa muṟpaṭṭaṉar.  itaṉ payaṉāka inta iṉiya icik karuvi airōppā muḻuvatum paraviyatu.  uruciya nāṭṭil kūṭa inta icikkaruvi celvākkup peṟṟu eḻuntatu.  putta kurumārkaḷiṉ naṉmuyaṟci yāl intiya yāḻ īḻam, cāvakam, kampōcakam, cappāṉ, campā, pāli cīṉam, parmā mutaliya kiḻakku āciya nāṭukaḷilellām paravi cīrum ciṟappum peṟṟu oḷirntatu.  eṉṟālum evarum inta icik karuvi mēṉāṭṭil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatākak kūṟa muṉvaravilli.  āriyarkaḷ kaṇṭa ariya icikkaruvi eṉṟu evarum eḻutavilli.  āṉāl cilar ivviṉṉicik karuvi āciyā miṉaril arumpiyatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉar.  cilar cumēriyāvil tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu moḻin taṉar.  cilar ekiptil eḻuntatu eṉṟu eḻutiṉar.  cilar tamiḻakatti ṉiṉṟu eḻuntatu eṉṟu varintaṉar.  palar itu āciyāviṉ mūlattiyuṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar; eḻuti varukiṉṟaṉar.
vaṭa intiya icik kaliñarkaḷ, vīṇi, kāṇṭi nēviyā, cārcciyā, kākacu pōṉṟa nāṭṭiṉar kaṇṭa icikkaruvi eṉṟu ātāramiṉṟi āyviṉṟik kūṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.  āṉāl airōppiya icivallunarkaḷ icik karuvikaḷip paṟṟi naṉku ārāynta nipuṇarkaḷ.  vīṇiyiyum yāḻiyum ārppiyum naṉku ārāyntu atu āciyāvil nērkōṇattil uḷḷa yāḻi yum ekiptiluḷḷa vaḷinta vil yāḻiyum tirāviṭa nāṭṭiluḷḷa amarāvati ciṟpa yāḻkaḷiyum pārttu ataṉ uruva amippu, ati mīṭṭum muṟi, atu tōṉṟiya kālam mutaliyavikaḷiyum atippaṟṟi ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ, ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, varalāṟṟu uṇmikaḷ mutaliyavikaḷiyum naṉku alaci ārāyntu intiya vīṇiyōṭu oppiṭṭup pārttu cila muṭipukaḷik kaṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  ati viḷakki aṟiñar pāṉum eṉpār 1927ām āṇṭu ki. pi. 600-1000 variyuḷḷa varalāṟṟiṉ iṭi nilikkālattil nilaviya narampicik karuvikaḷ eṉpaṉa paṟṟi naṉku ārāyntu oru ariya nūli eḻuti yuḷḷār.  atu naṉku ārāyntu pārkkattakka nalla nūlākum.1
āṉāl tamiḻakattiluḷḷa nām, ārp (Harp) eṉṉum mēṉāṭṭu icik karuvi, kuṟiñci nilattil arumpiya vilyāḻiṉ mūlattiyuṭiyatu eṉṟu meyppittuviṭa muṭiyum.  kuṟiñci nilattil mutaṉmutalākak kaṇṭa vil yāḻē vaḷarccipeṟṟu paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟukkum taṇ poruni yāṟṟiṟkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa mulli nilattil naṉku kaṇṭa āyaṉ mīṇṭi appāl atu cumēriyāviṟkum acīriyāviṟkum, ekiptiṟkum, kirīcukkum, urōm nāṭṭiṟkum ceṉṟu tēcīya icik karuviyāy uyarntatu eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭa muṭiyum.  vil yāḻiṉiṉṟu ātiyāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, kōṭṭuvāttiyam, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ, ārp āka vaḷarntu iṟutiyāka kōṭu nimirntu vīṇiyāka eppaṭi pariṇa mittatu eṉpati vakuttut tokuttu varicippaṭutti ataṉ vaḷarcciyic caṅkilit toṭarpōl eṭuttuk kāṭṭamuṭiyum.  vaṭa intiya icik kaliñarkaḷ ārp eṉṉum icik karuviyil kāṇappaṭum tantikaḷ 5, 7, 9 āka iruppati viḷakki atu intiya vīṇiyippōl irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.  vaṭa intiya vīṇiyil atikamāṉa tantikaḷik kāṇpatu aritu.  āṉāl tamiḻ nāṭṭu yāḻil 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7, 9, 11, 14, 16, 17, 19, 21, 100, 1000 (narampukaḷ) tantikaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu iṉṟum eṉṟum cāṉṟu kāṭṭamuṭiyum.  ārp tamiḻnāṭṭu yāḻiṉ aṭippaṭiyil arumpiya icik karuvi eṉpatutāṉ poruntum.  mēlum 2000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻarkaḷ yavaṉarkaḷōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭiruntārkaḷ.  4000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē epirēyar kaḷōṭum, ekiptiyarkaḷōṭum, cumēriyarkaḷōṭum toṭarpu koṇṭiruntārkaḷ eṉpataṟku eṇṇaṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu.  paratamuṉi tamatu nāṭṭiya nūlil cittirā, vipañci mutaliya vīṇi kaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi yavaṉa nāṭṭu ārp ivikaḷi yottatu eṉṟār, parataṉ kaṇṭa nāṭṭiyam tamiḻar kaṇṭa nāṭṭiyamē yoḻiya vēṟu enta iṉattārum kaṇṭa nāṭṭiyamaṉṟu.  mēlum atu tamiḻar nāṭṭiyatti naṉku viḷakkap peṟṟa cilappatikārat tiṟkup pintiya nūlākum.  naṭarājaṉ (āṭavallāṉ) tamiḻar nāṭṭut teyvamāka irukkum vari parata nāṭṭiyatti tamiḻarit tavira vēṟu yārum taṉateṉṟu urimi koṇṭāṭa muṭiyātu.
parata nāṭṭiyam vaṭamoḻiyil iruppati vittu itu tamiḻar nāṭṭiyam aṉṟu eṉṟu evarum kūṟamuṭiyātu.  paratar eṉṟa tamiḻc col kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattuk kaṭalōṭikaḷiyē kuṟikkum.  avarkaḷ ciṟanta vēḷāḷa vaṇikarkaḷ āvārkaḷ.  avarkaḷil oruvarē paratamuṉivar.  avar vaṭamoḻiyil tam nāṭṭuk kaliyi eḻutivittār.  akkali viḷaṅkum nāṭṭiṉi vaṭakkē vintiya mali, teṟkē mūṉṟu tikkilum kaṭal eṉṟu kūṟit taṉ tāyakamāṉa tamiḻnāṭṭiṉik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.  ituvē uṇmiyākum.  atēpōl vipañci, cittirā mutaliya vīṇikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ vaṭa moḻiyil iruppatik koṇṭu itu āriyarkaḷiṉ icikkaruvi eṉṟu nilināṭṭiviṭa muṭiyātu.  itu tamiḻar kaṇṭa icik karuviyēyākum.  vipañci ēḻāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil nikaḻnta civa camaya kuravaril oruvarākiya tiruñāṉa campantariṉ tirumaṇattil icikkappaṭṭatākap periyapurāṇam kūṟukiṟatu.  atu varumāṟu:
kōtiyar kuḻalcūḻ vaṇṭiṉ kuḻāttoli yorupāṟ kōla
vētiyar vēta vāymi mikumoli yorupāṉ mikka
vētamil vipañci vīṇi yāḻoli yorupā lēttu
nātamaṅ kalaṅkaḷ kīta nayappoli yorupā lāka					(periyapurāṇam 1200)
iṉṟikku āyirattu irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vipañci tamiḻakattil vaḻakkil irunta icik karuvi eṉpatu naṉku terikiṟatu. atu tamiḻar kaṇṭa icik karuviyāka iruttaliṉālē camaya kuravariṉ tirumaṇattil iṭampeṟṟatu.
vīṇi eṉṟa peyarō yāḻ eṉṟa peyarō vaṭa moḻi vētaṅkaḷil illi.  ayal nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta ātikāla āriyarkaḷ kaliyaṟivu peṟṟavarkaḷāka innāṭ ṭiṟku varavilli.  veṟum nāṭōṭikaḷāy iṅkup pōntaṉar.  vīṇiyiyō yāḻiyō avarkaḷ kaṇṭa tum illi; kēṭṭatum illi; icittatum illi.1
āriyarkaḷ intiyāviṟku varumpoḻutu avarkaḷiṉ moḻiyō, paṇpāṭō, kaliyō, nākarikamō, vaḷarcci peṟṟatāy irukkavilli.  eḻuttu eṉṟāl iṉṉateṉa aṟiyārkaḷ.  avarkaḷ moḻikūṭa intiyāviltāṉ cemmippaṭuttap peṟṟatu.  camakirutam eṉṟāl cemmippaṭuttap peṟṟatu eṉṟu poruḷ.  atu iṅkutāṉ vaḷarntatu.  avarkaḷ vētaṅkaḷ ellām iṅku tāṉ uruppeṟṟaṉa.  avarkaḷ intiyāvil eḻuttuk kaliyik kaṇṭaṉar.  avarkaḷatu ātivētam irukkukkūṭa iṅkutāṉ uruppeṟṟu vaḷarntatu.  ātit tirāviṭarkaḷ kaṇṭa arappāviṉ paṇpāṭē āriyarkaḷiṉ moḻi, neṟi paṇpāṭu kali, nākarikam aṉittum vaḷarat tuṇi ceytatu.  intiya maṇṇiltāṉ - ēṉ? - tirāviṭa intiyāviltāṉ, āriyam apivirutti eytiyatu; cīrum ciṟappumuṟṟatu.  viḷakkamākak kūṟuvatāṉāl cintuveḷi tirāviṭap paṇpāṭṭi āriyarkaḷ tammuṭiyatu pōlākkik koṇ ṭārkaḷ.
vaṭamoḻi vētaṅkaḷil vīṇi eṉṟa patam illi eṉpatu nūṟṟukku nūṟu uṇmi.  āṉāl cila vaṭa nāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷ vētaṅ kaḷil vāṇā, kasāṉā eṉṟa pataṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa; atu vīṇiyik kuṟikkum pataṅkaḷ eṉṟu valintu poruḷ koṇṭu vētaṅkaḷil iciyum icikkaruvikaḷum uṇṭu eṉṟu nilināṭṭa muyalkiṉ ṟaṉar.  itu veṟum kēlik kūttākum.  eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tamiḻaṉ oruvaṉ iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻarkaḷukku tuppākki ceyyat teriyum, tirukkuṟaḷil vaḷḷuvar
tuppārkkut tuppāya tuppākkit tuppārkkut
tuppāya tūum maḻi
eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār eṉṟu kūṟuvativiṭa mika maṭṭamāṉa vātam.  vaṭamoḻi vētaṅkaḷil vīṇi eṉṟa icikkaruvi kūṟappaṭṭiruk kiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu vīṇ muyaṟci.  immuyaṟciyil avarkaḷ veṟṟiyīṭṭa muṭiyātu.  ēṉeṉil ippoḻutu vētaṅkaḷi āriyar allātār paṭikkakkūṭātu eṉṟu taṭukkamuṭiyātu.  paṭitta varkaḷ nākki aṟukkamuṭiyātu.  toṭṭavarkaḷic cuṭṭuviṭa muṭiyātu.  mēk mullar, pārō pōṉṟa mēṉāṭṭārkaḷ āṉantakkumāracuvāmi, makipālaṉpaṭṭi katirēcaṉ ceṭṭiyār, tiruñāṉa campantam mutaliyavarkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyi naṉkukaṟṟiruk kiṟārkaḷ.  cilar vētaṅkaḷi āṅkilattil moḻiyākkam cey tuḷḷaṉar.  avi ciṟantaṉa eṉṟu ellōrum ēṟṟukkoṇṭu irukkiṉṟaṉar.  ivarkaḷiṟ palar vīṇikkum vētattiṟkum toṭarpilli eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭaṉar.
tamiḻakattil yāḻ, kuḻal, muracu pōṉṟa icik karuvikaḷ eṇṇāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tōṉṟi viṭṭaṉa eṉṟu iṉṟiyat tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ kūṟivarukiṟārkaḷ.  tamiḻarkaḷiṉ tavak kuṟiyiṉ kāraṇamāka tamiḻakattil eḻunta iruperum kaṭalkōḷi ṉāl ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, ciṟpaṅkaḷum, ōviyaṅkaḷum aḻintu pōyviṭṭataṉāl cāṉṟukāṭṭa muṭiyavilli.  āṉāl iṉṟu kiṭikkum paḻamperum nūlāṉa tolkāppiyam 2500 āṇṭu kaḷukku muṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟum tirukkuṟaḷ 2000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  avikaḷilē tamiḻarkaḷiṉ vaḷarccippeṟṟa yāḻ vakikaḷum kuḻal, paṟi vakikaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.  avikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyi ārāyntāl avi paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷāka vaḷarccipeṟṟa icikkaruvikaḷākak koḷḷalām eṉṟum avvaṟiñarkaḷ matippiṭukiṉṟārkaḷ.
mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻarkaḷ vāḻntuvanta aintu nilaṅkaḷilum ovvoru nilattiṟkum taṉittaṉiyāka yāḻ kaḷum paṟikaḷum, tuṭikaḷum, muracukaḷum, paṇkaḷum, piṟavum iruntuvantaṉa.  tolkāppiyam pāṇar paṟiyar tuṭiyar pōṉṟa kaliñarkaḷ iruntatik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.  āriyarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷiṭa miruntu inta icik karuvikaḷip peṟṟa kālattiyē ivvicik karuvikaḷ tōṉṟiya kālam eṉṟu cilar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.  āṉāl inta icik karuvikaḷ itaṟku muṉpē - āriyarkaḷ intiyāviṟkuḷ varuvataṟku muṉpē tirāviṭa intiyāvil tōṉṟi āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷāka vaḷarcciyaṭinta icik karuvi kaḷākum.
vaṭamoḻi ārāycci mikka aṟiñar oruvar, muṟkālattil ici mūṉṟu mukkiya apiviruttik kaṭṭaṅkaḷik kaṭantuḷḷatu.  ovvoṉṟun taṉittaṉmiyuḷḷa icik karuvikaḷ eṉṟu pirikkappaṭalām.  tuntupi, pūmi - tuntupi, paṉṉā pōṉṟavi kaḷip paḻamiyāṉaviyākak karutalām.  kuḻal allatu ūtu karuvikaḷ aṭuttapaṭiyāka arumpiyavikaḷākak karutalām.  nākarikam tuḷirtta kālattil narampukkaruvikaḷāṉa vāṉā, kacōṉā pōṉ ṟavi, atāvatu, yāḻ, vīṇi pōṉṟavi ovvoru taṉittaṉi iṉaṅkaḷiṉ piṟkāla icikkaruvikaḷākak karutalām eṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.1 inta muṭipukaḷ āriyarkaḷ campantap paṭṭa maṭṭil cariyāka irukkalām.  āṉār tamiḻarkaḷip poṟutta maṭṭilum itu tavaṟāṉa muṭipēyākum.  tamiḻarkaḷ tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭi makkaḷ āvar.  ivarkaḷ 5000, 6000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē palvēṟu narampuk karuvikaḷiyum tōṟkaruvikaḷiyum, tuḷik karuvikaḷiyum kaṇṭavarkaḷ eṉṟu pala mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ cāṉṟukāṭṭi eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.  tamiḻarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ arappā, āticcanallūr paṇpāṭṭi uruvākkiyavarkaḷ; avarkaḷ, cumēriyā, acīriyā, cālṭiyā, ekiptu, kirīṭ paṇpāṭṭi vaḷarttavarkaḷ āvar.  āriya rāmaṉ tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkup pōnta poḻutu pāṇṭiya nāṭṭut talinakarākiya kapāṭapurattil vāṉaḷāviya araṇkaḷum maṇikaḷ iḻittuc ceyyappeṟṟa poṟkatavukaḷum iruntaṉa eṉṟu vāṉmīki kūṟukiṉṟār.  atil avar kūṟum irāvaṇaṉiṉ ilaṅki nāṭṭut talinakaril irunta araṇmaṉiyiṉ pavaḻat tūṇkaḷiyum poṟkatavukaḷiyum, navamaṇikaḷāl iḻikkappeṟṟa aṇikaḷiyum ārāyntāl, iṉṟu iṅkilāntiluḷḷa elicapet araciyiṉ araṇmaṉi yiṉum ciṟappup peṟṟu viḷaṅkuvatākat teriyum.  irāmāyaṇa kālattiṟkup pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻakattil yāḻum, kuḻalum, muracum, paṟiyum, tuṭiyum ciṟappuṟṟiruntaṉa.  irāvaṇaṉ iṭiccaṅkak kālattil iruntavaṉ. avaṉatu kālattil palavita yāḻkaḷ iruntaṉa.  ataṟkup paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē teṉmaturiyil vilyāḻum ātiyāḻum piṉṉum palavum ciṟappuṟṟiruntaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu.  irāvaṇaṉ maṭṭumallāmal avaṉ maṉiviyum makkaḷum cukrīvaṉum icivallunarāy irun taṉar.  avaṉatu araṇmaṉiyil makaḷir vāḻum antappurattil yāḻum, kuḻalum iṉṉoli ceytukoṇṭē iruntaṉa.2 itu ematu kaṟpaṉiyaṉṟu, teṉṉintiya cāti varalāṟum irāmāyaṇak karupporuḷum eḻutiya tamiḻarāṉa maṉōṉmaṇiyam āciriyar em. ē. cuntaram piḷḷi kūṟṟākum.  maṟṟoru civap periyārāṉa, irāvaṇap periyār eṉṟa ēṭṭi eḻutiya em. e.  pūraṇa liṅkampiḷḷi karuttumaṉṟu; irāmaṉi uyartti irāvaṇaṉit tāḻtti eḻutiya vaṭamoḻip pulavar vāṉmīki eṭuttukkāṭṭum varalāṟṟu uṇmi.  itaṉi intiyāviṉ icikkaruvikaḷ eṉṟa nūlil tiru. e. kiruṣṇacuvāmi avarkaḷ eṭuttuk kāṭṭi yuḷḷār.  ivarkūṭa yāḻ tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ icikkaruvi eṉṟum vīṇi vētakāla icikkaruvi eṉṟum tam nūlil kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār.  cumārtta mataccārpuṭiyavarkaḷ evarāyiruppiṉum āriyar nākarikatti uyarttuvatē tam ilaṭciyamākak koṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ.  arappā nākarikam eḻunta kālatti piṉṉukkut taḷḷuvatil avarkaḷ añcuvatilli.  āriyar intiyāviṟku vanta kālatti muṉṉukkut taḷḷuvatilum avarkaḷ veṭkappaṭuvatilli.  eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavi uḷḷaṉa.
niṟka. iṉṟiya mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ cumēriyātāṉ tirāviṭar kaḷiṉ tāyakam eṉṟu kāṇkiṉṟārkaḷ.  aṅkētāṉ 6000 āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉ palvēṟu yāḻkaḷ arumpiṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.1  āṉāl kīḻnāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ cilar tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ tāyakam intiyāvē (vemūriyāk kaṇṭamē) eṉṟum, avarkaḷ piṟkālat til cumēriya nāṭṭil kuṭiyēṟi aṅkut taṅkaḷ nākarikattik kaṭṭi vaḷarttirukkalām eṉṟum cila pala kāraṇaṅkaḷāl mīṇṭum papilōṉiliruntu cintu camaveḷiyil vantu kuṭiyēṟi appāl āriyar paṭiyeṭuppāl teṉ intiyāviṟku mīṇṭum pōntiruk kalām eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.  itaṉāl tamiḻ yāḻē cālṭiyā nāṭṭilum ekiptilum cintu camaveḷiyilum piṟa nāṭukaḷilum ceṉṟuḷḷaṉa veṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.
ilemuriyāk kaṇṭattiṉiṉṟu tirāviṭarkaḷ cumēriyāvi lum cālṭiyāvilum kuṭiyēṟiyiruntālum cari, cumēriyarkaḷ cālṭiyā nāṭṭiliruntu cintu veḷiyil kuṭiyēṟit teṉ intiyā pōntiruntālum cari atippaṟṟi nām iṅku ippoḻutu ārāya vēṇṭiyatu avaciyamilli.  6000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tirāviṭarkaḷ, nākarikampeṟṟavarkaḷ; cīriya paṇpāṭṭi vaḷartta varkaḷ; amuta yāḻi, iṉṉici vīṇiyik kaṇṭavarkaḷ eṉpatu evarum oppukkoḷḷum uṇmiyāy irukkiṟatu eṉpatē mutaṉmiyāṉa karuttākum.  iti iṉṟu evarum maṟuppataṟ killi.  atōṭu 6000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē naṉi ciṟanta nākarikam vāynta ātitirāviṭarkaḷum cumēriyarkaḷum iratta campantamāṉa toṭarpuṭiya iṉattavarkaḷāka iruntārkaḷ eṉpatu iṉṟu kuṉṟiṉ mēliṭṭa viḷakkāka irukkiṟatu.2  ulakam oppiya uyarnta inta uṇmiyi evarum maṟuppataṟkilli.
cumēriya nāṭṭil paṇ (Pan or Ban) eṉṟa patam vilyāḻiṉ peyarāka payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  piṟkālattil atu vaṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu.
itu arēpiyāvil vāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu.  ekiptil mikap piṟkālam vari peṉ, peṉṟ allatu piṉ, piṉṟ eṉṟu kūṟappeṟṟatu.  itu tantikaḷ poruttappeṟṟa icikkaruvi.  iṉṟu itaṉi nēriṟ kaṇṭa aṟiñar itu āṟu tantikaḷōṭum piṟivaṭiva māṉa uṭalōṭum kuṟipāṭaṟṟa nīḷ uruḷi vaṭivāṉatum oṭukka māṉa uruvamāṉatum āka viḷaṅkukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
āriyarkaḷ intiyāviṉuḷ aṭiyeṭuttu vippataṟku 1500āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē arappā nākarikam arumpiviṭṭatu.  arappāvil arumpiya ariya āti tirāviṭa nākarikattil yāḻ eṉṉum narampukkaruvi eḻuntuviṭṭatu.  aṅkuḷḷa muttirikaḷil yāḻ, kuḻal, muracu pōṉṟa icik karuvikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ kaṇṭu piṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  iṉṟikku 5000āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ tuḷirtta cīriya cintuveḷip paṇpāṭu tamiḻar nākarikattiṉ tantiyākavum āriya nākarikattiṉ tāyākavum irukkiṟatu.  iṉṟu āriyar eṉṟu, kūṟikkoḷpavarkaḷ iti ēṟkāviṭiṉum ulakiṟ ciṟanta aṟiñarkaḷ palarum ēṟṟukkoṇṭuḷḷaṉar.  cintu veḷip paṇpāṭṭiṉiṉṟu intiyap paṇpāṭu pariṇamittuḷḷatu eṉpatu ulakam maṟukkamuṭiyāta uṇmiyāka uṟutippaṭuttap peṟ ṟuḷḷatu.  iti iṉṟu vaṭa intiyāviluḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷ palarum ēṟṟukkoṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ.  maṅkāp pukaḻ vāynta vaṅkap pēraṟiñarum cintuveḷi nākarikatti ārampak kālattilē akaḻntu kaṇṭa pēraṟiñarumāṉa ār. ṭi. pāṉarci avarkaḷ cintu camaveḷiyil untiya ematu muntiya nākarikam tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉpatil eṭṭuṇiyum aiyamilli eṉṟu aṟutiyiṭṭu uṟuti kūṟiyuḷḷār.1  ivar, ulakam muḻuvatum, āriya nākarikattiṉiṉṟē ciṟappuṟṟateṉṟu cintiyil eṇṇi, eṇṇi, atippaṟṟip pakaṟkaṉavu kaṇṭu, namatu palkalik kaḻaka māṇavarkaḷmuṉ uḷaṟik koṭṭiya puttakap pūcciyāṉa naccukkaṇṭaṉ pōṉṟavarallar.  cintuveḷi nākarikatti akaḻntu kaṇṭa, car. cāṇ mārcl eṉpavarōṭu cērntu akaḻntu ārāyntavar.  māccalukkup piṉṉarum aṅku nīṇṭakālam āḻntu pala nūṟkaḷ eḻutiyavar.
cintuveḷip paṇpāṭu ulakil uccakkaṭṭatti aṭinta uyariya paḻamperum paṇpāṭu.  aṅku akaḻntu kaṇṭa muttiri kaḷil poṟikkappaṭṭa yāḻ uruvaṅkaḷ cumēriyā nāṭṭil aracak kallaṟikaḷil kaṇṭeṭukkap peṟṟa yāḻkaḷōṭum tamiḻ nāṭṭil kaṇṭa yāḻkaḷōṭum oppumi uṭiyaṉa.1  cumēriyarkaḷ yāḻip paṇ eṉṟu pakarntaṉar.  appatam tamiḻar kaṇṭa taṉit tamiḻ collāṉa paṇ, paṇṇiyam, pāṇ, pāṇi eṉṟa vārtti kaḷōṭu mika neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyatāka miḷirkiṟatu.  ikkālat tamiḻarkaḷ paṇ eṉpati ici eṉṟum irākam eṉṟum kūṟuvar.  cilappatikāra uriyuḷ
paṇṇeṉpatu narappaṭivāl niṟan tōṉṟap
paṇṇappaṭā niṉṟa paṇṇum paṇṇiyaṟṟiṟamum
tiṟattiṟamum eṉṟavāṟu	(cilap. araṅkēṟ. vari 46 uriyuḷ)
eṉṟum,
paṇ paṇṇiyam tiṟam tiṟattiṟam eṉṉumivi
campūraṇam cāṭavam, auṭavam caturttam eṉum
vaṭamoḻip peyarāṉum vaḻaṅkum					(cilap. puṟañcēri. vari 106 uriyuḷ)
eṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvatu ārāyattakkatu.  paṇ icippavar pāṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar.  paṇṇiyar eṉṟum kūṟappeṟuvar.  paṇṭik kālattil yāḻi atāvatu paṇṇi icittavar paṇṇiyar allatu pāṇar eṉṟu aḻikkappeṟṟaṉar.  tamiḻar icip pāṭṭip paṇ eṉṟaṉar.  pāṇ eṉṟa patamum pāṭṭiyē kuṟikkum paṇṇum, pāṇiyum cērntu pāṇ āyiṟṟu.  pāṇ eṉṟa icip pāṭṭiṟku pā, paṇ, pāṇi eṉṟa mūṉṟu aṅkaṅkaḷ uṇṭu eṉṟu ici vallunarkaḷ kūṟiṉārkaḷ.  paṇṭik kālattil icit tuṟiyil īṭupaṭṭa tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ yāḻ eṉṟa patatti paṇṇiṟkē payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka,
yāma yāḻp peyar kuṟiñciyāḻum
cevvaḻi yāḻp peyar mulli yāḻum
pāli yāḻum maruta yāḻu meṉa
nālvaki yāḻum nāṟperum paṇṇē
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭum cēntaṉ tivākaramum,
pāli kuṟiñci marutañ cev vaḻiyeṉa
nālvaki yāḻā nāṟperum paṇṇē
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭum piṅkalanti nikaṇṭiyum nām nōkkum poḻutu cumēriyar, yāḻ eṉṟa icikkaruvikkup paṇ eṉṟu aḻittu vantatu tamiḻ marapiyoṭṭiyatē eṉṟāl atu oru ciṟitum mikiyākātu.
pērāciriyar pāṉum, mēṟku āciyāvil uḷḷa naṉi ciṟanta nākarikatti nayanta naṉmakkaḷāṉa cumēriyar, ācīriyar, iṭṭiṭ, elamiṭ, pāracīkar, ekiptiyar pōṉṟa paḻamperum nāṭṭiṉar paravalāka curat tāṉap palakikaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantatākak karutukiṉṟār.  tiru. ē. eyc. lāyṭ eṉṟa aṟiñar tamatu niṉavēyum aḻiyāp pakutikaḷum eṉṉum nūlil (1850) acīriyarkaḷ, cīriyarkaḷ, ekiptiyarkaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷ paṇṭik kālattil palvēṟu icikkaruvikaḷi vittiruntārkaḷ.  āṉāl ciṟpaṅkaḷil iraṇṭu vitamāṉa icikkaruvikaḷ, atāvatu muracum, mukkōṇa vaṭivāṉa yāḻumē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  (mukkōṇa yāḻ pēriyāḻ eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu.)  inta yāḻi iṭatu pakkattuk kik kiṭiyē vittuk koṇṭum kaḻuttiliruntu toṅka viṭappaṭṭum iyakki vantaṉar.  yāḻi iṭatu pakkam vittukkoṇṭu valatu kiyil oru ciṟu cilli (piḷakṭiram) eṭuttu vittukkoṇṭu tantiyit teṟittu mīṭṭuvārkaḷ.  iṭatu kiyiṉāl tantiyit taḷḷiyum aḻuttiyum curaṅkaḷi uruvākkappeṟum.  ekiptiya yāḻ pōṉṟu ēṉiya yāḻkaḷ taṭṭiyāṉa palaki allatu aṭippakutiyi nimirtti niṟkum taṇṭiṟkum iṭiyē kuṟukkut tuṇṭukaḷ kiṭiyā eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷār.  avar mēlum, inta yāḻil 9 tantikaḷ iruntaṉa; ekiptiya ōviyattil kāṇum yāḻil oru tantikaḷē uḷḷaṉa.  ituvum valatu kiyil cilli vittukkoṇṭu tantiyit teṟippatutāṉ eṉṟum viḷakkik kūṟiyuḷḷār.
caṅkak kālattilē tamiḻakattilē iciyip paṟṟiyum icikkaruvikaḷip paṟṟiyum arumperum ārāyccikaḷ ceyyap paṭṭaṉa.  ciṟappāka yāḻiṉ nirmāṇamum, amipput tiṟaṉum atika vaḷarcciyaṭintiruntaṉa.  yāḻ oṉṟu mutal 1000 vari narampukaḷ uḷḷatāyp palvēṟu vakiyāṉa vaṭivamum amippum peyarum peṟṟatāy viḷaṅkiyatu.  atippōl vīṇiyum palvēṟu eṇkaḷ uḷḷa narampukaḷiyum vevvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum peṟṟiruntatu.  paratamuṉi nāṭṭiya nūlil 22 vakiyāṉa vīṇi kaḷip paṟṟi viḷakkik kūṟiyuḷḷār.  avikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟu:-
1.	makati	2.	cittirā	3.	kōcāvaṇi
4.	cittiriki	5.	kūrmici	6.	cāraṅkam
7.	irāvaṇācuram	8.	kiṉṉari varāḷi	9.	kīti
10.	kucciki	11.	vipañciki	12.	parivātiṉi
13.	cakaṉavallaki	14.	caravīṇi	15.	anāvitam
16.	pirakati	17.	ilakulāṭci	18.	kaccari
19.	kāntari	20.	aṉumatam	21.	uruttiriki
22.	kaḻāvati
iviyaṉṟi (1) cata tantirikā (2) ikcōvaṉi (3) kaṇṭā (4) piccōrā (5) kōṭā (6) tampalā pōṉṟa vīṇikaḷum iruntaṉa eṉṟu terikiṉṟatu.
pañca vamcap piramāṇam eṉṉum nūlil vīṇi ceyyum vaḻikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  tantirikā vīṇi marattāl ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭum.  iti aḻakuṟa amikkavēṇṭum.  curikkāy cevvi tāyc ceyyappaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum.  civappu niṟamuḷḷa erutiṉ tōlāl mūṭappaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum.  itaṉ kaḻuttiṟkup piṉṉāl pattut toḷikaḷ iṭappaṭṭu atil muñcā allatu turlā pullāl ceyyappaṭṭa nāṇkaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum.  inta nāṇic cillāl1 (mūṅkil kuccāl) teṟittu ici mīṭṭa vēṇṭum.  kōcāvaṇi vīṇi eṉpatu pāṇā eṉṉum icik karuviyēyākum.  kaṇṭavīṇi eṉpatu mūṅkil kuḻalit tavira vēṟu oṉṟum illi.  parivātiṉi eṉṉum vīṇi kalimaṉṉaṉ ākiya toṇṭi nāṭāṇṭa pallavap pārvēntaṉākiya makēntira varmaṉ mīṭṭiya yāḻākum.  itil poṉ tantikaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟiruntaṉavām.  kāñcipuram kilācanātar kōyilil vīṇi tāṅkiya civa perumāṉuṭiya ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷ uṇṭu.  kumpa kōṇattil uḷḷa irāmacuvāmi kōyilil iru curakkuṭukki kaḷiyuṭiya vīṇiyik kalimakaḷ kiyil tāṅki niṟpatu pōṉṟa ciṟpaccili oṉṟu uḷḷatu.  intac ciṟpaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum vīṇikkum iṉṟu vaḻakkil irukkum vīṇikkum vēṟṟumi uṇṭu.  iṅku vēṟōr iṭattil pārvati vīṇiyi ēnti niṟkiṉṟāḷ.  itu ikkāla vīṇiyip pōl irukkiṟatu.  iṉṟu vaṭa intiyāvil kāṇappaṭum vīṇi uruttira vīṇi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.  tamiḻnāṭṭil iṉṟu peritum kāṇappaṭum vīṇi caracuvati vīṇi eṉappaṭum.  itaṉit tañcāvūr vīṇi eṉṟum kūṟuvar.
